= Audit Notary: REST API Register
:toc: left
:toclevels: 2
:toc-title: Table of Contents
:source-highlighter: highlight.js

xref:../README.adoc[Audit Notary documentation] » Specification

The register of every request of `/api/v1` that the web app makes or that a requirement names: one table, one row per
request. It is the contract between the web app, the mock server (`web-mock`) and the application: a screen is built
against a row, a mock profile answers a row, and a requirement that fixes a row is linked from it. The shell requests
and the common refusals are fixed in xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]; the outcomes of the operations are in
xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#operation-outcomes[Architecture and Modules, Operation Outcomes].

[#_reading_the_register]
== Reading the Register

* *Status* is that of the request, its method, path, body and outcomes:
** `SPECIFIED`: a requirement fixes it; the row links to it.
** `PROVISIONAL`: the specification is silent. The request is the one the web app and the mock use today; it is
decided provisionally so that no screen is blocked, and the owner may change it. A requirement that fixes it later
turns the status into `SPECIFIED` and links it.
* Where only the shape of an answer is open, the *Answer* column marks it "shape PROVISIONAL"; the request stays
`SPECIFIED`.
* *Outcomes* lists those of the request beyond the common refusals below. A refusal names its `outcome` in the JSON
body, and the web app decides by it, never by the status code alone
(xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]); a `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS` is therefore no lost session
(see <<_open_questions>>). Statuses are given only where a source fixes them.
* *Login* is required for every request except `GET /api/v1/status`, `GET /api/v1/messages` and the login endpoints.
* Every request that changes state carries the anti-CSRF header (xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-8[AUN-WEB-8]), except
`POST /j_security_check` of the local login. It is the one row that is no `/api/v1` request; it is in the register
because the web app posts it.
* The pages of the web app outside the shell (`/login`, `/signed-out`, `/login-problem`) are no requests of the API;
<<_addresses_outside_the_shell>> lists them with the requests they make.
* The control routes of the mock (`/mock/*`) are no part of the API.

[#_common_refusals]
== Common Refusals

Every request can end with these outcomes (xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9], Refusals):

[cols="1,1,3"]
|===
|Status |`outcome` |When

|`401`
|`UNAUTHENTICATED`
|No session; the body carries `login`

|`403`
|`CSRF_REJECTED`
|Anti-CSRF header missing or wrong, or a foreign `Origin`

|`403`
|`FORBIDDEN`
|No mapped right (xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-2[AUN-AUTH-2])

|`403`
|`FIRST_LOGIN_REQUIRED`
|Outside the first-login allow-list (xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-2[AUN-DIR-2])

|`404`
|`NOT_FOUND`
|The addressed object does not exist, or the user may not read it (PROVISIONAL: xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-2[AUN-DP-2]
reports a reference into a repository the user may not read as not found; the form kit's requests use the outcome)

|`422`
|`VALIDATION`
|`errors`: a list of `{"pointer", "code"}` with a JSON Pointer into the request body; for a request without a body
(PROVISIONAL), a JSON Pointer into its query parameters read as one object, a parameter name to its value (`/q`), or to
the array of its values when it is repeated (`/ref`; an element is `/ref/3`)

|`503`
|`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|The Git server is unreachable (xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#AUN-GEN-6[AUN-GEN-6]); a write keeps the draft
|===

[#_requests]
== Requests

[cols="3,2,3,3,2,1"]
|===
|Request |Body |Answer |Outcomes |Requirement |Status

6+h|Shell

|`GET /api/v1/status`
|none
|`{"version", "degraded", "loginProvider", "repositories": [{"alias", "fetch"}]}`; no login, no personal data. The
member `loginProvider` (`oidc` or `local`) says how the login works: with `local` the shell shows the notice of local
test accounts on every page and the login is the web app's page `/login`
(xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-6[AUN-AUTH-6]). A status without it reads as `oidc` (PROVISIONAL tolerance, so that
an older server does not break the shell). The member `stepUp` (`mfa`, `password` or `off`) is PROVISIONAL: the
confirmation reads how step-up confirms before it asks; absent means `mfa` with `oidc` and `off` with `local`, the
defaults of xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-4[AUN-AUTH-4]. The web app and the mock carry and read `loginProvider`;
`StatusResource` of the server does not carry it yet.
|`degraded` while a fetch fails
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]
|`SPECIFIED`; member `stepUp` `PROVISIONAL`; member `loginProvider` not yet carried by the server

|`GET /api/v1/messages?lang=en\|de`
|none
|The message catalogue: one object from key to text; no login
|`400` for any other `lang`; a missing `lang` returns English
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-7[AUN-WEB-7], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]
|`SPECIFIED`

|`GET /api/v1/session`
|none
|`{"user": {"pseudonym", "name"}, "rights": [{"repository", "actions"}], "csrfToken", "firstLoginRequired",
"directoryConfirmationDue"}`; `directoryConfirmationDue` (PROVISIONAL) is `true` when the own directory entry is due for
its confirmation (<<_decided_directory>>, point 13), absent reads `false`; a session
without a mapped right answers `"rights": []` and is a session like any other (<<_a_session_without_rights>>);
`csrfToken` belongs to the session, so another value on a later read means another session
(<<_a_session_that_is_replaced>>, PROVISIONAL)
|none
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]
|`SPECIFIED`; the use of `csrfToken` as the identity of the session and the member `directoryConfirmationDue` `PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/due`
|none
|`{"items": [{"kind", "lens", "title", "address", "due"}]}`; `kind` is `deadline`, `review` or `release`. A pending
release request of an agent (xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-8[UC-DOC-8]) adds the members `requestId`,
`requestedBy` `{"pseudonym", "name"}`, `requestedAt` and `comment` to its item (PROVISIONAL); `lens` is one of the
lenses of the rail, `categories` for the documents of the generic lens (<<_the_document_lifecycle>>). The item of a
deadline of an incident (<<_incident_deadlines>>) has the member `deadline` `{"rule", "status"}` (PROVISIONAL). The item of
the review of a released ISMS document or risk (<<_isms_due>>) is `kind` `review`, lens `isms` (PROVISIONAL)
|none
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]
|`SPECIFIED`; the members of a release request, the member `deadline`, the lens `categories` and the ISMS review items `PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/events`
|none
|`text/event-stream`: the event `document-saved` `{"repository", "id", "fields"}`, the event `status` with the body
of `GET /api/v1/status`, and the events of the document lifecycle `document-created`, `document-transitioned` and
`release-requested` (PROVISIONAL, <<_the_document_lifecycle>>) and `catalog-changed` `{"catalog", "id", "by"}`
(PROVISIONAL, <<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>). The stream belongs to the session that opened it and closes when that session ends: a
logout, a back-channel logout, a refused refresh, the end of idle time or lifetime, a bound, or a new login in the same
browser that replaced it (xref:../use-cases/01-access.adoc#change-events[UC-ACC changes, event stream])
|none
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]
|`SPECIFIED`; the closing with the session `PROVISIONAL` until the use cases are agreed; the three lifecycle events,
`catalog-changed` and the optional members of `document-saved` `PROVISIONAL`

6+h|Login and logout

|`GET /api/v1/auth/login?return=<address>`
|none
|A redirect to the identity provider; with local users a `302` to the web app's login page,
`/login?return=<checked address>` (PROVISIONAL: the register said only "the web app's login page"). `return` is checked
against the canonical addresses and never against the addresses outside the shell
|`502 IDENTITY_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`; `503 TOO_MANY_LOGINS` with `Retry-After: 60`. For a navigation of the browser
(`Accept` contains `text/html`) the same two refusals are answered as `303` to
`/login-problem?reason=idp-unavailable` and `/login-problem?reason=too-many-logins` (PROVISIONAL), so that a person
never reads raw JSON; any other client gets the JSON
|xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-1[AUN-AUTH-1], xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-6[AUN-AUTH-6]
|`SPECIFIED`; the redirect with local users and the `303` to `/login-problem` `PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/auth/callback`
|none (`code`, `state`, `iss` in the query)
|A page that navigates to the checked `return`
|`400 UNKNOWN_TRANSACTION` (PROVISIONAL): the login transaction is not found, because it expired, was taken, or was
dropped as the oldest of a source that started too many; nothing is created, the transaction cookie is cleared. For a
navigation of the browser a `303` to `/login-problem?reason=unknown-transaction`, which offers a new login
(xref:../use-cases/01-access.adoc#UC-ACC-1[UC-ACC-1])
|xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-1[AUN-AUTH-1]
|`SPECIFIED`; the outcome and the `303` `PROVISIONAL`

|`GET <step-up address>`
|none
|The step-up address that `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` carries: the web app leaves for it with the current address as `return`
and comes back after the renewed authentication. The client treats the address as opaque; the mock uses
`/api/v1/auth/login?step-up=mfa`. Its refusals are those of the login start: `502 IDENTITY_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `503
TOO_MANY_LOGINS`, and for a navigation the `303` to `/login-problem`
|as the login start
|xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-4[AUN-AUTH-4]
|`SPECIFIED`; the address format `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /j_security_check`
|`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`: `j_username` and `j_password`; no anti-CSRF header, because the request has no
session to take a token from (xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-8[AUN-WEB-8])
|Only with the login provider `local`. `200` with an empty body and the login cookie; the web app then navigates to the
checked `return`. A user without a directory entry or with an entry due for confirmation meets
xref:../use-cases/01-access.adoc#UC-ACC-4[UC-ACC-4] and xref:../use-cases/01-access.adoc#UC-ACC-5[UC-ACC-5] afterwards,
as with the identity provider
|`401` with an empty body and no `outcome` for an unknown user or a wrong password, the same answer for both (Quarkus'
form authentication, xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-6[AUN-AUTH-6]); `403` `{"outcome": "CSRF_REJECTED"}` when the
`Origin` header is missing or is not the application's own origin (the body is PROVISIONAL: the requirement says
"refused")
|xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-6[AUN-AUTH-6], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-8[AUN-WEB-8], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-9[AUN-WEB-9]
|`SPECIFIED`; the body of the `403` `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/auth/logout`
|none
|`{"redirect"}`: the address the web app navigates to. PROVISIONAL: with the identity provider the provider's
`end_session_endpoint` (the provider returns to `/signed-out`); without a usable one, and with local users, `/signed-out`
instead of the start page. The answer clears the session cookie. A `401` to a logout (the session had ended) counts
as signed out: the web app navigates to `/signed-out`
|none
|xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-1[AUN-AUTH-1], xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-6[AUN-AUTH-6]
|`SPECIFIED`; the landing `/signed-out` `PROVISIONAL`

6+h|Directory and catalogs

|`GET /api/v1/directory/me`
|none
|The prefill of the first-login form and the own entry: `{"name", "teams", "informationText", "entry"}` (shape
PROVISIONAL). `name` is the one of the token, `teams` the candidates from the groups; `entry` is `null` while the first
login is required and, once the entry is saved, the own entry `{"pseudonym", "name", "memberships": [{"team", "role"}],
"confirmedAt", "confirmationDue"}` (PROVISIONAL, <<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>). `memberships` are catalog
references (`team:payments`); `confirmedAt` is a date; `confirmationDue` is `true` when `confirmedAt` is older than
`directory.confirmation-interval` (<<_decided_directory>>, point 13)
|none
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-2[AUN-DIR-2], xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-3[AUN-DIR-3]
|`SPECIFIED`; the member `entry` `PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/directory/me`
|First login: `{"name", "team", "role"}`, with `binding` (`new` or a pseudonym) after `BINDING_CANDIDATES`; `team` and
`role` are catalog ids without the prefix. Own entry (PROVISIONAL): `{"name", "memberships": [{"team", "role"}]}`, one
to five memberships, the references with their prefix. Both forms are accepted; a user who has an entry sends the
second
|`{"outcome": "SAVED", "pseudonym"}`; for the own entry also `"confirmedAt"`: a saved change is a confirmation
|`VALIDATION` with `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` (pointer `/team`, `/role`, or `/memberships/0/team`); `BINDING_CANDIDATES`
(`409`, PROVISIONAL) with `candidates`, each `{"pseudonym", "name", "memberships"}`; `NOT_BINDABLE` (`409`,
PROVISIONAL). Bodies in <<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-2[AUN-DIR-2], xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-3[AUN-DIR-3]
|`SPECIFIED`; the second body, `confirmedAt` and the two statuses `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/directory/me/confirmation`
|none
|`{"outcome": "CONFIRMED", "confirmedAt"}`: the entry is unchanged, its confirmation date is today
|`FIRST_LOGIN_REQUIRED` from the gate while the first login is required (the request is outside the allow-list of
xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-2[AUN-DIR-2], so it never reaches the endpoint); `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `REJECTED`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-3[AUN-DIR-3]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}`
|none
|`{"entries": [{"id", "name", "retired"}]}` (shape PROVISIONAL); `retired` is a boolean and absent means `false`. The
list holds the retired entries too, so that a reference to one resolves (xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4]);
the form kit's `CatalogPicker` reads it for every catalog its schemas name (<<_form_kit_requests>>). `{catalog}` is one
of the five catalogs `team`, `role`, `service`, `system`, `external`; another id answers `404 NOT_FOUND`. No order is promised
(the web app sorts by name) and there is no cut-off
|`404 NOT_FOUND`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-2[AUN-DIR-2], xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4]
|`SPECIFIED` for `team` and `role`; the other catalogs and the member `retired` `PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}/schema`
|none
|`{"catalog", "schema"}`: the JSON Schema of the entries of the catalog, in the vocabulary of the form kit
(xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4]), the same for every user. The five schemas are in
<<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4], xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-5[AUN-DIR-5]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}/{id}`
|none
|One entry: `{"catalog", "id", "name", "retired", "value", "effective", "at", "history"}`. `value` is the entry as the
schema describes it; `effective` is `null` except for a service, where it holds the effective protection needs, the
dependencies and the cycle of <<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>; `history` is the signed history, newest first, at
most 20. The ids `new` and `schema` are no entries
|`404 NOT_FOUND`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4], xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-5[AUN-DIR-5]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}`
|`{"value"}`: a new entry, its `id` in the value
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "catalog", "id", "address", "at"}`
|Right `administer-catalogs` (`403 FORBIDDEN`); `VALIDATION` per field, pointers into `value`; `409 ID_TAKEN`;
`REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}/{id}`
|`{"value"}`: the whole entry, its `id` equal to the one of the path. The last save wins (no `blob`)
|`200 {"outcome": "SAVED", "catalog", "id", "at"}`
|Right `administer-catalogs`; `VALIDATION` per field (`REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` for a reference that is in no catalog,
`ID_MISMATCH`); `409 RETIRED`: a retired entry is not changed; `404 NOT_FOUND`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`. There is
no `DELETE`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}/{id}/retirement`
|none
|`200 {"outcome": "RETIRED", "catalog", "id", "at"}`: the entry has `retired: true`; the references to it stay valid. A
retired entry is retired again without a change of the answer. There is no way back in the web app
|Right `administer-catalogs`; `404 NOT_FOUND`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-4[AUN-DIR-4]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/catalogs/import/backstage/preview`
|`{"documents": [{"name", "content"}]}`: the descriptor documents as text, at most 100, together at most 2 MiB
|`{"outcome": "PREVIEW", "created", "updated", "unchanged", "leftOut"}`: what the import would do; nothing is written
(<<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>)
|Right `administer-catalogs`; `422 VALIDATION` (`/documents`, `REQUIRED` or `TOO_MANY`; `/documents/0/content`, `FORMAT`
for text that is no YAML); `413 TOO_LARGE` with `limit`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-6[AUN-DIR-6]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/catalogs/import/backstage`
|As the preview
|`{"outcome": "IMPORTED", "created", "updated", "unchanged", "leftOut"}`: the import is written in one commit
|As the preview, and `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-6[AUN-DIR-6]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/directory/persons`
|`{"persons": [{"name", "memberships": [{"team", "role"}]}]}`: one to 100 entries, each with a name and one to five
memberships (catalog references)
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "persons": [{"pseudonym", "name", "state", "memberships"}]}`: the saved, unbound entries,
in the order sent, in one commit
|Right `administer-catalogs`; `422 VALIDATION`, pointers such as `/persons/1/memberships/0/team`
(`REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND`), `/persons/0/name` (`REQUIRED`, `MAX_LENGTH`), `/persons/0/memberships` (`MIN_ITEMS`),
`/persons` (`TOO_MANY`); a person directory that is not `git` answers `409 MANAGED_BY_SOURCE`; `REJECTED`;
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-8[AUN-DIR-8]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/directory/erasures`
|`{"pseudonym"}`
|`200 {"outcome": "ERASED", "pseudonym", "erasedOn"}`: the erasure took effect at once; the pseudonym then resolves as
`former` (`GET /api/v1/directory/persons/{pseudonym}`) and is no longer listed
|Right `administer-catalogs`; `409 MANAGED_BY_SOURCE` with the member `type` `managed-by-source` (the problem type of
the requirement; the answer names no person); `404 NOT_FOUND` for a pseudonym without an entry; `REJECTED`;
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-3[AUN-DP-3]
|`SPECIFIED`; body, answer and the outcome name `PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/directory/persons?q=<text>`
|none; `q` optional
|`{"persons": [{"pseudonym", "name", "state", "memberships": [{"team", "role"}]}], "truncated"}`: the person entries a
document may reference, the bound ones (`state` `bound`) and the prefilled, unbound ones (`unbound`), ordered by
name, at most 100; `q` keeps the entries whose name contains the text, upper and lower case alike; `truncated` is `true`
when more entries matched. `team` and `role` are catalog references (`team:payments`). The erased are not listed. A
client filters the entries it received by the typed text as well, which covers only a complete answer: with
`truncated` `true` the answer is incomplete, and the client asks again with `q` and does not present its local filter
as the full set of matches. The server filters by `q`. The exact body is in <<_form_kit_requests>>. The administration
page `/directory/persons` reads the same list
|`403 FORBIDDEN` for a user without a mapped right (the entries are personal data, as the binding candidates of
xref:directory.adoc#AUN-DIR-2[AUN-DIR-2] are)
|xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-5[AUN-DP-5], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/directory/persons/{pseudonym}`
|none
|One person as the form shows them: `{"pseudonym", "name", "state", "memberships", "erasedOn"}`; `state` is `bound`,
`unbound` or `former`. A former member, an erased person, has `name: null`, the memberships recorded at the
erasure and `erasedOn`, the date of the erasure; the other states have `erasedOn: null`
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a pseudonym that resolves to no entry and to no erasure; `403 FORBIDDEN` as above
|xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-3[AUN-DP-3], xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-5[AUN-DP-5],
https://github.com/cuioss/git-document-repository/blob/main/doc/specification/git-directory.adoc#GDR-DIR-4[GDR-DIR-4]
|`PROVISIONAL`

6+h|Forms

|`GET /api/v1/categories`
|none
|`{"categories": [{"id", "generic", "kinds": [{"id", "repository"}]}]}`: the categories that are switched on and have a
repository the user may read. `generic` is `true` for a category that the generic documents lens shows (a custom
category, the test category `test-note`) and `false` for one with a lens of its own (SAMM, threat models, incidents,
ISMS). The web app shows the lens *Documents* in the rail only when one category is `generic`
(<<_the_document_lifecycle>>). The exact body is in <<_document_lifecycle_requests>>
|`403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:../use-cases/09-categories.adoc#change-web-3[UC-KND, AUN-WEB-3 change], xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#AUN-GEN-1[AUN-GEN-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`; step 10 extends it with the administration of categories

|`GET /api/v1/categories/{category}/schema`
|none
|`{"category", "kinds": [{"id", "repository", "schema"}]}`: per document type (kind) of the category its id, the alias
of its repository and its JSON Schema as the file `<kind>/schema.json` holds it, markers included
(xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4]). The same for every user; the request asks no more than a right on one of the
category's repositories. The exact body is in <<_form_kit_requests>>; the schema of `threat-models` is in <<_threat_model_data>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for an unknown category, or for one that is switched off and holds no documents; a switched-off category
that still holds documents is answered like any other, so that its read-only sheets have a form to show; `403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4], xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#AUN-GEN-1[AUN-GEN-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/render/asciidoc`
|`{"source"}`: the AsciiDoc source, a string of at most 65,536 bytes in UTF-8
|`{"html"}`: the source rendered by the server in safe mode `secure` as an embeddable fragment and sanitised against
the allow-list of xref:web-app.adoc#_asciidoc_preview[AUN-WEB-4, AsciiDoc Preview]. Nothing is stored or logged; the
request changes no state, and carries the anti-CSRF header as every `POST` does. An empty source answers
`{"html": ""}`
|`413 TOO_LARGE` with `limit`, the number of bytes (nothing is rendered); `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/source`
and the code `REQUIRED` when `source` is missing, or `TYPE` when it is no string; `503 RENDER_UNAVAILABLE` when the
renderer cannot run (the form then shows no preview and goes on); a user without a mapped right is refused `FORBIDDEN`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-8[AUN-WEB-8]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/references/resolve?ref=<reference>&ref=<reference>`
|none; `ref` is repeated, at most 50 times, each value URL-encoded (`#` as `%23`)
|`{"references": [{"ref", "found", "title", "address", "retired"}]}`, one entry per `ref`, in the order asked. For a
reference that resolves: `found` `true`, the `title` of the target (the name of a catalog entry), its canonical
`address` (xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-3[AUN-WEB-3]) or `null` when the target has none, `retired` `true` for a retired catalog entry. For one that
does not, or that points into a repository the user may not read: `found` `false` and `title`, `address` `null`
|`422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/ref` and the code `REQUIRED` for no `ref` or `TOO_MANY` for more than 50
|xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-2[AUN-DP-2],
https://github.com/cuioss/git-document-repository/blob/main/doc/specification/git-forms.adoc#GDR-FORMS-8[GDR-FORMS-8]
|`PROVISIONAL`

6+h|Documents and release

|`GET /api/v1/documents/{repository}`
|none; the optional query `kind` and `state` narrow the list
|`{"repository", "documents": [{"id", "kind", "title", "state", "version", "updatedAt", "updatedBy", "release",
"requested", "address"}], "truncated"}`: the documents of the repository, newest change first, at most 500; `truncated`
is `true` when more matched. A client filters by kind and state itself, which is complete only while `truncated` is
`false`; with `true` it asks again with the query and the server filters. The exact body is in
<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>
|`403 FORBIDDEN` for a repository the user may not read; `404 NOT_FOUND` for an unknown alias. Served from the last
fetched state while the Git server is unreachable
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-1[UC-DOC-1], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-3[AUN-WEB-3]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/search?q=<text>`
|none; `q` required
|`{"query", "results": [{"repository", "id", "category", "kind", "title", "state", "address"}], "truncated"}`: the
documents of every category whose title or id contains the text, upper and lower case alike, ordered by title, at most
20, only from repositories the user may read. Served from the index, also while the Git server is unreachable. The
exact body is in <<_document_lifecycle_requests>>
|`422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/q` and the code `REQUIRED` (missing), `MIN_LENGTH` (fewer than 2 characters after
trimming) or `MAX_LENGTH` (more than 200)
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-1[UC-DOC-1], xref:mcp-server.adoc#AUN-MCP-6[AUN-MCP-6] (`search`)
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/{repository}`
|`{"kind", "parts": {"<part>": {"fields"}}}`: the first save of a new document of a document type of the repository;
`fields` as in the save, without `blob`
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "id", "repository", "address", "state", "version", "at", "parts"}`: the server assigns the
id (a ULID) and writes the first commit; `parts` holds the new blob ids
|`VALIDATION` per field (`part` and `pointer` as in the save); `FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `REJECTED`;
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `404 NOT_FOUND` for an unknown `kind`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-3[UC-DOC-3], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/documents/{repository}/{id}`
|none; the optional query `at=<revision>` reads the document as it was at a release or a commit
|The document with its parts (one file each, with the blob id loaded), its state and its signed history. For the
documents of the generic lens, and for every category from now on, the shape of <<_document_lifecycle_requests>>:
`parts` carry their `value`, and the members `version`, `frozen`, `draft`, `transitions`, `releaseRequest` and, for a
revision, `at` are added. `samm-assessments` keeps its own shape (shape PROVISIONAL)
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a document or a revision that does not exist, or that the user may not read; `422 VALIDATION` with
the pointer `/at` and the code `FORMAT` for a value that is no revision
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5], xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-1[AUN-SAMM-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/documents/{repository}/{id}/revisions`
|none
|`{"repository", "id", "revisions": [{"ref", "commit", "kind", "tag", "version", "at", "by"}], "truncated"}`: every
commit of the document's working history and every release, newest first, at most 200; `ref` is what `at` takes: `v<n>`
for a release, the short commit id for any other commit
|`404 NOT_FOUND`; `403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-2[UC-DOC-2], xref:mcp-server.adoc#AUN-MCP-6[AUN-MCP-6] (`read_document` with a revision)
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/documents/{repository}/{id}/diff?from=released`, and, PROVISIONAL, `?from=<revision>&to=<revision>`
|none; `from` is `released` or a revision (`v<n>`, a commit id), `to` an optional revision or `working` (the default)
|The structured diff of the YAML data against the last released state, grouped by schema section: `{"repository",
"id", "from", "to", "sections": [{"part", "changes": [{"pointer", "before", "after"}]}]}` (shape PROVISIONAL); `from`
is `null` before the first release. The view groups the changes of one part by the first segment of the pointer, the
property of the schema (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>). With two revisions it is the comparison of two versions of the ISMS
documents (<<_isms_documents>>); `from` and `to` then hold the tag of a release, the commit id of a commit or `working`
|`422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/from` or `/to` and the code `FORMAT` (not a revision) or `REQUIRED` (no `from`); `404
NOT_FOUND` for a revision that does not exist
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-6[AUN-WEB-6], xref:../use-cases/07-isms.adoc#UC-ISMS-1[UC-ISMS-1]
|`SPECIFIED` for `from=released`; the two-revision form `PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/drafts/{repository}/{id}`
|`{"parts": {"<part>": {"blob", "fields"}}}`; with step-up by password `"password"`
|`{"outcome": "DRAFT_SAVED", "at"}`
|`VALIDATION` per field: a draft with field errors is refused and nothing is written (PROVISIONAL, <<_decided_lifecycle>>);
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE` does not apply, a draft stays in the application
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/{repository}/{id}/save`
|`{"parts": {"<part>": {"blob", "fields"}}}`, the blob ids the form loaded; with step-up by password `"password"`
|`{"outcome": "SAVED", "at", "parts"}` with the new blob ids; the first save after a release of a document type
released more than once also answers `"state": "draft"` and the new `"version"`
|`CONFLICT` per part with the fields that differ, own and other value, and who saved them; `VALIDATION`, among them
`REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND`; `DOCUMENT_RELEASED`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`. The bodies are in
<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5], xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#operation-outcomes[AUN-GEN, Operation Outcomes]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/{repository}/{id}/transitions/{t}`
|none, or `{"password"}`; `{t}` is a transition of the category's workflow, such as `samm.submit` or `test-note.submit`
|`{"outcome": "TRANSITIONED", "state"}`; with `"version"` and `"at"` (PROVISIONAL)
|`MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING` (`422`) with `missing`, the JSON Pointers of the empty fields; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` when the
workflow requires step-up for the transition, and `503 TOO_MANY_LOGINS` when it cannot be started
(<<_the_step_up_handler>>); `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `FORBIDDEN` without the transition's right; `NOT_FOUND` for
an unknown transition. The release is no transition of this request: it is the next row
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-4[AUN-SAMM-4] (the transition), xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#operation-outcomes[AUN-GEN, Operation Outcomes]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/releases/{repository}/{id}`
|none; with local users `{"password"}`. The request names no reviewed revision (PROVISIONAL, <<_decided_lifecycle>>)
|`{"outcome": "RELEASED", "tag", "at"}`
|`STEP_UP_REQUIRED` (`403`, PROVISIONAL) with `stepUp`, the step-up address (none with local users: the web app asks for
the password); `503 TOO_MANY_LOGINS` with `Retry-After: 60` when the step-up cannot be started because every place of
pending logins is taken (nothing is written, PROVISIONAL status); `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` (`401`, local users, nothing is
written); `MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW` (`409`, PROVISIONAL: someone
saved after the release was opened); `DOCUMENT_RELEASED` (`409`, PROVISIONAL: someone released the document
meanwhile, the body names the `tag`); `FORBIDDEN` without `approve`. The handling of the step-up outcomes is the contract
of <<_the_step_up_handler>>
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-6[AUN-WEB-6], xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-4[AUN-AUTH-4], xref:authentication.adoc#AUN-AUTH-6[AUN-AUTH-6]
|`SPECIFIED`; the outcome `CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW` and the status of `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` and `TOO_MANY_LOGINS` `PROVISIONAL`

6+h|SAMM

|`GET /api/v1/samm/models`
|none
|The pinned model versions: `{"models": [{"version", "source", "checksum", "pinnedAt", "default"}]}`, newest first, the one
the application takes for a new scope or assessment has `default: true`. A version that is not in the list cannot be
chosen. The exact body is in <<_samm_requests>>
|`403 FORBIDDEN`; an empty `models` for an installation that pins none (the web app then offers neither a new scope nor a
new assessment)
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-1[AUN-SAMM-1], xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-7[UC-SAMM-7]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/samm/models/{version}`
|none
|A pinned model version: business functions, practices, streams, questions and their answer sets
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a version that is not pinned
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-1[AUN-SAMM-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/samm/scopes`
|none
|The scopes with their latest released and their open assessment: `{"scopes": [{"id", "title", "subject", "latest",
"open", "assessments"}]}`
|none
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-2[AUN-SAMM-2]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/samm/scopes`
|`{"title", "subject", "modelVersion", "targets"}`: a new scope; `subject` is `team:<id>`, `service:<id>` or
`organisation`; `targets` (optional) maps a practice id to `{"target", "due"}`. The exact body is in <<_samm_requests>>
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "id", "document", "address", "at", "parts"}`: the server assigns the address segment `id` and
the document id and writes the first commit
|`422 VALIDATION` per field (`/title`, `/subject`, `/modelVersion`, `/targets/<practice>/target`, `/targets/<practice>/due`),
among them `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` for a subject that does not resolve (AUN-SAMM-2); `FORBIDDEN` without `edit` on
`samm-assessments`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-2[AUN-SAMM-2], xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-6[AUN-SAMM-6],
xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-1[UC-SAMM-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/samm/scopes/{scope}`
|none
|The scope: subject, model version, targets and the assessments with state, score and release; with `document` and the
`parts` `data` and `targets` (blob ids, the blob of the next save of the targets). The exact body is in <<_samm_requests>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND`
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-2[AUN-SAMM-2], xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-6[AUN-SAMM-6]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/samm-assessments/{document}/save`
|`{"parts": {"targets": {"blob", "fields"}}}`: the roadmap of a scope, the generic save of the scope as a document
(<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>); `{document}` is the `document` of the scope read, not the address segment; `fields` maps `/<practice>` to `{"target", "due"}`, or to `null` to remove the target
of the practice
|`{"outcome": "SAVED", "at", "parts"}` with the new blob id of `targets`
|`CONFLICT` per practice with the target of both sides; `VALIDATION` with `part` `targets`; `FORBIDDEN` without `edit`;
`REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`. A scope has no draft, no workflow and no release: the request is direct and never answers
`DOCUMENT_RELEASED`. The bodies are in <<_samm_requests>>
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-6[AUN-SAMM-6], xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-1[UC-SAMM-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/samm/{scope}/assessments`
|`{"title", "date", "modelVersion", "assessors"}`: a new assessment of the scope; `assessors` are further pseudonyms of the
directory, bound or not; the user who creates is always the first assessor. The exact body is in <<_samm_requests>>
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "id", "document", "scope", "address", "state": "draft", "at", "parts"}`: the assessment is
empty; `answers` and `evidence` are empty and every practice file is written by its first save
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a scope that does not exist; `409 ASSESSMENT_OPEN` with `assessment`, the address segment of the
assessment that is in `draft` or `in-review` already (PROVISIONAL: at most one is open); `422 VALIDATION` per field
(`/title`, `/date`, `/modelVersion`, `/assessors`, `/assessors/<n>` with `PSEUDONYM_UNKNOWN`); `FORBIDDEN` without `edit`;
`REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-1[AUN-SAMM-1], xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-2[UC-SAMM-2],
xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-5[AUN-DP-5]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/samm/{scope}/trend`
|none; the optional query `modelVersion` names the version of the points (default: the one of the latest released
assessment)
|The scores of the released assessments of the scope over time, oldest first, each with the overall, the business function
and the practice scores, unrounded; `omitted` counts the released assessments of other model versions. The exact body is
in <<_samm_requests>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND`; an empty `points` for a scope without a released assessment
|xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-5[UC-SAMM-5]; AUN-SAMM-5 names no trend
|`PROVISIONAL`. The trend of every scope in one answer is `GET /api/v1/reports/samm-trend` (<<_reports_requests>>)

|`GET /api/v1/samm/{scope}/compare?from=<assessment>&to=<assessment>`
|none
|Per business function, practice and stream: score `from`, score `to`, difference (shape PROVISIONAL)
|`422 MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER` (status PROVISIONAL) with `from` and `to`, each `{"assessment", "modelVersion"}`; `404
NOT_FOUND` for an assessment that is no assessment of the scope; `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/from` or `/to` and
the code `REQUIRED`. The two assessments may be in any state: an open one is read as saved (PROVISIONAL). The bodies are in
<<_samm_requests>>
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-5[AUN-SAMM-5]
|`SPECIFIED`; the status of `MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER` and the comparison of an open assessment `PROVISIONAL`. The page
`/reports/samm-compare` of the lens *Reports* reads this request and adds none (<<_reports_requests>>, where the `200`
body is written out)

|`GET /api/v1/samm/{scope}/gaps`
|none
|The gaps of the latest released assessment of the scope (UC-SAMM-6): `{"scope", "today", "assessment": {"id", "document", "title",
"date", "modelVersion", "release"} \| null, "gaps": [{"practice", "function", "score", "target", "gap", "due", "reference",
"risk"}]}`, the practices whose released score is below their target, in the order of the model; `reference` is
`samm-assessments:assessment/<id>#<practice>`, `risk` the risk of the register that names it or `null`. The exact body is in
<<_isms_origin>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND`; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `read` on `samm-assessments`; `"assessment": null` and `"gaps": []` for a scope without a
released assessment; the assessment and `"gaps": []` for a scope with a released assessment but without targets
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-6[AUN-SAMM-6], xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-6[UC-SAMM-6]
|`PROVISIONAL`; the requirement says "list every practice whose score is below its target", not how

|`GET /api/v1/documents/samm-assessments/{document}`
|none
|One assessment in the SAMM shape, which keeps its own layout: the answers per practice and question, the loaded `parts`,
the signed `history`, the `release` and, new, `evidence` per practice. The comment of an answer is an AsciiDoc fragment.
A released assessment reads `state` `released` and is frozen. The exact body is in <<_samm_requests>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND`
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-1[AUN-SAMM-1], xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-7[AUN-SAMM-7]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/drafts/samm-assessments/{document}` and `POST /api/v1/documents/samm-assessments/{document}/save`
|The generic body (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>); `{document}` is the `document` of the assessment read; the part is a practice id, `fields` maps `<question>/answer`,
`<question>/comment` and `evidence` (the whole list of the practice's evidence links) to the new value
|As the generic request: `DRAFT_SAVED`, `SAVED` with the new blob id of the practice
|`CONFLICT` per practice; `VALIDATION` per field, among them the evidence (`PATTERN` for a link that is not an absolute
`https` address, `REQUIRED`, `MAX_LENGTH`, `MAX_ITEMS`); `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` with `tag` for a released assessment,
for the draft and the save alike (the assessment is frozen, the draft stays); `FORBIDDEN`; `REJECTED`;
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`. The bodies are in <<_samm_requests>>
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-3[AUN-SAMM-3], xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-4[AUN-SAMM-4],
xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-7[AUN-SAMM-7]
|`SPECIFIED`; the evidence key, its refusals and the draft of a frozen assessment `PROVISIONAL`

6+h|Threat models

|`GET /api/v1/threat-models/{service}`
|none
|The page of one service in the lens *Threat models*: `{"today", "service": {"id", "name", "lifecycle", "retired", "owner",
"protectionNeed", "reviewInterval"}, "reviewIntervals", "models": [{"model", "document", "title", "address", "state",
"version", "lastReview", "daysSinceReview", "reviewDue", "dueInDays", "status", "threats", "requested", "updatedAt"}],
"drafts": [{"recipe", "step", "at"}]}`: the threat models of the service (none is a service without a model), the review
intervals of the kind and the person's own unfinished recipe drafts. The first column of the lens is the answer of
`GET /api/v1/reports/threat-evaluation`, which this request does not repeat. The exact body is in <<_threat_model_service>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a service that is in no catalog; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `read` on `threat-models`. Served from the
last fetched state while the Git server is unreachable
|xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-4[AUN-TM-4], xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc#UC-TM-4[UC-TM-4]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/threat-models/{service}/{model}`, optional query `at=<revision>`
|none; `at` reads the model as it was at a release (`v<n>`) or a commit
|One threat model: the generic document read of <<_document_lifecycle_requests>> (`parts` with the files `data` and
`model`, `state`, `version`, `frozen`, `draft`, `transitions`, `releaseRequest`, `history`, `release`; `at` for a revision)
and the members `service`, `model`, `title`, `address`, `review`, `threats` and `diagram`. The address segment `model` is
the one the server derived at the creation. The exact body, the data shape of the files and the schema are in
<<_threat_model_data>> and <<_threat_model_read>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a service, a model or a revision that does not exist, or that the user may not read; `422 VALIDATION`
with the pointer `/at` and the code `FORMAT`; `403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-1[AUN-TM-1], xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-2[AUN-TM-2],
xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-3[AUN-TM-3], xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc#UC-TM-2[UC-TM-2]
|`PROVISIONAL`; the data shape is open (<<_decided_threat_models>>)

|`POST /api/v1/threat-models/{service}`
|`{"recipe", "parts": {"data": {"fields"}, "model": {"fields"}}}`: the first save of a new threat model of the service;
`recipe` is optional (a model without a recipe), `fields` as in the generic save, without `blob`
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "model", "document", "address", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "at", "parts"}`: the server
writes `service` and `recipe`, derives the address segment `model` from `/project/name` and the document id (a ULID) and
writes the first commit; the recipe draft of the service and the recipe ends with it
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for an unknown service; `409 SERVICE_RETIRED`; `422 VALIDATION` per field (`part`, `pointer`, `code`;
`/project/name` `REQUIRED` is the only mandatory field of a first save), among them `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` for an owner role
that is in no catalog and `ENUM` for a recipe that does not exist; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `REJECTED`;
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-1[AUN-TM-1], xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-4[AUN-TM-4],
xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc#UC-TM-1[UC-TM-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`; a bespoke create, because the service is the path and the server derives the address segment (the creates
of a SAMM scope and assessment are the model)

|`GET /api/v1/documents/threat-models`, `GET …/{document}`, `GET …/{document}/revisions`, `GET …/{document}/diff?from=released`
|none
|The generic requests of <<_the_document_lifecycle>> for the repository `threat-models`: the list (the `address` of a row is
`/threat-models/<service>/<model>`), the document in the generic shape (also with `at`), its revisions and the diff against
the last release, grouped by part (`data`, `model`) and by the first segment of the pointer (`/threats`, `/mitigations`, …).
The release view reads these; the sheet reads the request by address above
|As the generic requests; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `read`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-1[UC-DOC-1], xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-2[UC-DOC-2],
xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-6[AUN-WEB-6]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/drafts/threat-models/{document}` and `POST /api/v1/documents/threat-models/{document}/save`
|The generic body (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>) with the parts `data` and `model` in one request; `fields` maps a JSON
Pointer into the value of the part to its new value. The parts, the pointers and the lists that are sent as a whole are in
<<_threat_model_save>>
|As the generic request: `DRAFT_SAVED`, `SAVED` with the new blob id of every part saved; the first save after a release
also answers `"state": "draft"` and the next `"version"`
|`CONFLICT` per part with the fields (the lists) that differ; `VALIDATION` per field, among them `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` for
a countermeasure whose threat is not in the model; `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` does not apply (a threat model is released more
than once); `FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5], xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-4[AUN-TM-4]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/threat-models/{document}/transitions/threat-model.submit` and `POST /api/v1/releases/threat-models/{document}`
|As the generic requests: none, or `{"password"}`
|`TRANSITIONED` (`draft` to `in-review`, right `edit`, no step-up) and `RELEASED` with the tag `models/<document>/v<n>`
(`in-review` to `released`, right `approve`, step-up). The release is the shared flow of the release view; the review
date of the model is content, so nothing is written after the release (UC-TM-2)
|As the generic requests: `MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING` (the schema's `required`, as pointers into the document's value: `/owner`,
`/last-review`, `/model/project/name`), `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`, `CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW`, `DOCUMENT_RELEASED`, `FORBIDDEN`, `REJECTED`,
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-6[UC-DOC-6], xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-7[UC-DOC-7],
xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-3[AUN-TM-3]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/schema`
|none
|The schema of the document type `threat-model` as the form kit reads it (<<_threat_model_data>>); the request of the form kit
(<<_form_kit_requests>>), not offered for SAMM, is offered for this category
|`403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4], xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-2[AUN-TM-2]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/recipes?lang=en\|de`
|none; a missing `lang` returns English
|`{"recipes": [{"id", "title", "description", "steps"}]}`: the recipes that ship with the application, in the order of the
list `steps` being the number of steps. The exact body is in <<_threat_model_recipes>>
|`403 FORBIDDEN`; `400` for any other `lang`; an empty list for an installation that ships none (the wizard is then not
offered)
|xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-5[AUN-TM-5], xref:mcp-server.adoc#AUN-MCP-6[AUN-MCP-6] (`read_recipe`)
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/recipes/{recipe}?lang=en\|de`
|none
|One recipe: `{"id", "title", "description", "steps": [{"id", "title", "guidance", "fills"}]}`; `guidance` is an AsciiDoc
fragment, `fills` a JSON Pointer into the model file (`/project`, `/components`, …). The exact body is in
<<_threat_model_recipes>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a recipe that does not exist; `403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-5[AUN-TM-5], xref:mcp-server.adoc#AUN-MCP-6[AUN-MCP-6] (`read_recipe`)
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/threat-models/{service}/drafts/{recipe}`
|none
|The unfinished recipe of the person for the service: `{"service", "recipe", "draft": {"step", "at", "parts": {"model":
{"fields"}, "data": {"fields"}}}}`, or `"draft": null` for none. The draft of a model that does not exist yet (UC-DOC-3 before
UC-DOC-4)
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for an unknown service or recipe; `403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-3[UC-DOC-3], xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc#UC-TM-1[UC-TM-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/threat-models/{service}/drafts/{recipe}`
|`{"step", "parts": {"model": {"fields"}, "data": {"fields"}}}`: the draft is replaced as a whole; `step` is the index of the
step the person is on, the number of steps for the final check
|`{"outcome": "DRAFT_SAVED", "at"}`
|`422 VALIDATION` per field (`part`, `pointer`, `code`; `/step` `MAXIMUM`): a draft with field errors is refused and nothing is
written; `404 NOT_FOUND`; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE` does not apply, a draft stays in the application
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-3[UC-DOC-3], xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5]
|`PROVISIONAL`

6+h|Incident reports

|`GET /api/v1/incidents`
|none
|The first column of the lens *Incidents*, the years: `{"today", "years": [{"year", "repository", "current", "incidents",
"open", "withMissedDeadline", "retentionEnd", "address"}]}`, newest year first, one entry per repository
`isms-incidents-<year>` the user may read. `current` is `true` for the year of today; a year with no entry marked
`current` has no repository yet, which the page says (UC-OPS-2). The exact body is in <<_incident_years>>
|`403 FORBIDDEN` is not answered: a user without a right on any incident repository gets `"years": []`
(PROVISIONAL, as for the other lists). Served from the last fetched state while the Git server is unreachable
|xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-1[AUN-IR-1], xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-1[AUN-DP-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/incidents/{year}`
|none
|The incidents of the year, the second column: `{"today", "asOf", "year", "repository", "incidents": [{"id", "title",
"address", "state", "version", "frozen", "severity", "categories", "personalDataAffected", "nis2Significant",
"awareAt", "resolvedAt", "stages": [{"id", "status"}], "deadlines": {"open", "met", "missed", "next"}, "requested",
"updatedAt", "updatedBy", "release"}], "truncated"}`, newest awareness first, at most 500. The stage statuses and the
deadline figures are the index of the use case (<<_incident_list>>)
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a year with no repository, or one the user may not read; `403 FORBIDDEN` is `404`
(AUN-DP-2, PROVISIONAL)
|xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-2[AUN-IR-2], xref:../use-cases/06-incidents.adoc#UC-IR-2[UC-IR-2],
xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_in_the_web_app[Staged Documents, In the Web App]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/incidents/{year}/{incident}`, optional query `at=<revision>`
|none; `at` reads the incident as it was at a release (`v1`) or a commit
|One incident: the generic document read of <<_document_lifecycle_requests>> (`parts` with the file `data` and one part
per stage, `state`, `version`, `frozen`, `draft`, `transitions`, `releaseRequest`, `history`, `release`; `at` for a
revision) and the members `year`, `address`, `asOf`, `stages`, `deadlines` and `protection`. The address segment is the
document id, a ULID. The exact body is in <<_incident_read>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for a year, an incident or a revision that does not exist, or that the user may not read; `422
VALIDATION` with the pointer `/at` and the code `FORMAT`
|xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-1[AUN-IR-1], xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-4[AUN-IR-4],
xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc[Staged Documents]
|`PROVISIONAL`; the data shape is open (<<_decided_incidents>>)

|`GET /api/v1/incidents/{year}/{incident}/report`, optional query `at=<revision>`
|none
|The generated report of the incident: `{"repository", "id", "title", "source", "asOf", "adoc"}`. `source` is `generated`
(a fresh generation from the saved working state, a preview) or `committed` (the `report.adoc` of the release). The
`adoc` is rendered by `POST /api/v1/render/asciidoc`. The exact body is in <<_incident_report>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND`; `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/at` and the code `FORMAT`; `503 GIT_UNAVAILABLE` does not apply
(served from the last fetched state)
|xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-1[AUN-IR-1], xref:../use-cases/06-incidents.adoc#UC-IR-3[UC-IR-3]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/categories/incident-reports/schema`
|none
|The schema of the document type `incident` as the form kit reads it (<<_incident_data>>): the request of the form kit
(<<_form_kit_requests>>) for this category; the parts of the document are the top-level properties marked `gdr-split`
|`403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4], xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-1[AUN-IR-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-{year}`, `GET …/{id}`, `GET …/{id}/revisions`, `GET …/{id}/diff?from=released`
|none
|The generic requests of <<_the_document_lifecycle>> for the repository of the year: the list (the `address` of a row is
`/incidents/<year>/<id>`), the document in the generic shape (the same body as the request by address above), its
revisions and the diff against the last release, grouped by part, which is the stage (`data`, then the stages in the
declared order), and within a part by the first segment of the pointer, the section. An incident has no earlier
release, so `from` is `null` and every field is an addition. The release view reads these; the lens reads the requests
by address above
|As the generic requests; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `read`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-1[UC-DOC-1], xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-2[UC-DOC-2],
xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-6[AUN-WEB-6]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-{year}`
|`{"kind": "incident", "parts": {"data": {"fields"}, "triage": {"fields"}}}`: the first save of a new incident, the
generic create; `fields` as in the save, without `blob`
|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "id", "repository", "address", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "at", "parts"}`: the server
assigns the id (a ULID) and writes the first commit into the repository of the year; the part `triage` and every
stage that carries a field are written, the others are absent until their first save
|`422 VALIDATION` per field (`part`, `pointer`, `code`; `/title` `REQUIRED` in the part `data` is the only mandatory
field of a first save); `404 NOT_FOUND` for a year with no repository; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `REJECTED`;
`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` and `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` as for every request that can ask, with the member `password`
of the body (<<_decided_incidents>> point 48)
|xref:../use-cases/06-incidents.adoc#UC-IR-1[UC-IR-1], xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-3[AUN-IR-3]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/drafts/isms-incidents-{year}/{id}` and `POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-{year}/{id}/save`
|The generic body (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>) with the parts in one request, any of `data` and the five stages;
`fields` maps a JSON Pointer into the value of the part to its new value. Notes on the parts and on `completion`
are in <<_incident_save>>
|As the generic request: `DRAFT_SAVED`, `SAVED` with the new blob id of every part saved
|`CONFLICT` per part with the fields that differ; `VALIDATION` per field, among them `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` for an
affected service or a risk that is in no catalog or register and `READ_ONLY` for a pointer to `completion`;
`409 STAGE_LOCKED` with `stages` for a write into a completed stage; `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` with `tag` once the
incident is released; `FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-5[AUN-WEB-5], xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_stage_status[Staged Documents, Stage
Status]
|`SPECIFIED`; body and answer `PROVISIONAL`; `STAGE_LOCKED` and the `READ_ONLY` of `completion` follow the concept

|`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-{year}/{id}/stages/{stage}/complete`
|none, or `{"blob", "password"}`: `blob` is the blob id of the part the form loaded (optional), `password` the step-up
password of a local user
|`200 {"outcome": "COMPLETED", "stage", "status": "completed", "at", "completion": {"at", "by"}, "parts", "stages":
[{"id", "status"}]}`: a save of the part onto the working branch, which writes `completion` (`at`, `by`); `parts` holds
the new blob id of the part, `stages` the statuses afterwards. With `step-up` declared for the stage (`review`) it asks
for step-up like a release (<<_the_step_up_handler>>). The exact bodies are in <<_incident_stages_requests>>
|`422 STAGE_NOT_COMPLETABLE` with `missing` (the JSON Pointers of the empty mandatory fields of the stage) and `open`
(the predecessors not completed); `409 STAGE_NOT_APPLICABLE`; `409 STAGE_STATE` with `status` for a stage that is
completed already; `409 CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW` for a `blob` that is no longer current; `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` with
`tag`; `404 NOT_FOUND` for a stage the document type does not declare; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`, `INVALID_CREDENTIALS`,
`TOO_MANY_LOGINS`; `FORBIDDEN` without `edit`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_stage_status[Staged Documents, Stage Status]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-{year}/{id}/stages/{stage}/reopen`
|none, or `{"blob", "password"}`
|`200 {"outcome": "REOPENED", "stage", "status": "open", "at", "parts", "stages"}`: a save that removes `completion`;
the earlier completion stays in the history. No other stage is reopened
|`409 STAGE_STATE` with `status` for a stage that is not completed; `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` with `tag`; `404
NOT_FOUND`; `CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW`; `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` (not asked in the shipped workflow, PROVISIONAL); `FORBIDDEN`
without `edit`; `REJECTED`; `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_stage_status[Staged Documents, Stage Status]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-{year}/{id}/transitions/incident.submit` and
`POST /api/v1/releases/isms-incidents-{year}/{id}`
|As the generic requests: none, or `{"password"}`
|`TRANSITIONED` (`draft` to `in-review`, right `edit`, no step-up) and `RELEASED` with the tag `incidents/<id>/v1`
(`in-review` to `released`, right `approve`, step-up, `incident.release`); the release also commits the generated
`report.adoc` and answers its path as `report`. An incident is released once: it is frozen afterwards
|`422 STAGES_OPEN` with `stages`, a list of `{"stage", "missing"}` for every applicable stage that is not completed
(PROVISIONAL, replaces `MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING` for a staged document, see <<_decided_incidents>>); `STEP_UP_REQUIRED`,
`CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW`, `DOCUMENT_RELEASED`, `FORBIDDEN`, `REJECTED`, `GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|xref:category-incident-reports.adoc#AUN-IR-3[AUN-IR-3], xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-6[UC-DOC-6],
xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-7[UC-DOC-7]
|`PROVISIONAL`

6+h|ISMS

|`GET /api/v1/isms/risks?as-of=<tag\|date>`
|none
|The risk register as of a tag or a date, released entries only, as it was at the commit that the tag or the date selects;
without `as-of` as of now: `{"asOf": {"requested", "date", "tag", "commit", "at"}, "tags": [{"tag", "commit", "at"}],
"risks": [{"id", "entry", "title", "rating", "treatment", "owner", "version", "releasedAt", "reviewDue", "reference",
"address"}]}`. The exact body is in <<_reports_requests>>
|`403 FORBIDDEN` without `read` on `isms-register`; `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/as-of` and the code `PATTERN`
(neither a calendar date nor a tag name), `FUTURE` (a date after today) or `UNKNOWN_TAG`; `503 GIT_UNAVAILABLE` for a
commit that is not indexed yet
|xref:category-isms.adoc#AUN-ISMS-3[AUN-ISMS-3], xref:../use-cases/07-isms.adoc#UC-ISMS-3[UC-ISMS-3]
|`SPECIFIED`; the answer's shape, the codes of `422` and `GIT_UNAVAILABLE` `PROVISIONAL`; read by the page

|`GET /api/v1/isms/register`
|none

(<<_form_kit_requests>>) for this category
|`403 FORBIDDEN`
|xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4], xref:category-isms.adoc#AUN-ISMS-1[AUN-ISMS-1],

|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/documents/isms-core`, `GET …/{id}`, `GET …/{id}/revisions`, `GET …/{id}/diff?from=released` and

|none; `at=<revision>` on the read
|The generic requests of <<_the_document_lifecycle>> for the repository `isms-core` (the list with `address`

|As the generic requests; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `read`
|xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-1[UC-DOC-1], xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc#UC-DOC-2[UC-DOC-2],
xref:../use-cases/07-isms.adoc#UC-ISMS-1[UC-ISMS-1], xref:category-isms.adoc#AUN-ISMS-1[AUN-ISMS-1]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-core`, `PUT /api/v1/drafts/isms-core/{id}`, `POST …/{id}/save`, `POST

|The generic bodies (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>); the create `{"kind": "policy" \| "isms-scope" \| "audit-report" \|

|`PROVISIONAL`

|`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-register`

|`201 {"outcome": "CREATED", "id": "R-043", "repository", "address", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "at", "parts"}`: the server

|`SPECIFIED` for the running number assigned at the first save; body, answer and the outcomes `PROVISIONAL`

|`PUT /api/v1/drafts/isms-register/{id}`, `POST /api/v1/documents/isms-register/{id}/save`, `POST
…/{id}/transitions/isms.submit` and `POST /api/v1/releases/isms-register/{id}`
|The generic bodies (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>)
|As the generic requests: `DRAFT_SAVED`, `SAVED`, `TRANSITIONED` (right `edit`) and `RELEASED` with the tag `risks/R-<n>/v<n>`
(right `approve`, step-up, `isms.release`); a risk is released more than once, a release merges the entry's path into `main`
|`MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING` (`/rating`, `/treatment`, `/owner`); `VALIDATION` per field, `READ_ONLY` for `/origin`; `CONFLICT`;

save reopens a released risk), only to a release that somebody else made first (PROVISIONAL, <<_isms_save_release>>);

|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/isms/risks/draft?origin=<reference>`
|none; `origin` is `samm-assessments:assessment/<id>#<practice>` or `threat-models:threat-model/<id>#<threat>`, `#` percent-encoded
|The risk the server prepares from a gap or a threat (UC-SAMM-6, UC-TM-3), stored nowhere: `{"origin": {"ref", "kind", "title",
"address"}, "fields": {"/title", "/description", "/owner", "/treatment"}}`. The exact bodies are in <<_isms_origin>>
|`404 NOT_FOUND` for an origin that does not exist or that the user may not read; `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/origin`
and the code `PATTERN` or `REQUIRED`; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `edit` on `isms-register`; `409 ORIGIN_HAS_RISK` with `risk`,
`ORIGIN_NOT_RELEASED`, `ORIGIN_NOT_A_GAP`, `ORIGIN_NOT_TRANSFERABLE`
|xref:category-samm.adoc#AUN-SAMM-6[AUN-SAMM-6], xref:category-threat-models.adoc#AUN-TM-4[AUN-TM-4],
xref:data-protection.adoc#AUN-DP-2[AUN-DP-2]
|`PROVISIONAL`; the requirements say "opens a new draft ... with a reference", not how

6+h|Reports

|`GET /api/v1/reports/summary`
|none
|The figures of the dashboard `/reports`, one section per repository the user may read: `{"today", "samm": {"scopes",
"withoutRelease", "open", "latest": [{"scope", "name", "assessment", "date", "overall", "change"}]}, "threatModels":
{"services", "withModel", "withoutModel", "openThreats", "untreatedThreats", "overdue", "dueSoon"}, "risks": {"entries",
"byRating", "dueSoon", "overdue", "latestRelease"}}`. A section the user may not read is absent, and no request fails for
it. The exact body is in <<_reports_requests>>
|none beyond the common refusals
|xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-5[UC-SAMM-5], xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc#UC-TM-4[UC-TM-4],
xref:../use-cases/07-isms.adoc#UC-ISMS-3[UC-ISMS-3]
|`PROVISIONAL`

|`GET /api/v1/reports/samm-trend`
|none
|The released assessments of every scope over time: `{"scopes": [{"scope", "title", "subject", "modelVersion", "omitted",
"points": [{"assessment", "document", "title", "date", "overall", "functions"}]}]}`, in the order of
`GET /api/v1/samm/scopes`, the points oldest first and unrounded. The points are those of
`GET /api/v1/samm/{scope}/trend` without `practices`. The exact body is in <<_reports_requests>>

`null` and `"points": []`

"model", "address", "state", "version", "lastReview", "daysSinceReview", "reviewDue", "dueInDays", "status", "threats":
{"total", "open", "untreated", "transferred"}}], "withoutModel": [{"service", "lifecycle", "owner", "protectionNeed",

[#_form_kit_requests]
== Form Kit Requests

The exact bodies of the requests of the form kit (xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4]); every one is PROVISIONAL.
The values are those of the resources-only test category `test-note`
(xref:architecture-and-modules.adoc#AUN-GEN-1[AUN-GEN-1]).

*The schema of a category*, `GET /api/v1/categories/test-note/schema`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "category": "test-note",
  "kinds": [
    {
      "id": "note",
      "repository": "test-notes",
      "schema": {
        "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["title", "noted", "status"],
        "properties": {
          "title":   { "type": "string", "maxLength": 120 },
          "noted":   { "type": "string", "format": "date-time" },
          "status":  { "type": "string", "enum": ["open", "agreed", "dropped"] },
          "pinned":  { "type": "boolean" },
          "service": { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "service" },
          "owner":   { "type": "string", "x-person": true },
          "related": { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link" },
          "summary": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 500, "x-free-text": true },
          "body":    { "type": "string", "x-asciidoc": true },
          "actions": {
            "type": "array",
            "maxItems": 20,
            "items": {
              "type": "object",
              "required": ["what"],
              "properties": {
                "what": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                "who":  { "type": "string", "x-person": true },
                "due":  { "type": "string", "format": "date" },
                "done": { "type": "boolean" }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
----

The schema is the content of the file, unchanged. The labels of this schema are the catalogue's keys
`category.test-note.note.field.title`, `….field.actions.what` and so on
(xref:web-app.adoc#_labels_and_texts[AUN-WEB-4, Labels and Texts]).

*The preview*, `POST /api/v1/render/asciidoc`:

[source,json]
----
{ "source": "== Decision\n\nWe *agree* to rotate the keys, see `ops/keys`." }
----

answers `200`

[source,json]
----
{ "html": "<h2>Decision</h2><div><p>We <strong>agree</strong> to rotate the keys, see <code>ops/keys</code>.</p></div>" }
----

and a source above 65,536 bytes answers `413`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "TOO_LARGE", "limit": 65536 }
----

A body without `source`, or with a `source` that is no string, answers `422`
`{"outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [{"pointer": "/source", "code": "REQUIRED"}]}` (`TYPE` for a number or an object).

*The persons*, `GET /api/v1/directory/persons?q=ha`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "persons": [
    { "pseudonym": "p-7d52e8", "name": "Mira Haas", "state": "unbound",
      "memberships": [ { "team": "team:mobile", "role": "role:developer" } ] }
  ],
  "truncated": false
}
----

and one person, `GET /api/v1/directory/persons/p-0f8a42`, here an erased one:

[source,json]
----
{ "pseudonym": "p-0f8a42", "name": null, "state": "former", "erasedOn": "2026-09-30",
  "memberships": [ { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:developer" } ] }
----

*The references*, `GET /api/v1/references/resolve?ref=service:ledger&ref=isms-register:risk/R-999`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "references": [
    { "ref": "service:ledger", "found": true, "title": "Ledger", "address": "/catalogs/service/ledger",
      "retired": true },
    { "ref": "isms-register:risk/R-999", "found": false, "title": null, "address": null, "retired": false }
  ]
}
----

*A refusal of the server* that the form shows at the fields, `422`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "outcome": "VALIDATION",
  "errors": [
    { "pointer": "/related", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND" },
    { "pointer": "/owner", "code": "PSEUDONYM_UNKNOWN" },
    { "pointer": "/actions/1/what", "code": "MAX_LENGTH" }
  ]
}
----

The pointers of the form are those into the form's own value (xref:web-app.adoc#_values[AUN-WEB-4, Values]). A refusal
of the save of a document names its pointers into the request body of that request, and how the two map onto each
other is the request's (<<_decided_forms>>, the last point).

[#_the_document_lifecycle]
== The Document Lifecycle

The requests of this section serve the use cases UC-DOC-1, -2, -3, -4, -6, -7, -8 and -9
(xref:../use-cases/02-documents.adoc[Use Cases: Documents]). Everything here is PROVISIONAL where the rows say so; the
exact bodies are in <<_document_lifecycle_requests>>, the decisions in <<_decided_lifecycle>>.

*The workflow of `test-note`.* The test category `test-note` has one document type, `note` (repository `test-notes`,
collection `notes`), released more than once. States `draft`, `in-review`, `released`; the transition
`test-note.submit` (`draft` to `in-review`, right `edit`, no step-up) and the release `test-note.release`
(`in-review` to `released`, right `approve`, step-up; the tag is `notes/<id>/v<n>`). A save is possible in every state.
The first save after a release starts the next version: the document is `draft` again, `version` is one higher and
`release` still names the last release, which the diff compares with. A document type released once (a SAMM
assessment) answers `DOCUMENT_RELEASED` instead and reads `frozen: true`.

*Versions.* `version` is the number of the working state of the document: `1` until the first release, `n+1` after the
release `v<n>` and the first save that follows. A released document has `version` equal to the number of its last
release.

*Single-file document types* have one part, named `data`; its `value` is the document's data, the value the form edits
(xref:web-app.adoc#_values[AUN-WEB-4, Values]).

*Reading while the Git server is unreachable.* Lists, reads, revisions and the search are served from the last fetched
state and answer `200`; `status.degraded` says that they may be old. Writes answer `503 GIT_UNAVAILABLE`, except the
draft, which stays in the application and is written.

[#_the_generic_documents_lens]
=== The Generic Documents Lens and Its Addresses

The documents of a category without a lens of its own (a custom category, `test-note`) are shown by the generic lens
*Documents* (German *Dokumente*), the ninth entry of the rail, between *ISMS* and *Catalogs*. It is in the rail only
while `GET /api/v1/categories` lists a category with `generic: true`. PROVISIONAL: the use cases of the categories
name it as an open point (xref:../use-cases/09-categories.adoc#open-points[UC-KND, Open Points], point 3, and the
change `change-web-3`).

[cols="2,3,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Shows

|`categories`
|`/categories`
|The generic categories (`GET /api/v1/categories`), one column; with one category the lens opens it

|`categories.category`
|`/categories/{category}`
|The documents of every document type of the category: title, state, version, last change; filter by state; the action
*New*

|`categories.new`
|`/categories/{category}/new?kind=<kind>`
|The form of a new document of the document type `kind` (without `kind`, the first one, or a choice when the category
has several); nothing is stored before the first save

|`categories.document`
|`/categories/{category}/{document}`, optional query `at`
|The document sheet: the form, the workflow acts, the history; with `at` the document as it was at that revision,
read-only

|`categories.release`
|`/categories/{category}/{document}/release`
|The review and release of the document
|===

`{category}` is a category id, a segment as in the other addresses. `{document}` is a document id, a ULID: it needs its
own placeholder pattern `document`, `[0-9A-Za-z][0-9A-Za-z._-]{0,127}`, because the default pattern of a segment is
lower case and a ULID is upper case. The segments `new` and `edit` (the editor of step 10) are reserved: their routes
come before `categories.document` in the file, and no document has such an id. The address names the category and the
document, never the repository: the web app finds the document among the repositories of the category's document types
(`GET /api/v1/categories` names them) and reads `404 NOT_FOUND` as an address that does not exist. An address under
`/categories/{category}` of a category that is not `generic` shows the not-found view, and so does one of a custom
category that is not switched on and holds no documents. A custom category that is switched off but still holds
documents is listed with `"enabled": false` (the member is absent for a category that is on): its generic addresses stay
readable, because the references to its documents and the search results resolve through them
(xref:../use-cases/09-categories.adoc[UC-KND-5], _Switched off with documents_), but they are read-only: the document
sheet and the revisions offer no save, no workflow act and no release, `new` shows the not-found view, and neither the
rail entry nor the lens counts the category. The writes answer `403 FORBIDDEN`. PROVISIONAL, see
<<_open_questions>>. The delivered categories keep their own addresses.

[#_allowed_queries]
=== Allowed Queries of an Address

Until now an address was a path. An address gains an *allowed query set*: a few named parameters that belong to the
view and are part of its canonical form. `canonical-addresses.json` has two additions (data; the excerpt shows them as they are in the
file):

[source,json]
----
{
  "params":  { "year": "[0-9]{4}", "document": "[0-9A-Za-z][0-9A-Za-z._-]{0,127}" },
  "queries": { "at": "(v[0-9]{1,4}|[0-9a-f]{7,40})", "kind": "[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}" },
  "addresses": [
    { "route": "categories",          "pattern": "/categories" },
    { "route": "categories.category", "pattern": "/categories/{category}" },
    { "route": "categories.new",      "pattern": "/categories/{category}/new", "query": ["kind"] },
    { "route": "categories.document", "pattern": "/categories/{category}/{document}", "query": ["at"] },
    { "route": "categories.release",  "pattern": "/categories/{category}/{document}/release" }
  ]
}
----

(`queries` is a new top-level member like `params`: name to value pattern; `query` is a new optional member of an
address: the names it admits, in the order they are written. The addresses are inserted in file order: `categories.new`
before `categories.document`.) The rules:

* A path is matched first, as today. The query string is read as `name=value` pairs split at `&`, each percent-decoded
  once; a `+` stays a plus.
* *Reading an address* (`parseAddress(pathname, search)`): a name the address does not admit is ignored, so a tracking
  parameter breaks no link; a name it admits whose value does not match the pattern of `queries`, or that appears twice,
  makes the address not found (`null`). The address carries its admitted parameters as `query`.
* *Writing an address* (`formatAddress`): the admitted names that have a value, in the order of the file, each value
  checked against its pattern; nothing else is written.
* *The `return` of a login* (`checkReturn`, AUN-AUTH-1) takes path and query: the path must be a canonical address,
  and every name must be one that address admits, with a matching value, each once; anything else, a fragment included,
  falls back to `/`. Without this, the step-up of a view that shows `?at=v1` would return to the start page.
* The query string is at most 256 characters; the path stays without query in the routes of the mock
  (`CanonicalAddresses.isCanonical(path)`).
* `?at=` is allowed on `categories.document` and on `isms.risk` (the risk as it was at a release, from the Reports step);
  later steps add it to the address of the other lenses. The Reports step adds the names `scope`, `from`, `to` and `as-of`
  for the four reports, each with its own pattern (<<_reports_addresses>>).
  The threat models add `at` on `threat-models.model` and the name `recipe` on the recipe wizard
  (<<_threat_model_addresses>>).
  The incident reports add `at` and the name `stage` on `incidents.incident` (<<_incident_addresses>>).
  The ISMS adds the name `origin` on `isms.risks.new`, `kind` on `isms.documents` and `isms.documents.new`, `at` on
  `isms.document` and `from` and `to` on `isms.document.compare` (<<_isms_addresses>>).

What the code does for it (built by the mock and UI steps, not by this contract):

[cols="2,3"]
|===
|File |Change

|`web-app/src/main/resources/META-INF/audit-notary/canonical-addresses.json`
|The two members above and the five addresses

|`web-app/src/lib/routes.ts`
|`CanonicalFile` gains `queries` and `query`; `compileAddresses` keeps each address's admitted names and value
patterns; `Address` gains `query?: Record<string, string>`; `parseAddress(pathname, search = '')` reads it; `formatAddress`
writes it; `checkReturn` splits the candidate at the first `?` and checks both parts; `Lens` and `LENSES` gain
`categories` (rail order: after `isms`), `routeTitleKey` gives `lens.categories`

|`web-mock/.../runtime/CanonicalAddresses.java`
|`of(JsonNode)` reads `queries` and `query`; `isCanonical(path)` stays; `checkReturn(candidate)` splits at the first
`?` and applies the rules above; the mock's login keeps redirecting to the checked `return` with its query

|`audit-notary/.../security/ReturnAddresses.java`
|Not part of the mock and UI steps: it still checks the path alone and returns it without the query, for every address that
admits one (`at`, `kind`, `recipe`, ...). It follows the rules above in the server step of the login, designed against Token
Sheriff's session layer (ADR-0004), not before
|===

[#_release_requests]
=== Release Requests

An agent's `request_release` (UC-DOC-8) has no web request: the web app only shows what it left. PROVISIONAL, because
the storage in Git is open (xref:../concepts/mcp-server.adoc#_open_questions[MCP Server, Open Questions]):

* The request is an item of `GET /api/v1/due` for the requesting user, `kind` `release`, with `requestId`,
  `requestedBy`, `requestedAt` and `comment`. Its `address` follows the state of the document: the document's address
  while it is in its first state (the user submits it there), the release address once it is in review.
* An approver sees the document in review as `kind` `review`; the item carries the same four members when a request
  exists, so that the comment is read before the release (decided provisionally: a pending request is shown to the
  approvers too).
* The document read carries `releaseRequest` (`null` without a request), so that the sheet and the review show the
  comment and who asked.
* The request ends with the release of the document; a document edited further keeps it. A release request carries no
