  reviewed revision: see the open question in <<_open_questions>>.

[#_lifecycle_events]
=== Events of the Lifecycle

PROVISIONAL, all on `GET /api/v1/events`, only for repositories the user may read:

[cols="2,3"]
|===
|Event |Data

|`document-saved`
|`{"repository", "id", "fields"}` as specified, with the optional members `part` (the part the save changed) and `by`
`{"pseudonym", "name"}`; for a document of the generic lens `fields` are JSON Pointers into the value of `part`. The
open form re-reads the document and refreshes the fields not edited locally

|`document-created`
|`{"repository", "id", "kind"}`; the lists refresh

|`document-transitioned`
|`{"repository", "id", "state", "version", "by"}`, for a submit and for a release; the open sheet re-reads, the lists and
Due refresh

|`release-requested`
|`{"repository", "id", "requestId"}`; Due refreshes
|===

[#_document_lifecycle_requests]
== Document Lifecycle Requests

The exact bodies of the requests of the document lifecycle, PROVISIONAL like the rows. The values are those of the test
category `test-note`; pseudonyms and catalog entries are those of the form kit (<<_form_kit_requests>>).

*The categories*, `GET /api/v1/categories`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "categories": [
    { "id": "test-note", "generic": true, "kinds": [ { "id": "note", "repository": "test-notes" } ] }
  ]
}
----

*The list*, `GET /api/v1/documents/test-notes`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "repository": "test-notes",
  "documents": [
    { "id": "01JZ8R7A9C1E3G5J7M9P1S3V5X", "kind": "note", "title": "Retention schedule for audit evidence",
      "state": "draft", "version": 1, "updatedAt": "2026-11-26T08:12:00Z",
      "updatedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" },
      "release": null, "requested": true,
      "address": "/categories/test-note/01JZ8R7A9C1E3G5J7M9P1S3V5X" },
    { "id": "01JZ8Q4Y5N6V7X8B9C0D1E2F3G", "kind": "note", "title": "Key rotation, decision of 24 November",
      "state": "released", "version": 1, "updatedAt": "2026-11-24T10:05:00Z",
      "updatedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" },
      "release": { "tag": "notes/01JZ8Q4Y5N6V7X8B9C0D1E2F3G/v1", "at": "2026-11-24T10:05:00Z" }, "requested": false,
      "address": "/categories/test-note/01JZ8Q4Y5N6V7X8B9C0D1E2F3G" }
  ],
  "truncated": false
}
----

`title` is the value of the property `title`; `requested` is `true` while a release request is pending; `release` is
the last release.

*The search*, `GET /api/v1/search?q=rotation`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "query": "rotation",
  "results": [
    { "repository": "test-notes", "id": "01JZ8Q4Y5N6V7X8B9C0D1E2F3G", "category": "test-note", "kind": "note",
      "title": "Key rotation, decision of 24 November", "state": "released",
      "address": "/categories/test-note/01JZ8Q4Y5N6V7X8B9C0D1E2F3G" }
  ],
  "truncated": false
}
----

A query of one character answers `422` `{"outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [{"pointer": "/q", "code": "MIN_LENGTH"}]}`.
No hit is an empty `results`.

*A document*, `GET /api/v1/documents/test-notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B` (the data is shortened):

[source,json]
----
{
  "repository": "test-notes",
  "id": "01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B",
  "category": "test-note",
  "kind": "note",
  "title": "Vendor onboarding questions",
  "state": "released",
  "version": 2,
  "frozen": false,
  "parts": {
    "data": {
      "blob": "7be21c4",
      "savedAt": "2026-11-20T14:02:00Z",
      "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber", "role": "Developer" },
      "value": { "title": "Vendor onboarding questions", "noted": "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z", "status": "agreed" }
    }
  },
  "draft": null,
  "history": [
    { "at": "2026-11-20T14:30:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" }, "kind": "release",
      "tag": "notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B/v2" },
    { "at": "2026-11-20T14:02:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber" }, "kind": "save",
      "part": "data", "count": 2 }
  ],
  "release": { "tag": "notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B/v2", "at": "2026-11-20T14:30:00Z",
               "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } },
  "releaseRequest": null,
  "transitions": []
}
----

* `transitions` are the transitions the workflow offers in the current state, whoever the user is (the web app reads
  the rights of the session): `{"id", "to", "right", "stepUp"}`, and `"release": true` on the one that is the release.
  A draft offers `{"id": "test-note.submit", "to": "in-review", "right": "edit", "stepUp": false}`; a document in
  review offers `{"id": "test-note.release", "to": "released", "right": "approve", "stepUp": true, "release": true}`;
  a released document offers none (a save reopens it). The release is requested with `POST /api/v1/releases/…`, any
  other transition with `POST …/transitions/{id}`.
* `draft` is the user's own unsaved draft, `null` without one: `{"at", "parts": {"data": {"blob", "fields"}}}`, `blob`
  the blob id the draft was started from (it may be older than `parts.data.blob`, when someone saved since) and
  `fields` as in a save.
* `frozen` is `true` for a document that refuses every save (a released SAMM assessment, a revision).
* `releaseRequest` is `{"id", "by", "at", "comment"}` or `null`.
* `history` is the newest lines of the signed history, at most 20, the kinds `create`, `save`, `submit`, `release`, and for a staged
  document `complete` and `reopen` (with the `stage`, <<_incident_stages_requests>>).

*A revision*, `GET /api/v1/documents/test-notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B?at=v1`: the same shape, as the document was
then, with the member `at`, and `frozen: true`, `draft: null`, `transitions: []`, `releaseRequest: null`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "…": "…",
  "state": "released",
  "version": 1,
  "frozen": true,
  "at": { "ref": "v1", "kind": "release", "commit": "c0ffee1", "tag": "notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B/v1",
          "at": "2026-11-05T09:40:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } }
}
----

`at` accepts `v<n>` (the release of that version), a commit id of seven to forty hexadecimal characters, or the full
tag (what an agent sends; the web app does not). A value of another shape answers `422`
`{"outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [{"pointer": "/at", "code": "FORMAT"}]}`; a revision that does not exist answers
`404 NOT_FOUND`.

*The revisions*, `GET /api/v1/documents/test-notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B/revisions`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "repository": "test-notes",
  "id": "01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B",
  "revisions": [
    { "ref": "v2", "commit": "9d41e07", "kind": "release", "tag": "notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B/v2",
      "version": 2, "at": "2026-11-20T14:30:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } },
    { "ref": "4be0c3a", "commit": "4be0c3a", "kind": "submit", "tag": null,
      "version": 2, "at": "2026-11-20T14:10:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber" } },
    { "ref": "7be21c4", "commit": "7be21c4", "kind": "save", "tag": null,
      "version": 2, "at": "2026-11-20T14:02:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber" } },
    { "ref": "v1", "commit": "c0ffee1", "kind": "release", "tag": "notes/01JZ8W1E3G5J7M9P1R3T5X7Z9B/v1",
      "version": 1, "at": "2026-11-05T09:40:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } },
    { "ref": "a3d9f20", "commit": "a3d9f20", "kind": "create", "tag": null,
      "version": 1, "at": "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber" } }
  ],
  "truncated": false
}
----

*The first save, a new document*, `POST /api/v1/documents/test-notes`:

[source,json]
----
{ "kind": "note", "parts": { "data": { "fields": {
    "/title": "Phishing drill notes", "/noted": "2026-11-26T09:30:00Z", "/status": "open" } } } }
----

answers `201`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "CREATED", "id": "01JZ9A3J5M7P9R1T3W5Y7B9D1F", "repository": "test-notes",
  "address": "/categories/test-note/01JZ9A3J5M7P9R1T3W5Y7B9D1F", "state": "draft", "version": 1,
  "at": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z",
  "parts": { "data": { "blob": "e51a7d3", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z",
                       "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } } } }
----

*A draft and a save*, `PUT /api/v1/drafts/test-notes/{id}` and `POST /api/v1/documents/test-notes/{id}/save`, the same
body:

[source,json]
----
{ "parts": { "data": { "blob": "3f9a1c2", "fields": {
    "/summary": "Rotate the signing keys every 90 days.",
    "/pinned": true,
    "/actions": [ { "what": "Write the runbook", "who": "p-2b7f10", "due": "2026-12-15" } ],
    "/service": null } } } }
----

`fields` maps a JSON Pointer into the value of the part to its new value; the form sends the properties of the top
level that changed, a list or a section as a whole; `null` removes the property. (For `samm-assessments` the keys are
those of the SAMM practice, `<question>/answer`; the key is opaque to the document controller.) With step-up by
password the body carries `"password"` as well. The save answers

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "SAVED", "at": "2026-11-26T09:45:00Z",
  "parts": { "data": { "blob": "b8c0d41", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:45:00Z",
                       "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } } } }
----

and the draft `{"outcome": "DRAFT_SAVED", "at": "2026-11-26T09:44:00Z"}`. A save after a release of this document type
adds `"state": "draft", "version": <n+1>`.

*Per-field refusals* of a draft, a save or a create, `422`: as in the form kit, with the part: the pointer is into the
value of the part named by `part`, so it is the pointer of the form (<<_decided_lifecycle>>, point 9):

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [
    { "part": "data", "pointer": "/related", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND" },
    { "part": "data", "pointer": "/owner", "code": "PSEUDONYM_UNKNOWN" },
    { "part": "data", "pointer": "/actions/1/what", "code": "MAX_LENGTH" } ] }
----

*A conflict*, `409`, per part with the blob id now current (the blob of the next save) and per field the own and the
other value, pointers into the value of the part:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "CONFLICT", "conflicts": [
    { "part": "data", "blob": "5d1e9a0", "fields": [
        { "pointer": "/summary", "own": "Rotate every 90 days.", "other": "Rotate every 60 days.",
          "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber", "role": "Developer" } },
        { "pointer": "/status", "own": "agreed", "other": "dropped",
          "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber", "role": "Developer" } } ] } ] }
----

The user decides per field; the next save carries the decided values as `fields` and `blob` `5d1e9a0`.

*A save to a document released meanwhile or frozen*, `409`:
`{"outcome": "DOCUMENT_RELEASED", "tag": "notes/01JZ8R7A9C1E3G5J7M9P1S3V5X/v1"}`; the draft stays.

*The refusals of the Git server*: `502 {"outcome": "REJECTED"}` (the push was refused) and
`503 {"outcome": "GIT_UNAVAILABLE"}`; nothing is written and the draft stays.

*A submit*, `POST /api/v1/documents/test-notes/{id}/transitions/test-note.submit`:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "TRANSITIONED", "state": "in-review", "version": 1, "at": "2026-11-26T09:50:00Z" }
----

or, `422`, with the empty mandatory fields (pointers into the document's value; for a single-part document the value
of its part; SAMM uses its own layout):

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING", "missing": [ "/noted", "/status" ] }
----

*A step-up demand*, `403`, as in the release: `{"outcome": "STEP_UP_REQUIRED", "stepUp": "/api/v1/auth/login?step-up=mfa"}`.
`401 {"outcome": "INVALID_CREDENTIALS"}` answers a wrong password.

*A release*, `POST /api/v1/releases/test-notes/{id}`, answers
`{"outcome": "RELEASED", "tag": "notes/01JZ8T5C7E9G1J3M5P7R9V1X3Z/v1", "at": "2026-11-26T10:05:00Z"}`, or `409`
`{"outcome": "CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW"}` when the working branch moved after the release was opened.

*A release request in Due*, `GET /api/v1/due` (the other members as before):

[source,json]
----
{ "kind": "release", "lens": "categories", "title": "Retention schedule for audit evidence",
  "address": "/categories/test-note/01JZ8R7A9C1E3G5J7M9P1S3V5X", "due": "2026-11-30",
  "requestId": "rr-01JZ8R9B1D3F5H7K9N1Q3S5V7X",
  "requestedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" }, "requestedAt": "2026-11-26T08:20:00Z",
  "comment": "Written from the interview notes; please submit it and release it." }
----

[#_outcomes_of_the_document_requests]
*The outcomes with their statuses.* A view decides by the outcome; the statuses are PROVISIONAL where no source fixes
them (the common refusals are above):

[cols="1,1,3"]
|===
|`outcome` |Status |Meaning

|`CREATED`, `SAVED`, `DRAFT_SAVED`, `TRANSITIONED`, `RELEASED`
|`201` for `CREATED`, else `200`
|Success

|`CONFLICT`
|`409`
|A file changed since the form loaded it; per part the fields that differ (AUN-WEB-5)

|`DOCUMENT_RELEASED`
|`409`
|The document is frozen or was released meanwhile; the draft stays

|`CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW`
|`409`
|The document was saved after the release was opened; nothing is written

|`MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING`
|`422`
|`missing`: the pointers of the empty required fields; nothing is written

|`REJECTED`
|`502`
|The Git server refused the push; nothing is written, the draft stays

|`STEP_UP_REQUIRED`
|`403`
|<<_the_step_up_handler>>

|`INVALID_CREDENTIALS`
|`401`
|A wrong password of a local user; not a lost session

|`GIT_UNAVAILABLE`
|`503`
|As in the common refusals
|===

[#_directory_and_catalog_requests]
== Directory and Catalog Requests

The requests of the use cases UC-ACC-4, UC-ACC-5 and UC-CAT-1 to UC-CAT-5
(xref:../use-cases/01-access.adoc[Use Cases: Access], xref:../use-cases/03-catalogs.adoc[Use Cases: Catalogs and
Persons]). The rows are in <<_requests>>, *Directory and catalogs*; this section fixes their bodies and the addresses
of the views. Every body is PROVISIONAL unless a row says otherwise; the decisions are in <<_decided_directory>>.

[#_directory_addresses]
=== The Addresses

The lens *Catalogs* already has `/catalogs`, `/catalogs/{catalog}` and `/catalogs/{catalog}/{id}`. The step adds four
addresses to `canonical-addresses.json`; the route ids of the administration pages start with `catalogs`, so that the
lens of an address, the first segment of its route id, is *Catalogs* although the path starts with `/directory`:

[cols="2,3,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Shows

|`catalogs`
|`/catalogs`
|The five catalogs as the first column: `team`, `role`, `service`, `system`, `external`, in this order, with their names
from the message catalogue (`catalog.<catalog>.name`). Needs no request; with a user's first visit the lens opens the
first catalog

|`catalogs.catalog`
|`/catalogs/{catalog}`
|The entries of the catalog (`GET /api/v1/catalogs/{catalog}`), retired ones marked; the action *New* with the right
`administer-catalogs`. An unknown catalog is the not-found view

|`catalogs.new`
|`/catalogs/{catalog}/new`
|The form of a new entry in the catalog's schema; nothing is stored before the save (`POST`), then the page moves to
the address of the entry. The segment `new` is reserved: no entry has the id `new`, and the route comes before
`catalogs.entry` in the file

|`catalogs.entry`
|`/catalogs/{catalog}/{id}`
|The entry sheet: the form of the schema, for a service also the effective protection needs, the dependencies and the
cycle notice; the acts *Save* and *Retire*, each only with the right. A retired entry is shown read-only

|`catalogs.import`
|`/directory/import`
|The Backstage import as a wizard: choose the descriptors, check what would happen (the preview), import, read the
result. Without the right the page says that importing needs it and shows nothing else

|`catalogs.persons`
|`/directory/persons`
|The person directory: the table of the persons (`GET /api/v1/directory/persons`) with their state, the prefill of
unbound entries and the erasure with a confirmation. Without the right the table is read-only: no prefill, no erasure

|`settings.profile`
|`/settings/profile`
|The own directory entry and "Is this still right?" (UC-ACC-5). The route comes before `settings.section`, so
`/settings/profile` is not a section called `profile`
|===

The prefix `/directory` is used only by the two administration pages; `/directory/*` never meets `/catalogs/{catalog}`.
There is no address `/directory` itself: it is the not-found view. The addresses admit no query.

[#_catalog_schemas]
=== The Schemas of the Catalogs

`GET /api/v1/catalogs/service/schema`, the schema of the richest catalog, written out; the `id` pattern leaves out the
reserved segments `new` and `schema`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "catalog": "service",
  "schema": {
    "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
    "type": "object",
    "required": ["id", "name", "lifecycle", "owner", "protection-needs"],
    "properties": {
      "id":          { "type": "string", "pattern": "^(?!(?:new|schema)$)[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" },
      "name":        { "type": "string", "maxLength": 120 },
      "description": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 500, "x-free-text": true },
      "system":      { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "system" },
      "lifecycle":   { "type": "string", "enum": ["planned", "active", "deprecated"] },
      "owner": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["team", "role"],
        "properties": {
          "team": { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "team" },
          "role": { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "role" }
        }
      },
      "repositories": {
        "type": "array", "maxItems": 20,
        "items": { "type": "string", "format": "uri", "maxLength": 300 }
      },
      "dependsOn": {
        "type": "array", "maxItems": 50, "uniqueItems": true,
        "items": { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": ["service", "external"] }
      },
      "protection-needs": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["confidentiality", "integrity", "availability"],
        "properties": {
          "confidentiality": { "type": "string", "enum": ["normal", "high", "very-high"] },
          "integrity":       { "type": "string", "enum": ["normal", "high", "very-high"] },
          "availability":    { "type": "string", "enum": ["normal", "high", "very-high"] }
        }
      },
      "data-classes": { "type": "array", "maxItems": 20, "items": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 60 } }
    }
  }
}
----

The other four, with the same `id` pattern and the same `name` (required) and `description`:

[cols="1,3"]
|===
|Catalog |Further properties

|`team`
|`parent`: `gdr-link` with `x-catalog: team` (Backstage `spec.parent`, AUN-DIR-6)

|`role`
|none

|`system`
|none

|`external`
|`kind`: `supplier`, `provider` or `other`
|===

`x-catalog` takes a catalog id or, for `dependsOn`, an array of them (<<_decided_directory>>, point 7). `retired` is not
a property of the form: the entry has it beside its `value`. The texts of the form are the catalogue keys
`category.catalog.<catalog>.field.<path>` with `.help` and `.option.<value>`: the form kit is given the category `catalog`
and the kind `<catalog>`. A catalog has no `required` mark on `id` while an entry is changed: the `id` is read-only
then, and the path decides.

[#_catalog_entries]
=== The Entries

`GET /api/v1/catalogs/service`:

[source,json]
----
{ "entries": [
    { "id": "customer-portal", "name": "Customer Portal" },
    { "id": "ledger", "name": "Ledger", "retired": true },
    { "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API" } ] }
----

`GET /api/v1/catalogs/service/payment-api` (the `effective` members are explained below):

[source,json]
----
{
  "catalog": "service", "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API", "retired": false,
  "value": {
    "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API", "description": "Payment processing for the web shop",
    "system": "system:webshop", "lifecycle": "active",
    "owner": { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:service-owner" },
    "repositories": ["https://git.example/payments/payment-api"],
    "dependsOn": ["service:customer-db", "service:identity-provider", "external:psp-provider"],
    "protection-needs": { "confidentiality": "high", "integrity": "high", "availability": "high" },
    "data-classes": ["personal", "payment"]
  },
  "effective": {
    "protectionNeeds": { "confidentiality": "very-high", "integrity": "very-high", "availability": "high" },
    "decidedBy": { "confidentiality": "service:customer-db", "integrity": "service:identity-provider",
                   "availability": "service:payment-api" },
    "dependencies": [
      { "ref": "external:psp-provider", "name": "Payment service provider", "depth": 1, "via": "service:payment-api",
        "retired": false, "counts": false, "protectionNeeds": null },
      { "ref": "service:customer-db", "name": "Customer database", "depth": 1, "via": "service:payment-api",
        "retired": false, "counts": true,
        "protectionNeeds": { "confidentiality": "very-high", "integrity": "high", "availability": "normal" } },
      { "ref": "service:identity-provider", "name": "Identity Provider", "depth": 1, "via": "service:payment-api",
        "retired": false, "counts": true,
        "protectionNeeds": { "confidentiality": "very-high", "integrity": "very-high", "availability": "high" } },
      { "ref": "external:cloud-hosting", "name": "Cloud hosting", "depth": 2, "via": "service:customer-db",
        "retired": false, "counts": false, "protectionNeeds": null }
    ],
    "cycle": null
  },
  "at": "2026-10-02T09:12:00Z",
  "history": [
    { "kind": "save", "at": "2026-10-02T09:12:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } },
    { "kind": "create", "at": "2026-02-11T13:40:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } }
  ]
}
----

The rules of `effective` (AUN-DIR-5; each one is also decided in <<_decided_directory>>, point 8):

* `protectionNeeds` is, per dimension, the highest value over the service itself and every dependency that counts; the
  order is `normal`, `high`, `very-high`.
* `dependencies` lists every service and external party reachable through `dependsOn`, transitively, each once, in
  breadth-first order (depth 1 first, within a depth by `ref`); `via` is the `ref` it was first reached from. A
  dependency `counts` when it is a service that is not retired and whose protection needs are known; an external party
  has none (`protectionNeeds` `null`), and a retired service is listed with the mark and does not count.
* `decidedBy` names, per dimension, the first of the service itself and then `dependencies` that counts and holds the
  highest value; the service itself is `service:<own id>`.
* `cycle` is `null`, or the `refs` of the services on a cycle in `dependsOn` that the service is on or reaches, in
  the order they depend on each other: `["service:reporting", "service:report-scheduler"]`. A cycle is a notice and
  never a refusal: the maximum is taken over every service reached once.
* For a catalog other than `service` `effective` is `null`.

Saving a service answers `{"outcome": "SAVED", "catalog": "service", "id": "payment-api", "at": "…"}`; the sheet reads the
entry again, which carries the new `effective`. A refusal is `422`:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [
    { "pointer": "/owner/team", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND" },
    { "pointer": "/dependsOn/1", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND" },
    { "pointer": "/name", "code": "REQUIRED" } ] }
----

The pointers are into `value`, the form's own value. Creating answers `201`
`{"outcome": "CREATED", "catalog": "service", "id": "notification-gateway", "address":
"/catalogs/service/notification-gateway", "at": "…"}`, and `409 {"outcome": "ID_TAKEN"}` for an id that exists, retired
or not. Retiring answers `200 {"outcome": "RETIRED", "catalog", "id", "at"}`; a change of a retired entry answers
`409 {"outcome": "RETIRED"}` and writes nothing.

[#_backstage_import]
=== The Backstage Import

The wizard reads the files the person chooses in the browser and sends their text; the application fetches nothing
from Backstage (AUN-DIR-6). The request, preview or import:

[source,json]
----
{ "documents": [
    { "name": "catalog-info.yaml", "content": "apiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1\nkind: Component\n…" } ] }
----

The answer of the preview (`PREVIEW`) and of the import (`IMPORTED`) has one shape:

[source,json]
----
{
  "outcome": "IMPORTED",
  "created": [
    { "catalog": "service", "id": "checkout-ui", "name": "Checkout UI" },
    { "catalog": "system", "id": "storefront", "name": "Storefront" },
    { "catalog": "team", "id": "storefront", "name": "Storefront" }
  ],
  "updated": [
    { "catalog": "service", "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API", "changed": ["lifecycle", "owner.team", "dependsOn"] },
    { "catalog": "team", "id": "payments", "name": "Payments", "changed": ["parent"] }
  ],
  "unchanged": 4,
  "leftOut": [
    { "entity": "component:default/billing-batch", "field": "spec.lifecycle", "reason": "LIFECYCLE_UNMAPPED", "detail": "experimental" },
    { "entity": "component:default/search-index", "field": null, "reason": "DEFAULTS_MISSING", "detail": "owner.role, protection-needs" },
    { "entity": "component:ops/legacy-export", "field": null, "reason": "NAMESPACE_NOT_DEFAULT", "detail": "ops" },
    { "entity": "component:default/payment-api", "field": "spec.dependsOn[2]", "reason": "REFERENCE_KIND_NOT_SUPPORTED", "detail": "resource:default/payments-db" },
    { "entity": "api:default/payments-openapi", "field": null, "reason": "KIND_NOT_SUPPORTED", "detail": "API" }
  ]
}
----

* `created` and `updated` name the catalog entries; `changed` lists the fields of `value` an update changed, as dotted
  paths. `unchanged` is the number of entries the descriptors matched without a difference.
* A `leftOut` item names the Backstage entity as its entity reference (`kind:namespace/name`, written in lower case as senders should,
  link:https://backstage.io/docs/features/software-catalog/references[Backstage: Entity References]), the `field` of
  the descriptor, or `null` for the entity as a whole, the `reason` and a `detail`. The reasons are the ones AUN-DIR-6
  names: `NAMESPACE_NOT_DEFAULT`, `REFERENCE_KIND_NOT_SUPPORTED` (the entity is imported, the reference left out),
  `LIFECYCLE_UNMAPPED` (an existing service keeps its value, a new one is not created), `DEFAULTS_MISSING` (a new
  service without `directory.backstage.defaults` for `owner.role` and `protection-needs` is not created), and
  `KIND_NOT_SUPPORTED` for a kind other than `Component`, `System` and `Group`. The text of a reason is the catalogue key
  `import.reason.<reason in lower case, with hyphens>`.
* A repeated import answers the same shape with `created` empty: it updates, never duplicates, never deletes.
* A `422 VALIDATION` names the document by position: `{"pointer": "/documents/0/content", "code": "FORMAT"}`. Nothing is
  written then. A body above the limits answers `413 {"outcome": "TOO_LARGE", "limit": 2097152}`.

[#_persons_and_erasure]
=== Prefilled Persons and Erasure

`POST /api/v1/directory/persons`:

[source,json]
----
{ "persons": [
    { "name": "Nadia Kovac", "memberships": [ { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:developer" } ] },
    { "name": "Omar Haddad", "memberships": [ { "team": "team:mobile", "role": "role:developer" } ] } ] }
----

answers `201`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "CREATED", "persons": [
    { "pseudonym": "p-5c2d19", "name": "Nadia Kovac", "state": "unbound",
      "memberships": [ { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:developer" } ] },
    { "pseudonym": "p-a7e341", "name": "Omar Haddad", "state": "unbound",
      "memberships": [ { "team": "team:mobile", "role": "role:developer" } ] } ] }
----

and a team that is in no catalog answers `422` with `{"pointer": "/persons/1/memberships/0/team", "code":
"REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND"}`; nothing is saved when one entry fails. The same name twice is allowed (two people may share a
name); the page warns about a name that is already in the table.

`POST /api/v1/directory/erasures` with `{"pseudonym": "p-2b7f10"}` answers `200`
`{"outcome": "ERASED", "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "erasedOn": "2026-11-26"}`; with a person directory of the
organisation's own source `409 {"outcome": "MANAGED_BY_SOURCE", "type": "managed-by-source"}` and nothing is erased
here. The page then says that the person is erased in the organisation's own source. The erasure cannot be undone; the
confirmation says so and names the person only by the name the table shows.

[#_own_entry_and_binding]
=== The Own Entry, the Confirmation and the Binding

`GET /api/v1/directory/me` of a user with an entry:

[source,json]
----
{ "name": "Alex Example", "teams": ["payments"], "informationText": "…",
  "entry": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example",
             "memberships": [ { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:security-champion" } ],
             "confirmedAt": "2026-01-14", "confirmationDue": false } }
----

`PUT /api/v1/directory/me` with `{"name": "Alex Example", "memberships": [{"team": "team:payments", "role":
"role:developer"}]}` answers `200 {"outcome": "SAVED", "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "confirmedAt": "2026-11-26"}`.
`POST /api/v1/directory/me/confirmation` answers `200 {"outcome": "CONFIRMED", "confirmedAt": "2026-11-26"}`. Neither
needs step-up.

The first login with candidates (UC-ACC-4): `PUT /api/v1/directory/me` with `{"name": "Robin Newcomer", "team":
"payments", "role": "developer"}` and no `binding`, from a user who has a mapped right and whose membership a prefilled
entry holds, saves nothing and answers `409`:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "BINDING_CANDIDATES", "candidates": [
    { "pseudonym": "p-3d7a15", "name": "Robin Newcomer",
      "memberships": [ { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:developer" } ] },
    { "pseudonym": "p-8e2b40", "name": "Rowan Newman",
      "memberships": [ { "team": "team:payments", "role": "role:developer" } ] } ] }
----

The repeated request carries `"binding": "p-3d7a15"` (bind) or `"binding": "new"` (a new entry) and answers
`200 {"outcome": "SAVED", "pseudonym": "p-3d7a15"}`; `"binding": "p-0b0b0b"`, a pseudonym that is no candidate, answers
`409 {"outcome": "NOT_BINDABLE"}` and the form shows the candidates again. A user without a mapped right never gets
`BINDING_CANDIDATES`.

*The reconfirmation.* `GET /api/v1/session` carries `directoryConfirmationDue: true` while the own entry's confirmation is
older than the interval. The shell then shows a notice on every view with a link to `/settings/profile`, which asks "Is
this still right?" with the acts "Yes, this is right" (`POST …/confirmation`) and *Save* (a changed entry, which
confirms as well); the web app reads the session again after either, and the notice goes. The notice is no gate: nothing
else is blocked (<<_decided_directory>>, point 13).

*The event.* `catalog-changed` `{"catalog", "id", "by"}` on `GET /api/v1/events` tells an open catalog list or entry
sheet that someone saved, created or retired an entry; the web app reads the list and the entry again (PROVISIONAL).


[#_samm_requests]
== SAMM Requests

The requests of the use cases UC-SAMM-1 to UC-SAMM-5 beyond the ones of the first SAMM lens
(xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc[Use Cases: OWASP SAMM Assessments]). The rows are in <<_requests>>, *SAMM*; this section
fixes their bodies and the addresses of the new views. Every body is PROVISIONAL unless a row says otherwise; the
decisions are in <<_decided_samm>>. The values are those of the mock: the scopes `payments`, `identity`, `shop` and
`organisation`, the practice ids of the model (`sm`, `pc`, `eg`, `ta`, `sr`, `sa`, `sb`, `sd`, `dm`, `aa`, `rt`, `st`,
`im`, `em`, `om`) and today, 2026-11-26.

[#_samm_addresses]
=== The Addresses

The lens *SAMM* has `/samm`, `/samm/{scope}`, `/samm/{scope}/{assessment}`, `/samm/{scope}/{assessment}/release` and
`/samm/{scope}/{assessment}/{practice}`. The step adds two addresses to `canonical-addresses.json`, each before the
address whose placeholder it would otherwise be read as:

[cols="2,3,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Shows

|`samm.new`
|`/samm/new`
|The form of a new scope: title, subject (a team, a service or the organisation), the model version and the targets;
nothing is stored before the save (`POST /api/v1/samm/scopes`), then the page moves to the address of the scope. The
route comes before `samm.scope` in the file

|`samm.assessment.new`
|`/samm/{scope}/new`
|The form of a new assessment of the scope: title, date, model version, assessors; nothing is stored before the save
(`POST /api/v1/samm/{scope}/assessments`), then the page moves to the address of the assessment. The route comes before
`samm.assessment` in the file. Refused with a reason while the scope has an open assessment
|===

The segment `new` is reserved in `{scope}` and in `{assessment}`: the server never assigns it as an address segment (a
derived slug that equals it is treated like a collision and gets a number appended), and neither `models` nor `scopes` (they are paths of the API under `/api/v1/samm/`). `/samm/new/{x}` reads as the
assessment `{x}` of a scope `new`, which does not exist: the not-found view. The targets editor, the trend and the way
to start a new assessment have no address of their own: they are parts of the scope sheet and of the frozen assessment.
The new addresses admit no query.

=== The Pinned Models

`GET /api/v1/samm/models`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "models": [
    { "version": "v2.2.0", "source": "https://github.com/owaspsamm/core/tree/v2.2.0/model",
      "checksum": "sha256:1b9d4e0c7a52f8316d0e94ab5c27f3d8e61a09b4c2d57e8f130a64b9c8d71e2f", "pinnedAt": "2026-02-03",
      "default": true },
    { "version": "v2.1.0", "source": "https://github.com/owaspsamm/core/tree/v2.1.0/model",
      "checksum": "sha256:6e30a97d12c4b5f808e1d7a3490c6b2f5d81e3a07c94b6d2185f0e7a3c49d86b", "pinnedAt": "2025-03-17",
      "default": false }
  ]
}
----

The versions are those of `samm.yaml` (AUN-SAMM-1), newest first; `default` is `true` for exactly one entry of a
non-empty list. The checksum is that of the vendored model files; the values above are fixtures. Both tags exist in
https://github.com/owaspsamm/core/tags[owaspsamm/core]. A version that is not listed answers `404 NOT_FOUND` on
`GET /api/v1/samm/models/{version}` and is refused with `ENUM` where a body names it. The web app has no request that
pins a version (UC-SAMM-7 is configuration): it shows the list in the form of a new scope and a new assessment.

=== A New Scope

`POST /api/v1/samm/scopes`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "title": "Mobile Apps",
  "subject": "team:mobile",
  "modelVersion": "v2.2.0",
  "targets": {
    "sm": { "target": 2 },
    "ta": { "target": 2.5, "due": "2027-03-31" },
    "sb": { "target": 1.5 }
  }
}
----

`subject` is `team:<id>`, `service:<id>` or `organisation` (AUN-SAMM-2), the form a `CatalogPicker` stores; `title` is
1 to 200 characters; `modelVersion` is a pinned version, the default when absent; `targets` is optional: a practice of
the model maps to a `target`, one of 0, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2, 2.5 or 3, and an optional `due`, a date `YYYY-MM-DD`. A
practice without an entry has no target. The request answers `201`

[source,json]
----
{
  "outcome": "CREATED", "id": "mobile", "document": "mobile", "address": "/samm/mobile", "at": "2026-11-26T09:30:00Z",
  "parts": {
    "data": { "blob": "c41e07d", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:30:00Z",
              "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } },
    "targets": { "blob": "9a02f5b", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:30:00Z",
                 "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } }
  }
}
----

`id` is the address segment the server derives from the title (lower case, a number appended on a collision),
`document` the document id (a ULID in the application; the mock uses the same text as `id`). The refusals, `422`, with
pointers into the body, like the create of a catalog entry:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [
    { "pointer": "/title", "code": "REQUIRED" },
    { "pointer": "/subject", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND" },
    { "pointer": "/targets/ta/target", "code": "ENUM" },
    { "pointer": "/targets/rt/due", "code": "PATTERN" } ] }
----

The other codes: `MAX_LENGTH` and `TYPE` for `/title`, `PATTERN` for a `/subject` of none of the three forms,
`ENUM` for `/modelVersion` and for a practice id the model does not have (`/targets/<practice>`). A subject that does not
resolve is the refusal AUN-SAMM-2 asks for: the pointer names the reference. A user without `edit` on `samm-assessments`
gets `403 FORBIDDEN`.

=== A Scope and Its Targets

`GET /api/v1/samm/scopes/payments` keeps its members (the targets are shortened) and gains `document` and `parts`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "id": "payments", "document": "payments", "title": "Payments",
  "subject": { "kind": "team", "id": "payments", "name": "Payments" },
  "modelVersion": "v2.2.0",
  "targets": { "sm": { "target": 2 }, "ta": { "target": 2.5, "due": "2027-03-31" } },
  "parts": {
    "data": { "blob": "5e7a1d0", "savedAt": "2026-01-12T10:00:00Z",
              "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad", "role": "Chief Information Security Officer" } },
    "targets": { "blob": "b81c4f3", "savedAt": "2026-09-08T08:41:00Z",
                 "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad", "role": "Chief Information Security Officer" } }
  },
  "assessments": [ "…" ]
}
----

The targets are saved with the generic save of a document, the scope being the document: `POST
/api/v1/documents/samm-assessments/payments/save` with the part `targets`. `fields` maps `/<practice>` to the whole
entry of the practice, or to `null` to remove it; the form sends the practices that changed:

[source,json]
----
{ "parts": { "targets": { "blob": "b81c4f3", "fields": {
    "/ta": { "target": 3, "due": "2027-03-31" },
    "/rt": { "target": 2, "due": "2027-09-30" },
    "/om": null } } } }
----

It answers `{"outcome": "SAVED", "at": "2026-11-26T09:45:00Z", "parts": {"targets": {"blob": "a41f09c", "savedAt": "…",
"savedBy": {"…": "…"}}}}`. Per-field refusals, `422`, with the part and the pointer into the value of the part, which is
the map of the practices:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [
    { "part": "targets", "pointer": "/ta/target", "code": "ENUM" },
    { "part": "targets", "pointer": "/rt/due", "code": "PATTERN" } ] }
----

A conflict, `409`, per practice with the whole entry of both sides:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "CONFLICT", "conflicts": [
    { "part": "targets", "blob": "c05e92a", "fields": [
        { "pointer": "/ta", "own": { "target": 3, "due": "2027-03-31" }, "other": { "target": 2 },
          "by": { "pseudonym": "p-2b7f10", "name": "Jordan Weber", "role": "Team Lead" } } ] } ] }
----

A scope is no workflow: no draft (`PUT /api/v1/drafts/…` is not used for it), no submit, no release, never frozen. A save
writes one signed commit to `main`. The event `document-saved` (`part` `targets`) and, after a create, `document-created`
(`kind` `scope`) reach the other tabs; the lists and an open scope sheet read again.

=== A New Assessment

`POST /api/v1/samm/shop/assessments`:

[source,json]
----
{ "title": "Web shop 2026 Q4", "date": "2026-11-26", "modelVersion": "v2.2.0", "assessors": ["p-2b7f10"] }
----

`title` is 1 to 200 characters, `date` a date `YYYY-MM-DD`, `modelVersion` a pinned version (the default when absent),
`assessors` zero to 30 further pseudonyms of the person directory, bound or not yet bound (UC-SAMM-2); the user who
creates the assessment is always its first assessor, so a user can start one in an installation whose directory lists no
one else. The assessment starts empty. It answers `201`

[source,json]
----
{
  "outcome": "CREATED", "id": "2026-q4", "document": "shop-2026-q4", "scope": "shop",
  "address": "/samm/shop/2026-q4", "state": "draft", "at": "2026-11-26T09:35:00Z", "parts": {}
}
----

`id` is the address segment the server derives from the title within the scope (lower case, a number appended on a
collision), `document` the document id, `parts` empty: no practice file exists before its first save. The refusals:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [
    { "pointer": "/date", "code": "PATTERN" },
    { "pointer": "/modelVersion", "code": "ENUM" },
    { "pointer": "/assessors/1", "code": "PSEUDONYM_UNKNOWN" } ] }
----

and `404 NOT_FOUND` for an unknown scope. A scope that has an assessment in `draft` or `in-review` refuses a second one,
`409`:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "ASSESSMENT_OPEN", "assessment": "2026-q4", "state": "draft" }
----

The web app links the open assessment from this refusal. The event `document-created` (`kind` `assessment`) reaches the
other tabs.

=== The Assessment

`GET /api/v1/documents/samm-assessments/payments-2026-q4` keeps the SAMM shape of the first lens and gains `evidence`
(shortened):

[source,json]
----
{
  "id": "payments-2026-q4", "repository": "samm-assessments", "scope": "payments", "slug": "2026-q4",
  "title": "Payments 2026 Q4", "date": "2026-11-24", "modelVersion": "v2.2.0", "state": "draft",
  "assessors": [ { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example", "role": "Security Champion" } ],
  "answers": { "sb": { "sb-b-1": { "answer": 1,
      "comment": "Dependency scanning runs in *every* pipeline.\n\n* Scanner: the template of the platform team\n* The SBOM is not yet published." } } },
  "evidence": {
    "sb": [ { "question": "sb-b-1", "title": "Pipeline of the payment service", "url": "https://ci.example/payments/pipeline" },
            { "question": null, "title": "Dependency policy", "url": "https://wiki.example/payments/dependency-policy" } ]
  },
  "parts": { "sb": { "blob": "4f10c8e", "savedAt": "2026-11-25T09:48:00Z",
                     "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-9e33a1", "name": "Sam Okafor", "role": "Developer" } } },
  "history": [ "…" ],
  "release": null
}
----

* `comment` is an AsciiDoc fragment of at most 8,000 characters, shown with the preview of `POST /api/v1/render/asciidoc`.
* `evidence` maps a practice id to its links in the order they were entered; a practice without a link is absent, an
  assessment without any has `"evidence": {}` (the member is always present). `question` is a question id of the
  practice or `null` for the practice as a whole; `title` is 1 to 200 characters; `url` is an absolute `https` address of
  at most 2,048 characters; a practice holds at most 50 links. Evidence is a link, never a file (AUN-SAMM-7).
* The blob of a practice covers its answers, its comments and its evidence: they are one file per practice,
  `answers/<practice-id>.yaml`, as the use case change `change-evidence-per-practice` fixes (no separate evidence file),
  read and written together.
* A released assessment reads `"state": "released"`, `release` set; it is frozen.

*A save with a comment and evidence*, `POST /api/v1/documents/samm-assessments/payments-2026-q4/save`, the generic body:

[source,json]
----
{ "parts": { "sb": { "blob": "4f10c8e", "fields": {
    "sb-b-1/answer": 1,
    "sb-b-1/comment": "Dependency scanning runs in *every* pipeline.",
    "evidence": [ { "question": "sb-b-1", "title": "Pipeline of the payment service",
                    "url": "https://ci.example/payments/pipeline" } ] } } } }
----

The keys of the practice are `<question>/answer`, `<question>/comment` and `evidence`, which holds the whole list; an
empty list or `null` removes the evidence of the practice. The answer is the generic `SAVED` with the new blob of `sb`.
The draft takes the same body. The per-field refusals name the pointer into the assessment as the SAMM read shows it,
`/answers/<practice>/<question>/answer`, `…/comment` and `/evidence/<practice>/<n>/<member>`:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "VALIDATION", "errors": [
    { "part": "sb", "pointer": "/evidence/sb/1/url", "code": "PATTERN" },
    { "part": "sb", "pointer": "/evidence/sb/0/title", "code": "REQUIRED" },
    { "part": "sb", "pointer": "/answers/sb/sb-b-1/comment", "code": "MAX_LENGTH" } ] }
----

`PATTERN` is a `url` that is no absolute `https` address (`http://…`, a relative path, a `file:` address), `REQUIRED` an
empty `title` or `url`, `MAX_LENGTH` a text above its limit, `MAX_ITEMS` the pointer `/evidence/<practice>` with more
than 50 links, `ENUM` a `question` the practice does not have. A conflict on the evidence is one field,
`/evidence/<practice>`, whose `own` and `other` are the two lists.

*A frozen assessment.* Every write to a released assessment, the draft, the save, the submit and the release alike,
answers `409`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "DOCUMENT_RELEASED", "tag": "assessments/payments-2026-q2/v1" }
----

and nothing is written. The web app does not offer a write for it: the sheets are read-only with the release as seal and
one act, *Start a new assessment*, which opens `/samm/{scope}/new` (the scope of the assessment). That form prefills the
model version of the frozen assessment when it is pinned and refuses with the reason when `ASSESSMENT_OPEN` is the answer.
An assessment released while it was open elsewhere answers the save with the same `409`; the draft stays and the sheet
reads the assessment again.

=== The Trend

`GET /api/v1/samm/payments/trend` (shortened; every point holds the five business functions and the fifteen practices):

[source,json]
----
{
  "scope": "payments", "modelVersion": "v2.2.0", "omitted": 0,
  "points": [
    { "assessment": "2025-q4", "document": "payments-2025-q4", "title": "Payments 2025 Q4", "date": "2025-12-08",
      "overall": 0.9583333333333334,
      "functions": { "governance": 0.9583333333333334, "design": 1.0, "implementation": 1.25,
                     "verification": 0.8333333333333334, "operations": 0.75 },
      "practices": { "sm": 1.125, "pc": 0.625, "eg": 1.125, "…": "…" } },
    { "assessment": "2026-q2", "document": "payments-2026-q2", "title": "Payments 2026 Q2", "date": "2026-06-16",
      "overall": 1.3583333333333332,
      "functions": { "governance": 1.4166666666666667, "design": 1.6666666666666667, "implementation": 1.5416666666666667,
                     "verification": 0.9583333333333334, "operations": 1.2083333333333333 },
      "practices": { "sm": 1.25, "pc": 1.125, "eg": 1.875, "…": "…" } }
  ]
}
----

The points are the released assessments of the model version `modelVersion`, oldest first by `date`, scores unrounded as
AUN-SAMM-5 asks. `omitted` is the number of the scope's released assessments taken with another model version, which the
trend does not mix (a comparison across versions is deferred). The query `modelVersion` asks for another version; the
default is the version of the latest released assessment. A scope without a released assessment answers `"points": []`
with `modelVersion` `null`. The trend of several scopes belongs to the lens *Reports* (<<_samm_cross_scope_trend>>).

=== The Comparison and `MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER`

`GET /api/v1/samm/payments/compare?from=2026-q2&to=2026-q4` for two assessments taken with different versions of the model
answers `422`

[source,json]
----
{
  "outcome": "MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER",
  "from": { "assessment": "2026-q2", "modelVersion": "v2.1.0" },
  "to": { "assessment": "2026-q4", "modelVersion": "v2.2.0" }
}
----

and nothing is compared. The web app shows a notice that names both versions and offers no comparison; the scores of each
assessment stay readable on the page of that assessment. A missing `from` or `to` is `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/from` or `/to` and the code
`REQUIRED`; an assessment that is none of the scope's is `404 NOT_FOUND`. An open assessment may be compared (its scores
are those of the answers as saved), the two may be in any state.

[#_reports_requests]
== Reports Requests

The requests of the lens *Reports* (xref:../use-cases/04-samm.adoc#UC-SAMM-5[UC-SAMM-5], xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc#UC-TM-4[UC-TM-4],
xref:../use-cases/07-isms.adoc#UC-ISMS-3[UC-ISMS-3]). The rows are in <<_requests>>, *Reports* and *ISMS*; this section
fixes their bodies and the addresses of the reports. Every body is PROVISIONAL unless a row says otherwise; the decisions
are in <<_decided_reports>>. The values are those of the mock: the scopes of <<_samm_requests>>, the services of the
catalog (<<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>) and today, 2026-11-26. A report only reads: it has no draft, no save and no
event of its own, and every figure is computed by the server from the index, so that the web app only draws it.

[#_reports_addresses]
=== The Addresses

The lens *Reports* had `/reports` and the generic `/reports/{report}`. The step adds four addresses, each before
`reports.report` in `canonical-addresses.json`, and gives each its allowed query (<<_allowed_queries>>):

[cols="2,3,2,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Query |Shows

|`reports`
|`/reports`
|none
|The dashboard (`DashboardPage`): one card per report the user may read, each with its figures from
`GET /api/v1/reports/summary` and a link to the report

|`reports.samm-trend`
|`/reports/samm-trend`
|`scope`
|The scores of every scope over time: the overall score of each scope as a line, and a table of the points. With `scope`
the page shows the five business functions of that scope as lines

|`reports.samm-compare`
|`/reports/samm-compare`
|`scope`, `from`, `to`
|Two assessments of one scope side by side, per business function, practice and stream (a dumbbell for each); a notice
for `MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER`. Without `from` and `to` the page offers the pairs the scope has

|`reports.threat-evaluation`
|`/reports/threat-evaluation`
|none
|The table of the threat models per service: open and untreated threats, days since the last review, the review due
date; below it the services without a model

|`reports.risk-register`
|`/reports/risk-register`
|`as-of`
|The risk register as of a tag or a date, released entries only; without `as-of` as of now
|===

The values of the names, each matched against the whole value (`queries` of the file): `scope`, `from` and `to` are
address segments (`[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,127}`); `as-of` is `[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._/-]{0,127}`, which holds a calendar
date (`2026-03-01`) and a tag name (`audit-2026-03`, `risks/R-012/v2`) alike. The pattern checks the form only: a value that
is no valid date and no known tag is answered by the server (`422`, see below), so that the page can say so. A name the
address does not admit is ignored, one that appears twice or does not match makes the address not found (the rules of
<<_allowed_queries>>). `/reports/{report}` stays for the address of every other report: it is the not-found view.
`/isms/risks/{entry}` (`isms.risk`) gains the allowed query `at`, so that an entry of the as-of list opens as it was then
(`/isms/risks/r-004?at=v1`); the page of the entry is built with the ISMS lens.

The reports have no address of their own for a chart, a table or a filter: they are parts of the page. The chart and the
table of a report state the same figures.

[#_reports_summary]
=== The Dashboard Summary

`GET /api/v1/reports/summary` for a user who may read `samm-assessments`, `threat-models` and `isms-register`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "today": "2026-11-26",
  "samm": {
    "scopes": 4, "withoutRelease": 1, "open": 2,
    "latest": [
      { "scope": "payments", "name": "Payments", "assessment": "2026-q2", "date": "2026-06-16",
        "overall": 1.3583333333333332, "change": 0.3999999999999998 },
      { "scope": "shop", "name": "Web shop", "assessment": "2026-q3", "date": "2026-09-22",
        "overall": 1.2333333333333334, "change": null },
      { "scope": "organisation", "name": "Organisation", "assessment": "2026-h1", "date": "2026-05-04",
        "overall": 1.2083333333333335, "change": null }
    ]
  },
  "threatModels": {
    "services": 8, "withModel": 6, "withoutModel": 2, "openThreats": 13, "untreatedThreats": 10,
    "overdue": 2, "dueSoon": 1
  },
  "risks": {
    "entries": 7, "byRating": { "critical": 1, "high": 3, "medium": 2, "low": 1 },
    "dueSoon": 1, "overdue": 1, "latestRelease": "2026-11-12T10:41:00Z"
  }
}
----

* A section is present exactly when the user holds `read` on its repository (`samm` on `samm-assessments`,
  `threatModels` on `threat-models`, `risks` on `isms-register`); a user with none of the three gets `{"today"}` and the
  empty state of a session without rights is shown by the shell before. The request never answers `FORBIDDEN`.
* `samm.latest` holds, for every scope that has a released assessment, the newest one in the order of the scope list;
  `change` is its `overall` minus the `overall` of the released assessment before it of the same model version, `null`
  when there is none. `withoutRelease` counts the scopes that have no released assessment, `open` those that have an
  assessment in `draft` or `in-review`.
* `threatModels` counts the services of the catalog that are not retired (`services`), those with at least one threat
  model (`withModel`) and without one (`withoutModel`), the open and the untreated threats of all models
  (<<_threat_evaluation>>) and the models whose review is `overdue` or `due-soon`.
* `risks` counts the released entries as of now (<<_risk_register_as_of>>), by `rating`, those whose `reviewDue` lies
  within 30 days (`dueSoon`) or before today (`overdue`), and the time of the newest release.
* The figures are those of the three reports, so a card and its report never disagree; the dashboard makes this one
  request, and each report its own.

[#_samm_cross_scope_trend]
=== The Trend of Every Scope

`GET /api/v1/reports/samm-trend` (shortened; every point holds the five business functions, the practices are left to
`GET /api/v1/samm/{scope}/trend`):

[source,json]
----
{
  "scopes": [
    { "scope": "payments", "title": "Payments", "subject": { "kind": "team", "id": "payments", "name": "Payments" },
      "modelVersion": "v2.2.0", "omitted": 0,
      "points": [
        { "assessment": "2025-q4", "document": "payments-2025-q4", "title": "Payments 2025 Q4", "date": "2025-12-08",
          "overall": 0.9583333333333334,
          "functions": { "governance": 0.9583333333333334, "design": 1.0, "implementation": 1.25,
                         "verification": 0.8333333333333334, "operations": 0.75 } },
        { "assessment": "2026-q2", "document": "payments-2026-q2", "title": "Payments 2026 Q2", "date": "2026-06-16",
          "overall": 1.3583333333333332,
          "functions": { "governance": 1.4166666666666667, "design": 1.6666666666666667,
                         "implementation": 1.5416666666666667, "verification": 0.9583333333333334,
                         "operations": 1.2083333333333333 } }
      ] },
    { "scope": "identity", "title": "Identity Platform",
      "subject": { "kind": "team", "id": "identity", "name": "Identity Platform" },
      "modelVersion": null, "omitted": 0, "points": [] },
    { "scope": "shop", "title": "Web shop", "subject": { "kind": "service", "id": "shop", "name": "Web shop" },
      "modelVersion": "v2.2.0", "omitted": 0, "points": [ "…" ] },
    { "scope": "organisation", "title": "Organisation",
      "subject": { "kind": "organisation", "name": "Organisation" },
      "modelVersion": "v2.2.0", "omitted": 0, "points": [ "…" ] }
  ]
}
----

* The scopes are in the order of `GET /api/v1/samm/scopes`; the points of a scope are those of
  `GET /api/v1/samm/{scope}/trend` without the member `practices`: the released assessments of the scope's
  `modelVersion`, oldest first by `date`, scores unrounded (AUN-SAMM-5). `modelVersion` is the version of the scope's
  latest released assessment, `omitted` the number of its released assessments of other versions, as in the request of
  one scope. A scope without a released assessment is listed with `modelVersion` `null` and `"points": []`, so that the
  table names it.
* Scopes may have different `modelVersion`s. The report does not mix versions inside a scope (as before), but it does draw
  scopes of different versions on one scale (0 to 3); the page then says which versions occur and that a score of
  another version is read with that caution. A comparison across versions stays deferred.
* The request has no query. The query `scope` of the address only chooses which scope the page shows by business function;
  the answer is the same.

[#_samm_compare_page]
=== The Comparison Page

The page `/reports/samm-compare?scope=payments&from=2025-q4&to=2026-q2` makes no request of its own beyond the scope list
(`GET /api/v1/samm/scopes`), the scope (`GET /api/v1/samm/scopes/payments`, whose `assessments` offer the pairs) and
`GET /api/v1/samm/payments/compare?from=2025-q4&to=2026-q2`. The `200` of the comparison, which the register had left as
"shape PROVISIONAL" (shortened to one practice; the figures are those of the mock):

[source,json]
----
{
  "from": "2025-q4", "to": "2026-q2", "modelVersion": "v2.2.0",
  "overall": { "from": 0.9583333333333334, "to": 1.3583333333333332, "delta": 0.3999999999999998 },
  "functions": [
    { "id": "governance", "from": 0.9583333333333334, "to": 1.4166666666666667, "delta": 0.45833333333333337,
      "practices": [
        { "id": "sm", "from": 1.125, "to": 1.25, "delta": 0.125,
          "streams": [
            { "letter": "A", "from": 1.0, "to": 1.5, "delta": 0.5 },
            { "letter": "B", "from": 1.25, "to": 1.0, "delta": -0.25 } ] },
        "…" ] },
    "…" ]
}
----

`delta` is `to` minus `from`, unrounded; the two assessments may be given in either order (the delta then changes its
sign) and may be in any state (an open one is read as saved). `MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER`, `404` and the `422` for a missing
`from` or `to` are those of <<_samm_requests>>. The page shows the notice of `MODEL_VERSIONS_DIFFER` with both versions
and no comparison; the scores of each assessment are read on the page of that assessment (the trend lists only the assessments of the
model version of the scope, so one of the two may have no row there). A `scope` that does not exist
is the not-found view of the page, `from` or `to` that is none of the scope's assessments the notice of the `404`.

[#_threat_evaluation]
=== The Threat Evaluation

`GET /api/v1/reports/threat-evaluation` (shortened to three models):

[source,json]
----
{
  "today": "2026-11-26",
  "models": [
    { "service": { "id": "customer-portal", "name": "Customer Portal" }, "protectionNeed": "very-high",
      "reviewInterval": "P6M", "model": "portal", "address": "/threat-models/customer-portal/portal",
      "state": "released", "version": 2, "lastReview": "2025-12-02", "daysSinceReview": 359, "reviewDue": "2026-06-02",
      "dueInDays": -177, "status": "overdue",
      "threats": { "total": 6, "open": 2, "untreated": 2, "transferred": 0 } },
    { "service": { "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API" }, "protectionNeed": "very-high",
      "reviewInterval": "P6M", "model": "checkout-flow", "address": "/threat-models/payment-api/checkout-flow",
      "state": "released", "version": 3, "lastReview": "2026-06-01", "daysSinceReview": 178, "reviewDue": "2026-12-01",
      "dueInDays": 5, "status": "due-soon",
      "threats": { "total": 9, "open": 3, "untreated": 2, "transferred": 1 } },
    { "service": { "id": "notification-service", "name": "Notification service" }, "protectionNeed": "normal",
      "reviewInterval": "P24M", "model": "notification-flows", "address": "/threat-models/notification-service/notification-flows",
      "state": "draft", "version": null, "lastReview": null, "daysSinceReview": null, "reviewDue": null,
      "dueInDays": null, "status": "unreleased",
      "threats": { "total": 3, "open": 3, "untreated": 3, "transferred": 0 } }
  ],
  "withoutModel": [
    { "service": { "id": "report-scheduler", "name": "Report scheduler" }, "lifecycle": "active",
      "owner": { "kind": "team", "id": "security-office", "name": "Security Office" }, "protectionNeed": "normal",
      "address": "/threat-models/report-scheduler" }
  ]
}
----

* *One row per threat model.* AUN-TM-4 names the service, the address names the model, so a service with two models has
  two rows. The rows are ordered by `reviewDue` (a model without one last), then by the name of the service. The web app
  sorts further on its own.
* `protectionNeed` is the highest of the three effective protection needs of the service (AUN-DIR-5, `normal`, `high` or
  `very-high`), `reviewInterval` the period AUN-TM-3 selects from it (`P6M`, `P12M`, `P24M`). `lastReview` is the
  `last-review` of the last released version; `reviewDue` is `lastReview` plus the interval; `daysSinceReview` and
  `dueInDays` are counted to `today` (the server's UTC date); `dueInDays` is negative when the review is overdue.
* `status`: `overdue` (`dueInDays` below 0), `due-soon` (0 to 30), `ok` (above 30) and `unreleased` for a model that was
  never released, which has no review and no due date (UC-TM-2 starts at a released model). `state` and `version` are the
  workflow state and the number of the last release (`null` before the first).
* `threats` counts the threats of the model as it is saved: `open` those with the status `open`, `untreated` the open ones
  without a countermeasure that are not transferred, `transferred` those that carry a reference into the risk register.
  `untreated` is never more than `open`. The status of a threat (`open`, `mitigated`, `accepted`) and the members
  that carry a countermeasure and a transfer are those of the data shape of <<_threat_model_data>>.
* `withoutModel` lists the services of the catalog that are not retired and have no threat model, a planned one too, the
  highest `protectionNeed` first and then by name; its `address` is the service's page in the lens *Threat models* (`/threat-models/<service>`), from which a model
  is created (the next step). `owner` is the owning team of the service.
* An installation without services answers both lists empty.

[#_risk_register_as_of]
=== The Risk Register as of a Date

`GET /api/v1/isms/risks?as-of=2026-03-01` (AUN-ISMS-3), shortened to two entries:

[source,json]
----
{
  "asOf": { "requested": "2026-03-01", "date": "2026-03-01", "tag": null, "commit": "3f9a1c2", "at": "2026-02-27T16:20:00Z" },
  "tags": [
    { "tag": "management-review-2026-06", "commit": "b81d4e6", "at": "2026-06-18T13:05:00Z" },
    { "tag": "audit-2026-03", "commit": "3f9a1c2", "at": "2026-02-27T16:20:00Z" }
  ],
  "risks": [
    { "id": "R-004", "entry": "r-004", "title": "Build agents run unpatched images", "rating": "medium",
      "treatment": "mitigate", "owner": { "kind": "team", "id": "security-office", "name": "Security Office" },
      "version": 1, "releasedAt": "2025-11-06T14:02:00Z", "reviewDue": "2026-05-06", "reference": null,
      "address": "/isms/risks/r-004?at=v1" },
    { "id": "R-007", "entry": "r-007", "title": "Signing keys held by a single administrator", "rating": "low",
      "treatment": "accept", "owner": { "kind": "team", "id": "security-office", "name": "Security Office" },
      "version": 1, "releasedAt": "2026-02-18T11:20:00Z", "reviewDue": "2026-11-18", "reference": null,
      "address": "/isms/risks/r-007?at=v1" }
  ]
}
----

* `as-of` is a tag or a calendar date (UTC); without it the register is read as of now (`requested` `null`, `date` today).
  A tag selects the tagged commit, a date the last commit on `main` at or before the end of that day (AUN-ISMS-3). Only a
  tag whose commit is on `main` counts: the register reads released entries only, and a tag that points into the
  collection branch (`refs/heads/risks`) is not a tag of the register (`UNKNOWN_TAG`). The answer never changes the
  current index.
* `asOf` names what was read: `requested` (the parameter as given, or `null`), `date` (the day the register is as of, UTC: the requested
  date, the day of the tagged commit for a tag, today without `as-of`; the commit's own day is that of `at`), `tag` (the tag when one was asked for), `commit` (seven hexadecimal digits) and `at` (the commit's time). A date
  before the first commit of the register has `commit` `null`, `at` `null` and `"risks": []`.
* `tags` are the newest tags of `main` in `isms-register`, newest first, at most 20, for the picker of the page; the
  application creates none (audit tags that mark a reporting date are deferred,
  xref:../concepts/category-isms.adoc#_open_questions[ISMS, Open Questions]), so the list may be empty.
* `risks` are the released entries at that commit, ordered by the running number, each as it was then: `id` (`R-<n>`),
  `entry` (the address segment, in lower case), `title`, `rating` (`low`, `medium`, `high`, `critical`), `treatment`
  (`mitigate`, `accept`, `transfer`, `avoid`), `owner` (a team), `version` (the number of the release shown) and
  `releasedAt`, `reviewDue` (a date or `null`) and `reference`, the origin of the risk as `{"ref", "title", "address"}` (a
  SAMM practice such as `samm-assessments:assessment/payments-2026-q2#st` or a threat such as
  `threat-models:threat-model/payment-api-checkout-flow#T-07`, the reference forms of AUN-DP-2; `null` for none). `address` is the entry's page; with `as-of` it always carries `?at=v<n>` of the release shown, so that the entry opens as
  it was then even when a newer release exists by the time the link is followed (UC-ISMS-3); the read as of now carries
  none. A draft or an entry
  that was never released is not listed.
* The list is not paged: the register is designed for a few thousand entries
  (xref:../concepts/category-isms.adoc[ISMS], the design assumption of the index).
* Refusals: `422 VALIDATION` with the pointer `/as-of` (a query parameter read as one object, see the common refusals)
  and `PATTERN` for a value that is neither a calendar date nor a tag name (`2026-13-45`), `FUTURE` for a date after
  today, `UNKNOWN_TAG` for a name that is no tag of the register. `503 GIT_UNAVAILABLE` for a commit whose index is not
  built and cannot be built while the Git server is down; the register as of now is answered from the index.
* The rights: `read` on `isms-register`. The owner is a team, never a person: the register names roles and teams, not
  people (xref:data-protection.adoc[Data Protection]).

[#_threat_model_requests]
== Threat Model Requests

The requests of the use cases UC-TM-1, UC-TM-2 and UC-TM-4 (xref:../use-cases/05-threat-models.adoc[Use Cases: Threat Models]);
the transfer of a threat into the risk register (UC-TM-3) is made with the ISMS (<<_isms_origin>>) and writes the member of a
threat that <<_threat_model_data>> reserves for it, which these requests only read. The rows are in <<_requests>>, *Threat models*; this section fixes their bodies,
the data shape of a threat model and the addresses of the new views. Every body is PROVISIONAL unless a row says otherwise;
the decisions are in <<_decided_threat_models>>. The values are those of the mock: the services of the catalog
(<<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>), the six models of the evaluation (<<_threat_evaluation>>) and today, 2026-11-26. The
threat model is a document of the repository `threat-models` and uses the document lifecycle of <<_the_document_lifecycle>>:
states `draft`, `in-review`, `released`, the transitions `threat-model.submit` (`draft` to `in-review`, right `edit`, no
step-up) and `threat-model.release` (`in-review` to `released`, right `approve`, step-up; the tag is `models/<id>/v<n>`), a
save in every state, and the next version after a release (a threat model is released more than once).

[#_threat_model_addresses]
=== The Addresses

The lens *Threat models* had `/threat-models`, `/threat-models/{service}`, `/threat-models/{service}/{model}` and
`/threat-models/{service}/{model}/release`. The step adds one address and one allowed query (<<_allowed_queries>>):

[cols="2,3,2,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Query |Shows

|`threat-models`
|`/threat-models`
|none
|The first column: the evaluation of every service (`GET /api/v1/reports/threat-evaluation`, the table of the report), the
services without a model below it

|`threat-models.service`
|`/threat-models/{service}`
|none
|The models of one service and the way to a new one (`GET /api/v1/threat-models/{service}`): the recipes to choose from and
the recipes the person has started

|`threat-models.new`
|`/threat-models/{service}/new`
|`recipe`
|The recipe wizard (a sheet of the lens, <<_decided_threat_models>> point 27) of a new threat model of the service; `recipe` names the recipe, without it the page offers the
recipes. Nothing is stored before the first step is left (the recipe draft), no model before the last. The route comes before
`threat-models.model` in the file

|`threat-models.model`
|`/threat-models/{service}/{model}`
|`at`
|The model sheet: the evaluation of the model, the diagram, the threat table with status and countermeasures, the data of the
model, the workflow acts, the history; with `at` the model as it was at that revision, read-only

|`threat-models.release`
|`/threat-models/{service}/{model}/release`
|none
|The review and release of the model (`ReleaseFlow`)
|===

`recipe` is a recipe id, `[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}`; `at` is the pattern of the other addresses (`v<n>` or a commit id). The
segment `new` is reserved as `{model}`: the server never derives it as an address segment (a title that yields it is treated
as a collision and gets a number appended), and `/threat-models/{service}/new` is the wizard, never a model. `/threat-models/new`
reads as the page of a service called `new`, which the catalog does not prevent and the register does not reserve. The
`recipe` name is refused on every other address (the rules of <<_allowed_queries>>); `at` is refused on the wizard.

[#_threat_model_data]
=== The Data Shape of a Threat Model

A threat model is a document of the document type `threat-model` in the repository `threat-models`, in the collection `models`
(AUN-TM-1). Its schema declares one split (`gdr-split`, https://github.com/cuioss/git-document-repository/blob/main/doc/specification/git-forms.adoc#GDR-FORMS-3[GDR-FORMS-3]), so the document
has two files and the document read two parts:

[cols="1,2,3"]
|===
|Part |File |Content

|`data`
|`models/<ulid>/document.yaml`
|`service` (a reference `service:<id>`), `owner` (a reference `role:<id>`), `last-review` (a date) and, when the model was
started from a recipe, `recipe` (the recipe's id, not editable). The workflow state is the document's, not a field

|`model`
|`models/<ulid>/model.yaml`
|The threat model as an Open Threat Model file, the subset below
|===

The format is Open Threat Model (OTM), serialised as YAML, which OTM allows (https://github.com/iriusrisk/OpenThreatModel[OTM on GitHub]; the
JSON Schema `otm_schema.json` and the example `EXAMPLE.yaml` at commit `c88c5a7`, the specification says "Current schema
version: 0.2.0"). The application reads and writes the members below and keeps every other member unchanged (AUN-TM-2). A
diagram is data: the `components`, `dataflows` and `trustZones`; the image is generated (<<_threat_model_read>>).

[source,yaml]
----
# models/01JZ…/document.yaml
service: service:payment-api
owner: role:service-owner
last-review: 2026-06-01
recipe: stride

# models/01JZ…/model.yaml  (shortened)
otmVersion: 0.2.0
project:
  id: checkout-flow
  name: Payment API, checkout flow
  description: The path of a payment from the checkout page to the payment provider.
trustZones:
  - id: internet
    name: Internet
    risk: { trustRating: 20 }
  - id: payments-zone
    name: Payments zone
    risk: { trustRating: 90 }
components:
  - id: browser
    name: Customer browser
    type: external-entity
    parent: { trustZone: internet }
  - id: payment-api
    name: Payment API
    type: process
    parent: { trustZone: payments-zone }
dataflows:
  - id: authorise
    name: Authorise a payment
    source: browser
    destination: payment-api
threats:
  - id: T-07
    name: Replay of a payment authorisation
    description: A recorded authorisation is sent again and charges the customer twice.
    categories: [Spoofing, Tampering]
    risk: { likelihood: 50, impact: 100 }
    attributes:
      status: accepted
      transferred: isms-register:risk/R-009
  - id: T-03
    name: Card data in the application log
    categories: [Information Disclosure]
    risk: { likelihood: 50, impact: 75 }
    attributes:
      status: open
mitigations:
  - id: M-03
    name: Mask card numbers before logging
    description: The logging filter keeps the last four digits.
    riskReduction: 75
    attributes:
      threat: T-03
      status: planned
      issues: [PAY-1423]
----

The schema of the document type, `GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/schema`, `kinds[0]` (the form kit's vocabulary,
xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4]; the bounds are PROVISIONAL, <<_decided_threat_models>>, point 11):

[source,json]
----
{
  "category": "threat-models",
  "kinds": [
    {
      "id": "threat-model",
      "repository": "threat-models",
      "schema": {
        "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["service", "owner", "last-review", "model"],
        "properties": {
          "service":     { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "service" },
          "owner":       { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "role" },
          "last-review": { "type": "string", "format": "date" },
          "recipe":      { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$", "readOnly": true },
          "model": {
            "type": "object",
            "gdr-split": true,
            "required": ["otmVersion", "project"],
            "properties": {
              "otmVersion": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$", "readOnly": true },
              "project": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["id", "name"],
                "properties": {
                  "id":          { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,127}$", "readOnly": true },
                  "name":        { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                  "description": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 2000, "x-free-text": true }
                }
              },
              "trustZones": {
                "type": "array", "maxItems": 20,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object", "required": ["id", "name", "risk"],
                  "properties": {
                    "id":   { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$" },
                    "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                    "risk": { "type": "object", "required": ["trustRating"],
                              "properties": { "trustRating": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100 } } }
                  }
                }
              },
              "components": {
                "type": "array", "maxItems": 200,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object", "required": ["id", "name", "type", "parent"],
                  "properties": {
                    "id":          { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$" },
                    "name":        { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                    "type":        { "type": "string", "enum": ["external-entity", "process", "data-store"] },
                    "description": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 2000, "x-free-text": true },
                    "parent":      { "type": "object", "required": ["trustZone"],
                                     "properties": { "trustZone": { "type": "string" } } }
                  }
                }
              },
              "dataflows": {
                "type": "array", "maxItems": 400,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object", "required": ["id", "name", "source", "destination"],
                  "properties": {
                    "id":            { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$" },
                    "name":          { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                    "source":        { "type": "string" },
                    "destination":   { "type": "string" },
                    "bidirectional": { "type": "boolean" }
                  }
                }
              },
              "threats": {
                "type": "array", "maxItems": 200,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object", "required": ["id", "name", "risk", "attributes"],
                  "properties": {
                    "id":          { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$" },
                    "name":        { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                    "description": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 2000, "x-free-text": true },
                    "categories":  { "type": "array", "uniqueItems": true,
                                     "items": { "type": "string", "enum": ["Spoofing", "Tampering", "Repudiation",
                                       "Information Disclosure", "Denial of Service", "Elevation of Privilege"] } },
                    "risk": { "type": "object", "required": ["likelihood", "impact"],
                              "properties": { "likelihood": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100 },
                                              "impact":     { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100 } } },
                    "attributes": {
                      "type": "object", "required": ["status"],
                      "properties": {
                        "status":      { "type": "string", "enum": ["open", "mitigated", "accepted"] },
                        "transferred": { "type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "readOnly": true }
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              },
              "mitigations": {
                "type": "array", "maxItems": 400,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object", "required": ["id", "name", "riskReduction", "attributes"],
                  "properties": {
                    "id":            { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$" },
                    "name":          { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
                    "description":   { "type": "string", "maxLength": 2000, "x-free-text": true },
                    "riskReduction": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100 },
                    "attributes": {
                      "type": "object", "required": ["threat"],
                      "properties": {
                        "threat": { "type": "string" },
                        "status": { "type": "string", "enum": ["planned", "implemented"] },
                        "issues": { "type": "array", "maxItems": 20, "uniqueItems": true,
                                    "items": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 64 } }
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
----

What the shape says (the rules the server enforces, and the mock follows):

* *A threat has one status,* `attributes.status`: `open` (not yet decided), `mitigated` (countermeasures treat it) or
  `accepted` (the organisation lives with it). OTM binds a state and the mitigations to the instance of a threat on a
  component or a data flow; Audit Notary evaluates a threat once, so it keeps both on the threat and the countermeasure
