  (<<_decided_threat_models>>, point 5).
* *A countermeasure belongs to one threat:* a mitigation names it in `attributes.threat`, which must be the `id` of a threat of
  the model (`REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND`, pointer `/mitigations/<n>/attributes/threat`). Its `issues` are the ids of the issues that
  carry out the work, entered by a person or an agent; the application connects to no issue tracker (AUN-TM-4).
* *The transfer* is the member `attributes.transferred` of a threat, the reference `isms-register:risk/<id>` of the risk it
  became (AUN-TM-4 names the reverse reference, `threat-models:threat-model/<ulid>#<threat-id>`, on the risk). The transfer
  is made with the ISMS (UC-TM-3, <<_isms_origin>>), which writes the member when the risk is saved for the first time: this
  step reads the member and never writes it. The schema marks it `readOnly`, so the form does
  not offer it, and a save keeps the stored `transferred` of every threat of the same `id` whatever the sent list carries
  (PROVISIONAL, <<_decided_threat_models>>, point 18).
* *The ids* of the file are chosen by the application when a person adds a row: `T-<nn>` for a threat, `M-<nn>` for a
  mitigation (the next free number, two digits at least); the reference of AUN-TM-4 ends with the threat id
  (`…#T-07`). The ids of components, data flows and trust zones are lower-case words.
* *The counts of the evaluation* are read from the file as it is saved (<<_threat_evaluation>>): `total` is the number of
  threats, `open`, `mitigated` and `accepted` those of that status, `untreated` the open ones that no mitigation names and
  that carry no `transferred`, `transferred` those that carry it. `total` = `open` + `mitigated` + `accepted`.
* *The review date and the interval:* `last-review` is a field of the reviewed revision (UC-TM-2). The interval comes from the
  highest of the three effective protection needs of the service (AUN-DIR-5) by the table `very-high` `P6M`, `high` `P12M`,
  `normal` `P24M`, which the kind carries as data and the requests below serve as `reviewIntervals`; the due date is
  the `last-review` of the last released version plus the interval.

[#_threat_model_service]
=== A Service and Its Models

`GET /api/v1/threat-models/payment-api` (the rows of `models` are those of the evaluation without `service` and `protectionNeed`,
with the members `document`, `title`, `requested` and `updatedAt`):

[source,json]
----
{
  "today": "2026-11-26",
  "service": { "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API", "lifecycle": "active", "retired": false,
               "owner": { "kind": "team", "id": "payments", "name": "Payments" },
               "protectionNeed": "very-high", "reviewInterval": "P6M" },
  "reviewIntervals": { "very-high": "P6M", "high": "P12M", "normal": "P24M" },
  "models": [
    { "model": "checkout-flow", "document": "payment-api-checkout-flow", "title": "Payment API, checkout flow",
      "address": "/threat-models/payment-api/checkout-flow", "state": "released", "version": 3,
      "lastReview": "2026-06-01", "daysSinceReview": 178, "reviewDue": "2026-12-01", "dueInDays": 5, "status": "due-soon",
      "threats": { "total": 9, "open": 3, "untreated": 2, "transferred": 1 },
      "requested": false, "updatedAt": "2026-06-02T09:30:00Z" }
  ],
  "drafts": [ { "recipe": "stride", "step": 2, "at": "2026-11-25T16:10:00Z" } ]
}
----

`models` is ordered as the evaluation orders its rows. `drafts` are the person's own unfinished recipes for this service
(<<_threat_model_recipe_draft>>); a service without a model answers `"models": []`. A retired service
(`"retired": true`) keeps its models readable and gets no new one (`409 SERVICE_RETIRED` on the create). The members of a
row are computed as in <<_threat_evaluation>>; a model that was never released has the `null`s of its `unreleased` row.

[#_threat_model_read]
=== A Threat Model

`GET /api/v1/threat-models/payment-api/checkout-flow` (the files are shortened to the members the examples above show):

[source,json]
----
{
  "repository": "threat-models", "id": "payment-api-checkout-flow", "category": "threat-models", "kind": "threat-model",
  "title": "Payment API, checkout flow", "state": "released", "version": 3, "frozen": false,
  "service": { "id": "payment-api", "name": "Payment API" }, "model": "checkout-flow",
  "address": "/threat-models/payment-api/checkout-flow",
  "parts": {
    "data": { "blob": "4d2a7e1", "savedAt": "2026-06-01T09:40:00Z",
              "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example", "role": "Developer" },
              "value": { "service": "service:payment-api", "owner": "role:service-owner", "last-review": "2026-06-01",
                         "recipe": "stride" } },
    "model": { "blob": "9c1b3f8", "savedAt": "2026-06-01T09:40:00Z",
               "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example", "role": "Developer" },
               "value": { "otmVersion": "0.2.0", "project": { "id": "checkout-flow", "name": "Payment API, checkout flow" },
                          "threats": [ "…" ], "mitigations": [ "…" ] } }
  },
  "draft": null,
  "history": [ "…" ],
  "release": { "tag": "models/payment-api-checkout-flow/v3", "at": "2026-06-02T09:30:00Z",
               "by": { "pseudonym": "p-7d2e58", "name": "Noor Haddad" } },
  "releaseRequest": null,
  "transitions": [],
  "review": { "today": "2026-11-26", "protectionNeed": "very-high", "reviewInterval": "P6M",
              "reviewIntervals": { "very-high": "P6M", "high": "P12M", "normal": "P24M" },
              "lastReview": "2026-06-01", "daysSinceReview": 178, "reviewDue": "2026-12-01", "dueInDays": 5,
              "status": "due-soon" },
  "threats": { "total": 9, "open": 3, "untreated": 2, "transferred": 1 },
  "diagram": { "mediaType": "image/svg+xml", "width": 640, "height": 360,
               "dataUrl": "data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciIC4uLg==" }
}
----

* The members of the generic document read keep their meaning (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>); `transitions` of a model
  in `draft` is `threat-model.submit`, of one in `in-review` `threat-model.release` with `"release": true`, of a released
  one empty (a save reopens it). A revision (`at`) has `frozen: true`, no draft and no transitions, and its `review`,
  `threats` and `diagram` are those of that revision.
* `review` is the review of the last released version, as in the evaluation row (`lastReview`, `reviewDue` and `status` are
  `null`, `null` and `unreleased` before the first release); `lastReview` is not the `last-review` of the working files, which
  a review changes before the release. `reviewIntervals` lets the sheet show the due date of a review date that is typed
  and not yet released.
* `threats` counts the saved working state of the model, as the evaluation does; `review` is that of the last release.
* `diagram` is generated by the server from `trustZones`, `components` and `dataflows` and is never stored: a scalable
  vector image as a data URL, `null` when the model has no component. The web app shows it as an image with a text
  alternative made from the names of the components. No script of the image runs (an image element), and the
  data URL is not an address of the app.
* The same body is the answer of `GET /api/v1/documents/threat-models/{id}` and, with `at`, of the revision; a view that
  has the document id reads either.

[#_threat_model_save]
=== Saving a Model

The save and the draft of a threat model are the generic requests with the parts `data` and `model` in one body. `fields`
maps a JSON Pointer into the value of the part to its new value, as everywhere:

[source,json]
----
{ "parts": {
    "data":  { "blob": "4d2a7e1", "fields": { "/last-review": "2026-11-26" } },
    "model": { "blob": "9c1b3f8", "fields": {
        "/threats": [ { "id": "T-03", "name": "Card data in the application log", "categories": ["Information Disclosure"],
                        "risk": { "likelihood": 50, "impact": 75 }, "attributes": { "status": "mitigated" } } ],
        "/mitigations": [ { "id": "M-03", "name": "Mask card numbers before logging", "riskReduction": 75,
                            "attributes": { "threat": "T-03", "status": "implemented", "issues": ["PAY-1423"] } } ] } } } }
----

* *A list is sent as a whole:* `/threats`, `/mitigations`, `/components`, `/dataflows` and `/trustZones` carry the whole list,
  and `/project` the whole object; the form kit's rule (a list or a section as a whole) is kept and a pointer into a list
  (`/threats/3/…`) is not used. A threat and its countermeasures are two lists, so the editor of a countermeasure sends both
  in one save and the file never holds a countermeasure of a threat that is gone.
* *A conflict is per list:* `{"part": "model", "blob", "fields": [{"pointer": "/threats", "own": […], "other": […], "by"}]}`;
  the user decides per list. A finer conflict (per threat) would need an element key in the pointer (<<_decided_threat_models>>,
  point 9).
* *The refusals* are those of the generic save with the pointer into the value of the part, for example
  `{"part": "model", "pointer": "/mitigations/1/attributes/threat", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND"}`,
  `{"part": "model", "pointer": "/threats/0/risk/impact", "code": "MAXIMUM"}`,
  `{"part": "data", "pointer": "/owner", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND"}`.
* *The review is an act of the sheet:* the sheet prefills `/last-review` with `review.today` when the person starts the review
  of a released model (the act, not every change, sets the date: a corrected typo is no review), and the first save after the
  release starts the next version in `draft` (UC-TM-2).

[#_threat_model_create]
=== A New Model

`POST /api/v1/threat-models/report-scheduler`, the wizard's last request (the fields are those the steps filled; the example
is shortened):

[source,json]
----
{ "recipe": "quick-start",
  "parts": {
    "data":  { "fields": { "/owner": "role:service-owner", "/last-review": "2026-11-26" } },
    "model": { "fields": {
        "/project": { "name": "Scheduler overview", "description": "How a report is scheduled and sent." },
        "/trustZones": [ { "id": "internal", "name": "Internal", "risk": { "trustRating": 80 } } ],
        "/components": [ { "id": "scheduler", "name": "Scheduler", "type": "process", "parent": { "trustZone": "internal" } } ],
        "/threats": [] } } } }
----

answers `201`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "CREATED", "model": "scheduler-overview", "document": "report-scheduler-scheduler-overview",
  "address": "/threat-models/report-scheduler/scheduler-overview", "state": "draft", "version": 1,
  "at": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z",
  "parts": { "data":  { "blob": "a41f09c", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z", "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } },
             "model": { "blob": "e0b73d2", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z", "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } } } }
----

`model` is the address segment the server derives from `/project/name` (lower case, a number appended on a collision; `new`
counts as a collision), written into `project.id`; `document` is the document id (a ULID in the application; the mock uses the
service and the segment, `<service>-<model>`). The server writes `service`, `recipe` and `otmVersion` (the version of the
OTM schema it writes) itself; the web app never sends them. Only `/project/name` is mandatory in a first save (`REQUIRED`);
the other mandatory fields of the schema are asked for by the transition, as everywhere (`MANDATORY_FIELDS_MISSING`).
`/last-review` is prefilled with today by the wizard's last page: the model is the review of its own first version. The event
`document-created` (`kind` `threat-model`) reaches the other tabs, the evaluation lists the model as `unreleased`.

[#_threat_model_recipes]
=== The Recipes

AUN-TM-5 fixes a recipe as `categories/threat-models/recipes/<id>.yaml` with `title`, ordered `steps`, each with `title`,
`guidance` (an AsciiDoc fragment) and `fills` (a JSON Pointer into the model file). Provisionally a step also has an `id`,
the recipe a `description`, and every text may be given per language, so that the files read:

[source,yaml]
----
title: { en: "STRIDE, step by step", de: "STRIDE, Schritt für Schritt" }
description: { en: "Every component, every data flow, six kinds of threat.", de: "Jede Komponente, jeder Datenfluss, sechs Arten von Bedrohung." }
steps:
  - id: zones
    title: { en: Trust zones, de: Vertrauenszonen }
    guidance: { en: "Name the areas that trust each other…", de: "Benennen Sie die Bereiche, die einander vertrauen…" }
    fills: /trustZones
----

`GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/recipes?lang=de`:

[source,json]
----
{ "recipes": [
    { "id": "quick-start", "title": "Schnellstart", "description": "Vier Schritte: der Rahmen, die Zonen, die Teile, die Bedrohungen.", "steps": 4 },
    { "id": "stride", "title": "STRIDE, Schritt für Schritt", "description": "Jede Komponente, jeder Datenfluss, sechs Arten von Bedrohung.", "steps": 6 } ] }
----

`GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/recipes/stride?lang=en`:

[source,json]
----
{ "id": "stride", "title": "STRIDE, step by step", "description": "Every component, every data flow, six kinds of threat.",
  "steps": [
    { "id": "project",  "title": "The service",         "guidance": "Describe what the model covers…", "fills": "/project" },
    { "id": "zones",    "title": "Trust zones",         "guidance": "Name the areas that trust each other…", "fills": "/trustZones" },
    { "id": "components", "title": "Components",        "guidance": "List every process, store and outside party…", "fills": "/components" },
    { "id": "dataflows", "title": "Data flows",         "guidance": "Draw every path between two components…", "fills": "/dataflows" },
    { "id": "threats",  "title": "Threats",             "guidance": "For each component and flow ask the six STRIDE questions…", "fills": "/threats" },
    { "id": "mitigations", "title": "Countermeasures",  "guidance": "For each threat you do not accept, name a countermeasure…", "fills": "/mitigations" } ] }
----

* The texts are resolved to the language of `lang` (English when the recipe has no text in it), so the answer holds plain
  strings; the language of the interface is the language of the guidance. The texts are the recipe's data, not catalogue
  messages.
* `fills` names a node of the model file's schema that the form kit can show as a whole: an array of objects (`DataTable`)
  or an object (a section), reached by `properties` and `items` only. The wizard builds the step's form from the schema
  of `GET /api/v1/categories/threat-models/schema` at that node, and a step edits exactly the value at `fills` of the part
  `model`: `fills` is the key of `fields` in a recipe draft and in the create.
* The guidance is rendered with `POST /api/v1/render/asciidoc`, like every AsciiDoc text; the wizard asks once per step.
* The two recipes of the mock are `quick-start` (four steps: `project`, `zones`, `components`, `threats`) and `stride`
  (six steps as above). Which recipes ship is the owner's question (AUN-TM-5, the concept's open questions).

[#_threat_model_recipe_draft]
=== The Recipe Draft

The wizard keeps what a person filled between its steps as a draft of a model that does not exist yet (UC-DOC-3 before
UC-DOC-4): one draft per person, service and recipe, in the application and never in Git.

`PUT /api/v1/threat-models/report-scheduler/drafts/stride`, when the person leaves step 2 (zero-based):

[source,json]
----
{ "step": 2,
  "parts": { "model": { "fields": {
      "/project": { "name": "Scheduler overview", "description": "How a report is scheduled and sent." },
      "/trustZones": [ { "id": "internal", "name": "Internal", "risk": { "trustRating": 80 } } ] } } } }
----

answers `{"outcome": "DRAFT_SAVED", "at": "2026-11-26T09:20:00Z"}`. The draft is replaced as a whole by the body, so the
wizard sends every field filled so far; `step` is the step the person goes on to, and the number of steps for the final page
(owner and review date, then the create). `GET /api/v1/threat-models/report-scheduler/drafts/stride` answers

[source,json]
----
{ "service": "report-scheduler", "recipe": "stride",
  "draft": { "step": 2, "at": "2026-11-26T09:20:00Z",
             "parts": { "model": { "fields": { "/project": { "…": "…" }, "/trustZones": [ "…" ] } } } } }
----

or `"draft": null`. A draft with field errors is refused (`422`, the codes and pointers of a save with the part) and
nothing is written, so the wizard goes to the next step only when the step is valid; the create ends the draft; there is no
request that discards one (a draft ends with the create or by expiry, as every draft of this application).

[#_threat_model_derivation]
=== Where the Figures of the Evaluation Come From

Every figure of <<_threat_evaluation>> and of the dashboard summary is derived from the documents; the mock's files must
agree with each other. For a threat model `m` of a service `s`, today `t`:

* `protectionNeed` is the highest effective protection need of `s` (catalog), `reviewInterval` the interval of the table above;
* `state` is the workflow state of the document and `version` the number of its last release (`null` before the first),
  `lastReview` is the `last-review` of the `data` part at that release, `reviewDue` is `lastReview` plus the interval, `daysSinceReview` is `t`
  minus `lastReview` and `dueInDays` is `reviewDue` minus `t`, `status` is `overdue` below 0, `due-soon` from 0 to 30, `ok`
  above, `unreleased` for a document without a release;
* `threats` is counted from the `model` part of the document's saved working state, by the rules of <<_threat_model_data>>;
* `address` is `/threat-models/<s>/<project.id>`, the risk that came from a threat is read from its `transferred`, and the
  reference `threat-models:threat-model/<document>#<threat id>` on that risk names the threat back.

[#_incident_requests]
== Incident Report Requests

The requests of the use cases UC-IR-1, UC-IR-2 and UC-IR-3 (xref:../use-cases/06-incidents.adoc[Use Cases: Incident Response
Reports]). The rows are in <<_requests>>, *Incident reports*; this section fixes their bodies, the data shape of an incident,
the stages and their completion, the deadlines and the addresses of the new view. Every body is PROVISIONAL unless a row says
otherwise; the decisions are in <<_decided_incidents>>. The values are those of the mock: the services of the catalog
(<<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>), the people of the directory and today, 2026-11-26.

An incident is a *staged document* (xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc[Staged Documents]) of the document type `incident`
of the category `incident-reports`, in the repository `isms-incidents-<year>` of the year of awareness (AUN-DP-1). It uses the
document lifecycle of <<_the_document_lifecycle>>: states `draft`, `in-review`, `released`, the transitions `incident.submit`
(`draft` to `in-review`, right `edit`, no step-up) and `incident.release` (`in-review` to `released`, right `approve`,
step-up; the tag is `incidents/<id>/v1`), and a save in the states `draft` and `in-review`. An incident is released once:
afterwards it is `frozen` and every write answers `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` (the behaviour of a SAMM assessment). The rights are
`read`, `edit` and `approve` on the repository of the year; completing and reopening a stage need `edit`.

[#_incident_addresses]
=== The Addresses

The lens *Incidents* had `/incidents`, `/incidents/{year}`, `/incidents/{year}/{incident}` and
`/incidents/{year}/{incident}/release`. The step adds one address, one allowed query and one placeholder pattern
(<<_allowed_queries>>):

[cols="2,3,2,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Query |Shows

|`incidents`
|`/incidents`
|none
|The first column: the years with a repository (`GET /api/v1/incidents`)

|`incidents.year`
|`/incidents/{year}`
|none
|The second column: the incidents of the year with their stage statuses and deadlines (`GET /api/v1/incidents/{year}`), and
the act *New incident*

|`incidents.new`
|`/incidents/{year}/new`
|none
|The form of a new incident of the year (a sheet of the lens): the title and the triage; nothing is stored before the first
save. The route comes before `incidents.incident` in the file

|`incidents.incident`
|`/incidents/{year}/{incident}`
|`at`, `stage`
|The incident sheet: the header with the state, the deadlines and the stages at a glance, the tabs, the history; `stage`
selects the tab; with `at` the incident as it was at that revision, read-only

|`incidents.release`
|`/incidents/{year}/{incident}/release`
|none
|The review and release of the incident (`ReleaseFlow`: the diff by stage, then section, the report, the seal)
|===

`year` is four digits (the existing pattern). `{incident}` is a document id, a ULID, and so needs a placeholder pattern of
its own, `incident`: `(?!new(?![0-9A-Za-z._-]))[0-9A-Za-z][0-9A-Za-z._-]{0,127}`. It is case sensitive, as the pattern
`document` of the generic lens, and refuses the segment `new` (a segment that only begins with it, `new-2`, is an incident
as before), so that `/incidents/{year}/new` is the new page, never an incident, and `/incidents/{year}/new/release` is
nothing. `stage` is a stage id, `[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}`; `at` is the pattern of the other addresses (`v<n>` or a commit
id). Both may be written together (`?at=v1&stage=review`, in the order of the file). A name outside the set is ignored in an
address and refused in a login's `return` (the rules of <<_allowed_queries>>). The query `stage` is part of the canonical
form because the step-up of a stage (`review`) leaves the page and returns to it (<<_the_step_up_handler>>): the person comes
back to the same tab.

[#_incident_data]
=== The Data Shape of an Incident

An incident is a document of the document type `incident` in the repository `isms-incidents-<year>`, collection `incidents`.
Its schema declares one split (`gdr-split`, https://github.com/cuioss/git-document-repository/blob/main/doc/specification/git-forms.adoc#GDR-FORMS-3[GDR-FORMS-3]) per stage, so the document has
six files and the document read six parts (the layout of
xref:../concepts/category-incident-reports.adoc#_asciidoc_is_the_output_not_the_truth[Incident Reports, AsciiDoc Is the Output, Not the Truth]):

[cols="1,2,3"]
|===
|Part |File |Content

|`data`
|`incidents/<ulid>/document.yaml`
|`title`. The workflow state is the document's, not a field

|`triage`
|`incidents/<ulid>/triage.yaml`
|The times, the classification, the affected services and the ticket ids of the stage `triage`

|`privacy-assessment`
|`incidents/<ulid>/privacy-assessment.yaml`
|The data subjects, the assessment and the decisions of the stage of that name

|`response`
|`incidents/<ulid>/response.yaml`
|The actions, the timeline and `resolved-at`

|`notifications`
|`incidents/<ulid>/notifications.yaml`
|Each notification sent, with its time, recipient and channel

|`review`
|`incidents/<ulid>/review.yaml`
|The root causes, the follow-up measures, the links to risks and the narrative. The three narrative fields (`course`,
`root-cause-analysis`, `lessons`) are AsciiDoc fragments, `incidents/<ulid>/narrative/*.adoc`, in the repository; the
application joins them into the value of the part `review`, where the form edits them as `x-asciidoc` strings
|===

Every stage object also carries `completion` (`at`, `by`), written only by the requests *complete* and *reopen*
(<<_incident_stages_requests>>). The schema of the document type, `GET /api/v1/categories/incident-reports/schema`,
`kinds[0]` (the form kit's vocabulary, xref:web-app.adoc#AUN-WEB-4[AUN-WEB-4]; the bounds and the lists of values are
PROVISIONAL, <<_decided_incidents>>). The `repository` member is the pattern `isms-incidents-<year>` because one kind
addresses one repository per year (open, xref:../concepts/data-protection-and-repository-layout.adoc#_open_questions[Data
Protection and Repository Layout, Open Questions]); the web app never reads it:

[source,json]
----
{
  "category": "incident-reports",
  "kinds": [
    {
      "id": "incident",
      "repository": "isms-incidents-<year>",
      "schema": {
        "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["title"],
        "properties": {
          "title": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
          "triage": {
            "type": "object",
            "gdr-split": true,
            "required": ["categories", "severity", "detected-at", "aware-at", "personal-data-affected", "nis2-significant"],
            "properties": {
              "categories": {
                "type": "array",
                "minItems": 1,
                "uniqueItems": true,
                "items": {"type": "string", "enum": ["security-incident", "personal-data-breach"]}
              },
              "severity": {"type": "string", "enum": ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"]},
              "occurred-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
              "detected-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
              "aware-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
              "personal-data-affected": {"type": "boolean"},
              "nis2-significant": {"type": "boolean"},
              "affected-services": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 50,
                "uniqueItems": true,
                "items": {"type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "service"}
              },
              "tickets": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 20,
                "uniqueItems": true,
                "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 64}
              },
              "completion": {
                "type": "object",
                "readOnly": true,
                "required": ["at", "by"],
                "properties": {"at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}, "by": {"type": "string", "x-person": true}}
              }
            }
          },
          "privacy-assessment": {
            "type": "object",
            "gdr-split": true,
            "required": ["data-subjects", "risk", "risk-assessment", "notify-authority", "inform-data-subjects"],
            "properties": {
              "data-subjects": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 20,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["category", "approximate-number"],
                  "properties": {
                    "category": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 120},
                    "approximate-number": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0}
                  }
                }
              },
              "records-approximate": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0},
              "risk": {"type": "string", "enum": ["unlikely", "risk", "high-risk"]},
              "risk-assessment": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 8000, "x-asciidoc": true, "x-free-text": true},
              "notify-authority": {"type": "boolean"},
              "inform-data-subjects": {"type": "boolean"},
              "reasons-for-delay": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 2000, "x-free-text": true},
              "completion": {
                "type": "object",
                "readOnly": true,
                "required": ["at", "by"],
                "properties": {"at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}, "by": {"type": "string", "x-person": true}}
              }
            }
          },
          "response": {
            "type": "object",
            "gdr-split": true,
            "required": ["actions", "resolved-at"],
            "properties": {
              "actions": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 100,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["kind", "what"],
                  "properties": {
                    "kind": {"type": "string", "enum": ["containment", "recovery", "other"]},
                    "what": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                    "role": {"type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "role"},
                    "issues": {
                      "type": "array",
                      "maxItems": 20,
                      "uniqueItems": true,
                      "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 64}
                    },
                    "done": {"type": "boolean"}
                  }
                }
              },
              "timeline": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 200,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["at", "what"],
                  "properties": {
                    "at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                    "what": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 300, "x-free-text": true}
                  }
                }
              },
              "resolved-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
              "completion": {
                "type": "object",
                "readOnly": true,
                "required": ["at", "by"],
                "properties": {"at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}, "by": {"type": "string", "x-person": true}}
              }
            }
          },
          "notifications": {
            "type": "object",
            "gdr-split": true,
            "required": [],
            "properties": {
              "supervisory-authority": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                "properties": {
                  "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                  "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                  "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                  "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100}
                }
              },
              "data-subjects": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                "properties": {
                  "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                  "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                  "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                  "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100}
                }
              },
              "nis2-early-warning": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                "properties": {
                  "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                  "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                  "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                  "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100}
                }
              },
              "nis2-notification": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                "properties": {
                  "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                  "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                  "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                  "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100}
                }
              },
              "nis2-intermediate-reports": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 20,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["due", "sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                  "properties": {
                    "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                    "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                    "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                    "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100},
                    "due": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}
                  }
                }
              },
              "nis2-progress-report": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                "properties": {
                  "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                  "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                  "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                  "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100}
                }
              },
              "nis2-final-report": {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                "properties": {
                  "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                  "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                  "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                  "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100}
                }
              },
              "others": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 20,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["sent-at", "recipient", "channel"],
                  "properties": {
                    "sent-at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                    "recipient": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                    "channel": {"type": "string", "enum": ["reporting-portal", "email", "phone", "post", "other"]},
                    "reference": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 100},
                    "comment": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 500, "x-free-text": true}
                  }
                }
              },
              "completion": {
                "type": "object",
                "readOnly": true,
                "required": ["at", "by"],
                "properties": {"at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}, "by": {"type": "string", "x-person": true}}
              }
            }
          },
          "review": {
            "type": "object",
            "gdr-split": true,
            "required": ["root-causes", "measures", "course", "root-cause-analysis", "lessons"],
            "properties": {
              "root-causes": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 20,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["category", "description"],
                  "properties": {
                    "category": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": ["configuration", "software-defect", "process", "access-control", "third-party", "other"]
                    },
                    "description": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 500, "x-free-text": true}
                  }
                }
              },
              "measures": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 50,
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": ["what"],
                  "properties": {
                    "what": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200},
                    "role": {"type": "string", "format": "gdr-link", "x-catalog": "role"},
                    "issues": {
                      "type": "array",
                      "maxItems": 20,
                      "uniqueItems": true,
                      "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 64}
                    },
                    "due": {"type": "string", "format": "date"}
                  }
                }
              },
              "risks": {
                "type": "array",
                "maxItems": 20,
                "uniqueItems": true,
                "items": {"type": "string", "format": "gdr-link"}
              },
              "course": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 8000, "x-asciidoc": true, "x-free-text": true},
              "root-cause-analysis": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 8000, "x-asciidoc": true, "x-free-text": true},
              "lessons": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 8000, "x-asciidoc": true, "x-free-text": true},
              "completion": {
                "type": "object",
                "readOnly": true,
                "required": ["at", "by"],
                "properties": {"at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}, "by": {"type": "string", "x-person": true}}
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
----

What the shape says (the rules the server enforces, and the mock follows):

* *Only `title` is required of the document;* the mandatory fields are those of each stage, and the library reports them
  per pointer (`/triage/severity`). An absent part is read as an empty part, so its mandatory fields are missing
  (xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_stage_status[Staged Documents, Stage Status]); a part of a stage that has no
  mandatory field (`notifications`) can be completed while absent.
* *The times* are instants in UTC, ISO 8601 with the `Z` suffix. `occurred-at` is optional and starts no deadline,
  `detected-at` and `aware-at` are mandatory, `aware-at` starts the deadlines (AUN-IR-1, AUN-IR-2).
* *The classification* is `categories` (one or both of `security-incident`, `personal-data-breach`), `severity` (`low`,
  `medium`, `high`, `critical`), and the two decisions that carry the deadlines, `personal-data-affected` and
  `nis2-significant`. A boolean that is absent is unanswered; `false` is an answer.
* *The affected services* are references `service:<id>` of the service catalog (AUN-IR-4); the protection needs of the
  incident are derived (<<_incident_read>>), not stored.
* *No person is named* (AUN-DP-5): the free-text fields carry the hint of the form kit (`x-free-text`); the narrative fields
  (`risk-assessment`, `course`, `root-cause-analysis`, `lessons`) are `x-asciidoc` and carry the hint as well, with a bound
  of 8,000 characters each (<<_incident_report>>); a person is a pseudonym only where a field is an `x-person` (`completion.by`). A recipient of a notification
  is the name of an organisation or an office, not of a person.
* *A notification* is an object with `sent-at`, `recipient`, `channel` (`reporting-portal`, `email`, `phone`, `post`,
  `other`) and an optional `reference` (the number the authority gave). `supervisory-authority` (GDPR Art. 33),
  `nis2-early-warning`, `nis2-notification`, `nis2-progress-report` and `nis2-final-report` are single objects;
  `nis2-intermediate-reports` is a list whose entries also carry the `due` the authority asked for; `data-subjects`
  (GDPR Art. 34, no deadline of the category) and `others` (a list, with a `comment`) record the other notices. The names
  are those of the deadline rules (AUN-IR-2) with the part `notifications` as their root; the table of the lens is these
  members, one row each, in this order.
* *The ids of the lists* are not keys: a list is sent as a whole, as in the form kit.

[#_incident_stages]
=== The Stages

The stages are declared in the workflow of the document type, `workflow.yaml`, beside its states and deadlines
(xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_the_declaration[Staged Documents, The Declaration]). The delivered declaration of
the incident file is that of xref:../concepts/category-incident-reports.adoc#_stages_of_an_incident[Incident Reports, Stages
of an Incident]; the web app reads it from the member `stages` of an incident, never from the file:

[source,yaml]
----
stages:
  - { id: triage,             part: triage }
  - { id: privacy-assessment, part: privacy-assessment, after: [triage],   when: /triage/personal-data-affected }
  - { id: response,           part: response,           after: [triage] }
  - { id: notifications,      part: notifications,      after: [triage],
      when: { any: [/triage/personal-data-affected, /triage/nis2-significant] } }
  - { id: review,             part: review,             after: [response],
      narrative: [course, root-cause-analysis, lessons], complete: { step-up: true } }
----

*The member `stages`* of the read, in the order of the declaration (the order of the tabs), one object per stage:

[cols="1,3"]
|===
|Member |Meaning

|`id`, `part`, `after`
|As declared; `after` is the list of stage ids

|`when`
|The condition as data, `null` for a stage that always applies: `{"field": "/triage/personal-data-affected"}`, or
`{"any": [{"field": …}, {"field": …}]}` (`all` likewise). A field is true when it is `true`, or, if it is no boolean,
present. The web app builds the sentence "Applies when …" of a stage that does not apply from its catalogue key
`category.incident-reports.stage.<id>.when`, never from the condition

|`narrative`
|The narrative fields of the stage (`[]` or `["course", "root-cause-analysis", "lessons"]`)

|`stepUp`
|`true` when completing the stage asks for step-up

|`status`
|`not-applicable`, `open` or `completed` (derived, <<_incident_derivation>>)

|`unanswered`
|`true` for a stage that is `not-applicable` because the field of its condition is not answered yet (absent); the stage
then reads "applies when …" and not "does not apply"

|`completion`
|`{"at", "by": {"pseudonym", "name"}}` for a completed stage, else `null`

|`predecessorReopened`
|`true` for a completed stage that has an open stage among its predecessors, directly or through the chain of `after`: it was
signed off and a stage before it was reopened since

|`missing`
|The JSON Pointers (into the document's value, `/<part>/<field>`) of the mandatory fields of the part that are empty, for
an `open` stage; `[]` for the others

|`blockedBy`
|The ids of the stages of `after` that are `open`, for an `open` stage; `[]` for the others
|===

[#_incident_years]
=== The Years

`GET /api/v1/incidents`:

[source,json]
----
{
  "today": "2026-11-26",
  "years": [
    { "year": 2026, "repository": "isms-incidents-2026", "current": true, "incidents": 7, "open": 6,
      "withMissedDeadline": 1, "retentionEnd": "2031-12-31", "address": "/incidents/2026" },
    { "year": 2025, "repository": "isms-incidents-2025", "current": false, "incidents": 2, "open": 0,
      "withMissedDeadline": 1, "retentionEnd": "2030-12-31", "address": "/incidents/2025" }
  ]
}
----

`open` counts the incidents that are not released, `withMissedDeadline` those with a deadline `MISSED` at the time of the
list (in a released one, at its release, <<_incident_deadlines>>). `retentionEnd` is 31 December of the year plus the retention
of the repository (`P5Y` by default, AUN-DP-1, AUN-DP-4). The years are those of the repositories the user may read,
`isms-incidents-<year>` with a year of four digits, newest first. `"years": []` is an installation without an incident
repository (the empty state tells the person to ask for it, UC-OPS-2). When no year is `current`, the repository of the
year of today is not configured: the lens shows the older years and says so, and offers no *New incident*; the web app
never creates a repository (AUN-DP-1).

[#_incident_list]
=== The Incidents of a Year

`GET /api/v1/incidents/2026` (shortened to two rows):

[source,json]
----
{
  "today": "2026-11-26", "asOf": "2026-11-26T10:00:00Z", "year": 2026, "repository": "isms-incidents-2026",
  "incidents": [
    { "id": "01JZA6D8F0H2K4M6P8R0T2W4Y6", "title": "Suspicious logins from an unknown network",
      "address": "/incidents/2026/01JZA6D8F0H2K4M6P8R0T2W4Y6", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "frozen": false,
      "severity": null, "categories": ["security-incident"], "personalDataAffected": null, "nis2Significant": null,
      "awareAt": "2026-11-26T08:30:00Z", "resolvedAt": null,
      "stages": [ { "id": "triage", "status": "open" }, { "id": "privacy-assessment", "status": "not-applicable" },
                  { "id": "response", "status": "open" }, { "id": "notifications", "status": "not-applicable" },
                  { "id": "review", "status": "open" } ],
      "deadlines": { "open": 0, "met": 0, "missed": 0, "next": null },
      "requested": false, "updatedAt": "2026-11-26T08:40:00Z",
      "updatedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" }, "release": null },
    { "id": "01JZ9T4M6Q8S0V2X4B6D8F0H2K", "title": "Card data exposed in the checkout log",
      "address": "/incidents/2026/01JZ9T4M6Q8S0V2X4B6D8F0H2K", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "frozen": false,
      "severity": "high", "categories": ["security-incident", "personal-data-breach"], "personalDataAffected": true,
      "nis2Significant": true, "awareAt": "2026-11-19T07:40:00Z", "resolvedAt": "2026-11-20T16:00:00Z",
      "stages": [ { "id": "triage", "status": "completed" }, { "id": "privacy-assessment", "status": "open" },
                  { "id": "response", "status": "open" }, { "id": "notifications", "status": "open" },
                  { "id": "review", "status": "open" } ],
      "deadlines": { "open": 1, "met": 1, "missed": 2, "next": { "rule": "nis2-final-report", "due": "2026-12-22T10:20:00Z" } },
      "requested": false, "updatedAt": "2026-11-22T10:25:00Z",
      "updatedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" }, "release": null }
  ],
  "truncated": false
}
----

* The rows are ordered by `awareAt`, newest first, a row without one last; `severity`, `personalDataAffected`,
  `nis2Significant` and `resolvedAt` are `null` while unanswered. `categories` is the list of the triage.
* `stages` has the five stages in the order of the declaration with the derived `status` (<<_incident_derivation>>), so a lens
  can show "triage open" or list every incident whose review is open
  (xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_in_the_web_app[Staged Documents, In the Web App]).
* `deadlines` counts the deadlines of the incident by status at `asOf`; `next` is the open deadline with the earliest `due`
  (`null` without a date or without an open one). `release` is `{"tag", "at"}` or `null`.
* A year that has no repository, or that the user may not read, answers `404 NOT_FOUND`; a year with no incident answers
  `"incidents": []`.

[#_incident_read]
=== An Incident

`GET /api/v1/incidents/2026/01JZ9T4M6Q8S0V2X4B6D8F0H2K` (the parts are shortened to the members the figures need):

[source,json]
----
{
  "repository": "isms-incidents-2026", "id": "01JZ9T4M6Q8S0V2X4B6D8F0H2K", "category": "incident-reports", "kind": "incident",
  "title": "Card data exposed in the checkout log", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "frozen": false,
  "year": 2026, "address": "/incidents/2026/01JZ9T4M6Q8S0V2X4B6D8F0H2K", "asOf": "2026-11-26T10:00:00Z",
  "parts": {
    "data": { "blob": "5c0e2a7", "savedAt": "2026-11-19T07:55:00Z",
              "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example", "role": "Security Champion" },
              "value": { "title": "Card data exposed in the checkout log" } },
    "triage": { "blob": "b81d4f3", "savedAt": "2026-11-19T09:30:00Z",
                "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example", "role": "Security Champion" },
                "value": { "categories": ["security-incident", "personal-data-breach"], "severity": "high",
                           "occurred-at": "2026-11-17T22:00:00Z", "detected-at": "2026-11-19T07:10:00Z",
                           "aware-at": "2026-11-19T07:40:00Z", "personal-data-affected": true, "nis2-significant": true,
                           "affected-services": ["service:payment-api", "service:customer-db"], "tickets": ["SEC-2210"],
                           "completion": { "at": "2026-11-19T09:30:00Z", "by": "p-4c1a9e" } } },
    "privacy-assessment": { "blob": "0a97c3d", "savedAt": "2026-11-20T11:00:00Z", "savedBy": { "…": "…" },
                            "value": { "data-subjects": [ { "category": "Customers", "approximate-number": 1200 } ],
                                       "records-approximate": 1500 } },
    "response": { "blob": "…", "…": "…" },
    "notifications": { "blob": "…", "…": "…" },
    "review": { "blob": null, "savedAt": null, "savedBy": null, "value": {} }
  },
  "draft": null,
  "history": [
    { "at": "2026-11-22T10:25:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" }, "kind": "save",
      "part": "notifications", "count": 1 },
    { "at": "2026-11-19T09:30:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" }, "kind": "complete",
      "stage": "triage" }
  ],
  "release": null, "releaseRequest": null,
  "transitions": [ { "id": "incident.submit", "to": "in-review", "right": "edit", "stepUp": false } ],
  "stages": [
    { "id": "triage", "part": "triage", "after": [], "when": null, "narrative": [], "stepUp": false, "status": "completed",
      "unanswered": false, "completion": { "at": "2026-11-19T09:30:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } },
      "predecessorReopened": false, "missing": [], "blockedBy": [] },
    { "id": "privacy-assessment", "part": "privacy-assessment", "after": ["triage"],
      "when": { "field": "/triage/personal-data-affected" }, "narrative": [], "stepUp": false, "status": "open",
      "unanswered": false, "completion": null, "predecessorReopened": false,
      "missing": ["/privacy-assessment/risk", "/privacy-assessment/risk-assessment", "/privacy-assessment/notify-authority",
                  "/privacy-assessment/inform-data-subjects"], "blockedBy": [] },
    { "…": "response, notifications" },
    { "id": "review", "part": "review", "after": ["response"], "when": null,
      "narrative": ["course", "root-cause-analysis", "lessons"], "stepUp": true, "status": "open", "unanswered": false,
      "completion": null, "predecessorReopened": false,
      "missing": ["/review/root-causes", "/review/measures", "/review/course", "/review/root-cause-analysis", "/review/lessons"],
      "blockedBy": ["response"] }
  ],
  "deadlines": [ "…" ],
  "protection": {
    "needs": { "confidentiality": "very-high", "integrity": "very-high", "availability": "high" },
    "decidedBy": { "confidentiality": "service:payment-api", "integrity": "service:payment-api", "availability": "service:payment-api" }
  }
}
----

* The members of the generic document read keep their meaning (<<_document_lifecycle_requests>>). Every part of the schema is
  present in `parts`; a part never saved has `"blob": null`, `"savedAt": null`, `"savedBy": null` and `"value": {}`. The
  value of a part holds `completion` as `{"at", "by"}` with the pseudonym; `stages[].completion` carries the name as well.
* `history` adds the kinds `complete` and `reopen`, each with the `stage`; the earlier completion of a reopened stage stays
  in it, the evidence that the stage changed after its sign-off.
* `transitions` of an incident in `draft` is `incident.submit`, of one in `in-review` `incident.release` with
  `"release": true`, of a released one empty. The workflow offers the transition whatever the stages are; the view
  disables *Submit* with the reason while an applicable stage is not completed, and the request answers `STAGES_OPEN`.
* `asOf` is the instant the deadlines were computed at: the time of the read for the working state, the release time for a
  released incident or a revision from the release on, the commit time of a revision before the first release
  (<<_incident_deadlines>>).
* `protection` is derived, never stored: per dimension (`confidentiality`, `integrity`, `availability`) the highest of the
  *effective* protection needs of the affected services (`effective.protectionNeeds` of the service entry, which already
  include what the service depends on, AUN-DIR-5, AUN-IR-4), in the order `normal`, `high`, `very-high`; `decidedBy` is the
  first affected service, in the order of the list, whose effective need equals that highest one. Without an affected
  service it is `null`. It is derived for as long as the incident is not released; the release records the derived
  values and the titles of the affected services in the release commit (`incidents/<ulid>/protection.yaml`), and a
  released incident and every revision from the release on read them from that record, never from the catalog again, so
  that the committed `report.adoc` stays equal to a fresh generation when the catalog changes later (<<_decided_incidents>>
  point 19).
* A revision (`?at=`) has `frozen: true`, no draft and no transitions, and the member `at` of the generic revision; its
  `stages`, `deadlines` and `protection` are those of that revision. `GET /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-2026/{id}` answers
  the same body.
* A `parts.<stage>.value` of a stage that is `not-applicable` is kept and shown as such; its data are never rewritten.

[#_incident_deadlines]
=== The Deadlines

The deadlines are computed from the data of the parts and the clock, never stored (AUN-IR-2: computed from the in-memory
index). Each row of AUN-IR-2 is one rule of `workflow.yaml`; the shipped rules, with the field paths of the parts (the
change that moves the paths of AUN-IR-2 into the parts, which the concept announced, <<_decided_incidents>> point 1):

[cols="2,3,3,3"]
|===
|Rule |Applies when |Due |Met by

|`gdpr-art33`
|`/triage/personal-data-affected`
|`/triage/aware-at` + `PT72H`
|`/notifications/supervisory-authority`

|`nis2-early-warning`
|`/triage/nis2-significant`
|`/triage/aware-at` + `PT24H`
|`/notifications/nis2-early-warning`

|`nis2-notification`
|`/triage/nis2-significant`
|`/triage/aware-at` + `PT72H`
|`/notifications/nis2-notification`

|`nis2-intermediate-report`
|`/notifications/nis2-intermediate-reports/*`, one deadline per entry
|its `due`
|that entry

|`nis2-final-report`
|`/notifications/nis2-notification/sent-at` is present
|that `sent-at` + `P1M`
|`/notifications/nis2-final-report`, or `/notifications/nis2-progress-report` while `/response/resolved-at` is absent or later than the due time

|`nis2-final-report-after-handling`
|the due time of `nis2-final-report` has passed and `/response/resolved-at` is absent or later than it
|`/response/resolved-at` + `P1M`
|`/notifications/nis2-final-report`
|===

The grammar is the one AUN-IR-2 describes, PROVISIONAL because the library does not offer it yet (the open question of
https://github.com/cuioss/git-document-repository/blob/main/doc/concepts/schema-driven-documents.adoc#_open_questions[Schema-Driven
Documents, Open Questions]); the mock reads the rules as data and holds them in the generator, not in code of a view:

[source,yaml]
----
deadlines:
  - id: gdpr-art33
    when: /triage/personal-data-affected
    due: { from: /triage/aware-at, plus: PT72H }
    met-by: [ { field: /notifications/supervisory-authority } ]
  - id: nis2-final-report
    when: /notifications/nis2-notification/sent-at
    due: { from: /notifications/nis2-notification/sent-at, plus: P1M }
    met-by:
      - { field: /notifications/nis2-final-report }
      - { field: /notifications/nis2-progress-report, while-unresolved: true }
----

*The status of a deadline* at `asOf`:

. A deadline whose date field is empty has `due: null` and is `OPEN`.
. A `met-by` entry holds `sent-at`; the deadline is `MET` when one entry's `sent-at` is not after `due`.
. Otherwise it is `MISSED` when `asOf` is after `due`, else `OPEN`. A notification sent after the due time therefore leaves
  the deadline `MISSED` and carries its `sentAt`: it was sent late, and that stays visible.
. A `met-by` entry marked `while-unresolved` counts only when `resolved-at` is absent or later than `due`.
. Periods are added to the instant in UTC (`PT72H` as hours, `P1M` as a calendar month, the last day of the month when the
  day does not exist).
. A rule whose `when` is false is not listed; a boolean that is absent is false.

*The member `deadlines`* of an incident read, in the order of the rules, the entries of a list rule in the order of the list:

[source,json]
----
[
  { "rule": "gdpr-art33", "element": null, "startedAt": "2026-11-19T07:40:00Z", "due": "2026-11-22T07:40:00Z",
    "status": "MISSED", "sentAt": null, "metBy": "/notifications/supervisory-authority" },
  { "rule": "nis2-early-warning", "element": null, "startedAt": "2026-11-19T07:40:00Z", "due": "2026-11-20T07:40:00Z",
    "status": "MET", "sentAt": "2026-11-20T06:55:00Z", "metBy": "/notifications/nis2-early-warning" },
  { "rule": "nis2-notification", "element": null, "startedAt": "2026-11-19T07:40:00Z", "due": "2026-11-22T07:40:00Z",
    "status": "MISSED", "sentAt": "2026-11-22T10:20:00Z", "metBy": "/notifications/nis2-notification" },
  { "rule": "nis2-final-report", "element": null, "startedAt": "2026-11-22T10:20:00Z", "due": "2026-12-22T10:20:00Z",
    "status": "OPEN", "sentAt": null, "metBy": "/notifications/nis2-final-report" }
]
----

`startedAt` is the instant the clock started from (`null` when the start field is empty), `element` the index of the entry of
a list rule (else `null`), `metBy` the pointer of the record that meets it (the `sent-at` the row carries comes from that record: for `nis2-final-report`
met by the progress report it is `/notifications/nis2-progress-report`). The web app writes the name of a rule and the
legal basis from its catalogue keys `category.incident-reports.deadline.<rule>` and `….basis`; the data carry no text.

*At release the clock stops.* The `deadlines` of a released incident, and of every revision from the release on, are computed at
the instant of the release (`asOf`), because the released `report.adoc` must be reproducible from the data (AUN-IR-1): a
deadline that was open at the release stays `OPEN` in the released record, and a `MISSED` one stays `MISSED` and visible in
the report (AUN-IR-2). A revision before the first release is computed at its commit time: the clock was still running then.
A missed deadline never stops a release. A deadline that is still `OPEN` at the release can no longer be met in the incident
(a released incident is frozen, and it leaves the Due lens), so the release page lists the open deadlines before the person
confirms; the request has no new member (the web app reads them from the incident). Whether the release should be refused
while a deadline is open is an owner question (<<_decided_incidents>> point 16).

*In the Due lens* (`GET /api/v1/due`, PROVISIONAL) an incident that is not released is an item of the kind `deadline`, lens
`incidents`, for every deadline of it that is `OPEN` with a date, or `MISSED` and not sent at all (`sentAt` is `null`): a
deadline that was sent late is no duty any more, it is only visible in the incident. The item is
`{"kind": "deadline", "lens": "incidents", "title": "<incident title>: <rule>", "address":
"/incidents/2026/<id>?stage=notifications", "due": "2026-11-22", "deadline": {"rule": "gdpr-art33", "status": "MISSED"}}`:
`due` is the date of the due time in UTC, the member `deadline` is new (PROVISIONAL) so that the web app writes the
localised name and marks a missed one without parsing the title. The items are for the users with `read` on the repository
of the year (the incident responders, AUN-IR-2).

[#_incident_stages_requests]
=== Completing and Reopening a Stage

`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-2026/01JZA2B4D6F8H0K2N4Q6S8V0X2/stages/privacy-assessment/complete`, with the body
`{"blob": "7e13b9c"}` (the blob id of the part the form loaded; optional, without it nothing is compared), answers

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "COMPLETED", "stage": "privacy-assessment", "status": "completed", "at": "2026-11-26T10:20:00Z",
  "completion": { "at": "2026-11-26T10:20:00Z", "by": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } },
  "parts": { "privacy-assessment": { "blob": "d40c5e2", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T10:20:00Z",
                                     "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } } },
  "stages": [ { "id": "triage", "status": "completed" }, { "id": "privacy-assessment", "status": "completed" },
              { "id": "response", "status": "open" }, { "id": "notifications", "status": "open" },
              { "id": "review", "status": "open" } ] }
----

The completion is a save of the part onto the working branch: the signed commit names the person and `completion` records
the time in the data. A blob id that is no longer the current one of the part answers `409 CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW` (someone
saved the part since the form loaded it; the person would otherwise sign off data they did not see); nothing is written.
The event `document-saved` (`part` the stage, `by`) reaches the other tabs. `…/reopen` has the same body and answers

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "REOPENED", "stage": "triage", "status": "open", "at": "2026-11-25T15:00:00Z",
  "parts": { "triage": { "blob": "…", "savedAt": "2026-11-25T15:00:00Z", "savedBy": { "…": "…" } } },
  "stages": [ { "id": "triage", "status": "open" }, { "id": "privacy-assessment", "status": "completed" }, "…" ] }
----

Reopening writes no other stage: the stages after it stay completed and the read marks them `predecessorReopened`.

*The refusal of a completion*, `422`, names both causes at once; a list may be empty:

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "STAGE_NOT_COMPLETABLE", "stage": "response",
  "missing": ["/response/actions", "/response/resolved-at"], "open": ["triage"] }
----

`missing` are the pointers of the empty mandatory fields (the `required` of the part, an absent part read as `{}`), `open` the
predecessors that are neither completed nor not applicable. The view lists both (the jump to a field is that of the
shared transition bar). Nothing is written.

*The other refusals:* `409` `{"outcome": "STAGE_NOT_APPLICABLE", "stage"}` for a stage whose condition is false (it cannot be
completed, reopened is no act of it either); `409` `{"outcome": "STAGE_STATE", "stage", "status"}` for completing a
completed stage or reopening one that is not (`status` is its status); `409 DOCUMENT_RELEASED` `{"tag"}` for a released
incident; `404 NOT_FOUND` for a stage id the document type does not declare; `403 FORBIDDEN` without `edit`.

*Step-up.* The stage `review` declares `complete: {step-up: true}`: its completion answers `403 STEP_UP_REQUIRED` with
`stepUp` (the address of the confirmation, or, with local users, the password dialog) like a release, and `401
INVALID_CREDENTIALS` for a wrong password; the member `password` of the body carries the confirmation
(<<_the_step_up_handler>>). The other stages and every reopen go out without a question. The margin line of the control
is that of the release.

[#_incident_save]
=== Creating and Saving

`POST /api/v1/documents/isms-incidents-2026`, the first save of a new incident (the generic create, the example is
shortened):

[source,json]
----
{ "kind": "incident",
  "parts": {
    "data":   { "fields": { "/title": "Suspicious logins from the VPN gateway" } },
    "triage": { "fields": { "/categories": ["security-incident"], "/detected-at": "2026-11-26T08:05:00Z",
                            "/aware-at": "2026-11-26T08:30:00Z", "/affected-services": ["service:identity-provider"] } } } }
----

answers `201`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "CREATED", "id": "01JZA8F0H2K4M6P8R0T2W4Y6A8", "repository": "isms-incidents-2026",
  "address": "/incidents/2026/01JZA8F0H2K4M6P8R0T2W4Y6A8", "state": "draft", "version": 1, "at": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z",
  "parts": { "data":   { "blob": "e51a7d3", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z", "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } },
             "triage": { "blob": "3a8f10c", "savedAt": "2026-11-26T09:41:00Z", "savedBy": { "pseudonym": "p-4c1a9e", "name": "Alex Example" } } } }
----

The year of the address is the repository's: the first save needs no `aware-at`, but a save that sets `/triage/aware-at` to a
time whose year (UTC) is not the year of the repository answers `422 VALIDATION` with `{"part": "triage", "pointer":
"/aware-at", "code": "OUT_OF_YEAR"}` (PROVISIONAL, <<_decided_incidents>> point 24): the repository is the year of the
awareness, and the person who learned of a breach on 31 December writes it in the year before. Only `/title` is mandatory (`{"part": "data", "pointer": "/title", "code": "REQUIRED"}`); the other
mandatory fields are asked for by the completion of the stage and by the transition. The event `document-created` (`kind`
`incident`) reaches the other tabs.

*A save and a draft* are the generic requests with the parts in one body; `fields` maps a JSON Pointer into the value of the
part to its new value, the properties of the top level of the part as a whole (a list or a section as a whole, as everywhere):

[source,json]
----
{ "parts": {
    "notifications": { "blob": "9d2b6e4", "fields": {
        "/supervisory-authority": { "sent-at": "2026-11-26T09:45:00Z", "recipient": "State data protection authority",
                                    "channel": "reporting-portal", "reference": "2026-1187" } } },
    "response": { "blob": "c0a3f55", "fields": { "/actions": [ { "kind": "containment", "what": "Block the exposed log index" } ] } } } }
----

* *`completion` is not a field of a save.* A save or a draft that sets, changes or removes a pointer below `/completion` of
  a part answers `422 VALIDATION` with `{"part", "pointer": "/completion", "code": "READ_ONLY"}` and writes nothing: only
  *complete* and *reopen* write it. This holds for every client, an agent included (xref:../concepts/staged-documents.adoc#_agents[Staged
  Documents, Agents]).
* *A completed stage is read-only until it is reopened.* A save or a draft with a field of a completed stage answers `409
  {"outcome": "STAGE_LOCKED", "stages": ["triage"]}`, listing every completed stage the body touches; nothing is written,
  not even the other parts. A stage that is `not-applicable` can be saved (the data are kept, never checked).
* *The conflict is per part,* as for every document, and per top-level field within it
  (`{"part": "notifications", "blob", "fields": [{"pointer": "/supervisory-authority", "own", "other", "by"}]}`); the
  completion of another person is such a conflict for the part, not a field.
* *The refusals* are those of the generic save with the part, for example
  `{"part": "triage", "pointer": "/affected-services/0", "code": "REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND"}`,
  `{"part": "notifications", "pointer": "/supervisory-authority/sent-at", "code": "FORMAT"}`,
  `{"part": "triage", "pointer": "/completion", "code": "READ_ONLY"}`.
* *The answers move the clocks:* a save of `/triage/aware-at` or of `/notifications/…` changes `deadlines` and `stages` at the next
  read; the answer carries no deadlines (the web app reads the incident again, as after every save).

[#_incident_submit_release]
=== Submitting and Releasing

`POST …/transitions/incident.submit` answers `200 {"outcome": "TRANSITIONED", "state": "in-review", "version": 1, "at"}` when
every applicable stage is completed, and otherwise `422`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "STAGES_OPEN",
  "stages": [ { "stage": "triage", "missing": [] },
              { "stage": "review", "missing": ["/review/root-causes", "/review/lessons"] } ] }
----

with every applicable stage that is not completed and its missing mandatory fields (a stage that is `open` and complete is
listed with `"missing": []`: it only needs its sign-off). `POST /api/v1/releases/isms-incidents-2026/{id}` answers the
same refusal when a stage was reopened after the submission, else `200`

[source,json]
----
{ "outcome": "RELEASED", "tag": "incidents/01JZ9V7P9S1T3W5Y7A9C1E3G5J/v1", "at": "2026-11-26T10:05:00Z",
  "report": "incidents/01JZ9V7P9S1T3W5Y7A9C1E3G5J/report.adoc" }
----

The release is the shared flow of the release view; `CHANGED_SINCE_REVIEW`, `DOCUMENT_RELEASED`, `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` and
`INVALID_CREDENTIALS` are those of <<_outcomes_of_the_document_requests>>. The diff `GET …/diff?from=released` is the generic one;
for an incident `from` is `null`, the `sections` are the parts in the order `data`, `triage`, `privacy-assessment`, `response`,
`notifications`, `review` (the stages of the declaration; a stage that does not apply is left out), and the web app groups
the changes of a part by the first segment of the pointer. The release page shows the report beside it (<<_incident_report>>).

[#_incident_report]
=== The Report

`GET /api/v1/incidents/2026/01JZ9V7P9S1T3W5Y7A9C1E3G5J/report`:

[source,json]
----
{ "repository": "isms-incidents-2026", "id": "01JZ9V7P9S1T3W5Y7A9C1E3G5J", "title": "Credential stuffing against the login form",
  "source": "generated", "asOf": "2026-11-26T10:00:00Z",
  "adoc": "= Credential stuffing against the login form\n\n== Summary\n\n…" }
----

* `source` is `generated` for an incident that is not released: `report.adoc` freshly generated by the template from the saved
  working state, a preview that is never committed (it may be incomplete); `committed` for a released one or a revision from
  the release on: the `report.adoc` that the release committed, which the CI check holds equal to a fresh generation
  (AUN-IR-1). A revision before the first release has no committed report: `GET …/report?at=<revision>` generates it from
  that revision, `source` is `generated` and `asOf` is the commit time of the revision.
* The report of the working state describes the *saved* state: it follows a save, a completion, a submission and the clock
  (the mock keeps one report per state of its profile, the states that change what the report says have a file of their own).
* The `adoc` is an AsciiDoc document. The web app renders it with `POST /api/v1/render/asciidoc`, like every AsciiDoc text,
  one request for each level-2 section, because the request is limited to 65,536 bytes of source; a section that is still
  larger is shown as its source text. The narrative fields are bounded (8,000 characters each) so that each of them, a section
  of its own, fits; the bounds of the other fields do not add up to one request (the part `response` alone may hold
  more than 200,000 characters), which is why the report is rendered by section (<<_decided_incidents>> point 25). The web app
  shows the report as a read-only document; it makes no download. A PDF for authorities is made from the
  committed file outside the web app (the concept names no export).
* The template joins the data and the fragments in a fixed order (reproducible generation, AUN-IR-1): the title, a summary
  table (state, severity, the three times, the classification), the affected services with the derived protection needs,
  the deadlines table with the status of every deadline and the time it was sent (a missed one is a row like another, marked
  `MISSED`), the notifications, one section per stage that applies in the order of the declaration, then the narrative
  (course, root-cause analysis, lessons). Texts that the template writes (headings, labels) are in English in the file; the
  web app does not translate a generated report.

[#_incident_derivation]
=== Where the Figures Come From

Every figure of the lens is derived from the parts; the mock's files must agree with each other. For an incident `i` and
the instant `asOf`:

* *`status` of a stage* is `not-applicable` when its `when` is false for the data of the part `triage` (a field that is
  `true`, or, if it is no boolean, present; a stage without `when` always applies), `completed` when the part holds
  `completion`, else `open`. `unanswered` is true for a `not-applicable` stage none of whose condition fields is present.
  `missing` is, for an `open` stage, the list of its `required` fields that are absent from the part, in the order of the
  schema, as `/<part>/<field>`; `blockedBy` its predecessors that are `open`; `predecessorReopened` is true for a `completed`
  stage with an `open` stage among its ancestors by `after`.
* *The deadlines* are those of <<_incident_deadlines>>; `deadlines.open`, `.met` and `.missed` of a list row count them by
  status, `next` is the `OPEN` deadline with a date and the earliest `due`.
* *`withMissedDeadline`* of a year is the number of its incidents with at least one `MISSED` deadline; `open` the number of
  incidents that are not released; `incidents` the number of rows of the year.
* *`state`* of a row is the workflow state; a released incident has `version` 1, `frozen` true and the `release` of its tag.
  `frozen` is `false` in `draft` and `in-review`.
* *`protection`* is derived as in <<_incident_read>>.
* *`awareAt`, `resolvedAt`, `severity`, `categories`, `personalDataAffected`, `nis2Significant`* of a row are the values of the
  parts, `null` when absent. The year of every `aware-at` is the year of the repository.
* *The Due items* are those of <<_incident_deadlines>>.

[#_incident_sources]
=== Sources of the Deadline Rules

The periods are those of the concept (xref:../concepts/category-incident-reports.adoc#_the_deadline_clock[Incident
Reports, The Deadline Clock]) and of AUN-IR-2; this step adds no legal statement to them. What was checked against its source
while this section was written, and what was not:

[cols="2,3,2"]
|===
|Statement |Source |Checked

|The early warning within 24 hours, the notification within 72 hours of becoming aware of a significant security incident,
and the final report within one month after the 72-hour notification, are fixed for Germany by § 32 BSIG; after a progress
report ("Fortschrittsmeldung") for an incident that is still ongoing, the final report ("Abschlussmeldung") follows after the
incident was handled conclusively (§ 32 para. 2)
|https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/bsig_2025/__32.html[§ 32 BSIG]
|Yes, the wording of paras. 1 and 2, with the page of gesetze-im-internet.de (the law calls them "Meldung", not "report")

|The notification of a personal data breach within 72 hours of becoming aware of it, with reasons for a delay, and the
documentation of every breach
|https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg/2016/679/oj[Regulation (EU) 2016/679], Art. 33(1) and (5)
|No: EUR-Lex returned no text to the tool of this change. Repeated from the concept; open for the owner

|The early warning within 24 hours, the notification within 72 hours, the intermediate report on request, the final report no
later than one month after the notification, and the progress report for an incident still ongoing
|https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2022/2555/oj[Directive (EU) 2022/2555], Art. 23(4)
|No, as above; open for the owner
|===

The Art. 33(1) exception (no notification when the breach is unlikely to result in a risk to the rights and freedoms of natural
persons) is not part of the rules: AUN-IR-2 computes the deadline whenever personal data are affected (<<_decided_incidents>>,
for the owner).


[#_isms_requests]
== ISMS Requests

The requests of the use cases UC-ISMS-1, UC-ISMS-2 and UC-ISMS-3 (xref:../use-cases/07-isms.adoc[Use Cases: ISMS]) and of the two
use cases that carry something into the register, UC-SAMM-6 (a gap becomes a risk) and UC-TM-3 (a threat is transferred). The
rows are in <<_requests>>, *ISMS*, *SAMM* and *Threat models*; this section fixes their bodies, the data shape of the ISMS
documents and of a risk, the numbering of a risk, the addresses of the new views and the two requests that serve the transfer.
Every body is PROVISIONAL unless a row says otherwise; the decisions are in <<_decided_isms>>. The values are those of the mock:
the scopes of <<_samm_requests>>, the threat models of <<_threat_model_requests>>, the catalog of
<<_directory_and_catalog_requests>>, the risk register of <<_risk_register_as_of>> and today, 2026-11-26.

The category `isms` has two repositories and five document types (xref:category-isms.adoc[Category ISMS]):

[cols="2,2,2,3"]
|===
|Document type (`kind`) |Repository |Collection |Tag of a release

|`policy`
|`isms-core`
|`policies/<id>/`
|`policies/<id>/v<n>`

|`isms-scope`
|`isms-core`
|`scope/<id>/`
|`scope/<id>/v<n>`

|`audit-report`
|`isms-core`
|`audits/<id>/`
|`audits/<id>/v<n>`

|`management-review`
|`isms-core`
|`management-reviews/<id>/`
|`management-reviews/<id>/v<n>`

|`risk`
|`isms-register`
|`risks/R-<n>.yaml`
|`risks/R-<n>/v<n>`
|===

Every type uses the document lifecycle of <<_the_document_lifecycle>> and is *released more than once* (the behaviour of a
threat model and of `test-note`, not of a SAMM assessment): states `draft`, `in-review`, `released`; one workflow for the
category, the transitions `isms.submit` (`draft` to `in-review`, right `edit`, no step-up) and `isms.release` (`in-review` to
`released`, right `approve`, step-up); a save in every state; the first save after a release starts the next version. The
rights are `read`, `edit` and `approve` on the repository. The id of a document is a ULID; the id of a risk is its running
number `R-<n>`, which is also the name of its file. The web app reads and writes every document of the category with the
generic requests; the domain requests of this section exist only where the address or a figure needs more than the generic
answer (the register, the gaps, the draft of a risk from an origin).

[#_isms_addresses]
=== The Addresses

The lens *ISMS* had `/isms`, `/isms/risks`, `/isms/risks/{entry}`, `/isms/risks/{entry}/release`, `/isms/documents`,
`/isms/documents/{id}` and `/isms/documents/{id}/release`. The step adds three addresses, the allowed queries below and two placeholder
patterns (<<_allowed_queries>>):

[cols="2,3,2,3"]
|===
|Route id |Address |Query |Shows

|`isms`
|`/isms`
|none
|The first column of the lens: the document types with their documents and the risk register with its entries; the page
reads `GET /api/v1/documents/isms-core` and `GET /api/v1/isms/register`

|`isms.risks`
|`/isms/risks`
|none
|The risk register (`GET /api/v1/isms/register`): every entry in every state, with rating, treatment, owning team, review
date and origin, and the act *New risk*. The register as of a date is the page `/reports/risk-register`

|`isms.risks.new`
|`/isms/risks/new`
|`origin`
|The form of a new risk (a sheet of the lens). Nothing is stored and no number is shown before the first save. With `origin` the
form is prefilled from a SAMM gap or a threat (<<_isms_origin>>). The route comes before `isms.risk` in the file

|`isms.risk`
|`/isms/risks/{entry}`
|`at`
|The sheet of a risk: the form, the workflow acts, the origin, the history; with `at` the risk as it was at that revision,
read-only (the address the register as of a date links to)

|`isms.risk.release`
|`/isms/risks/{entry}/release`
|none
|The review and release of the risk (`ReleaseFlow`)

|`isms.documents`
|`/isms/documents`
|`kind`
|The documents of the category in the repository `isms-core`, by document type; with `kind` the documents of that type, and
the act *New document*

|`isms.documents.new`
|`/isms/documents/new`
|`kind`
|The form of a new document of the type `kind` (without `kind`, a choice among the four); nothing is stored before the first
save. The route comes before `isms.document` in the file

|`isms.document`
|`/isms/documents/{ismsdocument}`
|`at`
|The document sheet: the form, the workflow acts, the history and the releases to compare; with `at` the document as it was at
that revision, read-only

|`isms.document.compare`
|`/isms/documents/{ismsdocument}/compare`
|`from`, `to`
|Two versions of one document side by side: the changes between two releases (or a release and the working state), by
field. Without `from` the page offers the pairs of neighbouring releases and two pickers over the revisions

|`isms.document.release`
|`/isms/documents/{ismsdocument}/release`
|none
|The review and release of the document (`ReleaseFlow`)
|===
